# Module map

One line per module: what it defines. The module's own `/-!` docstring has
the detail; this file only says where to look. Source-support verdicts are
the [feature inventory](solidity-feature-inventory.md)'s; module presence alone
is not one.

**Start at `Theorems.lean`**: the main theorems on one page, in notation
(`⊢ φ → ⊨ᶠ φ`, `(P, σ) ⇓ o`, `⟦P⟧b`), each proved by the original's name.

```
Solidity/  KeySort  AST  Syntax  Semantics  Update  SpecSyntax  Theorems
           Semantics/         the interpreter's satellites
           Calculus/          the taclets and everything proved about them
           Theory/            solkey's data-structure theories, typed (Typed/), and their path side conditions
           Typing/            value typing, reachability
           SortCheck/         solkey's sort annotations, checked
           Counterexamples/   refutations that pin a design decision
           Evm/               the compiler and its simulation proof
           Tools/             the `#run`, `#wp`, `#verify`, … commands
           Corpus/            solkey's example suites (own target)
           Frontend/          solc's JSON AST read at elaboration time
           Solkey/            solkey's contracts imported from solc (own target)
           Examples/          the worked examples, in the default build
```

The library roots: `Solidity.lean` (the default build, every module),
`SolkeyTestSuite.lean` (solkey's `TestSuite.sol` imported and derived),
`SolidityCorpus.lean` (the generated corpus), `SolidityLean.lean` (the
native CLI entry point, `docs/cli.md`), `TrustAudit.lean` (the transitive
axiom gate of the public bridges).

| Root | What it builds |
|---|---|
| `Solidity.lean` | The `Solidity` library: every module of the default build. |
| `SolkeyTestSuite.lean` | `Solidity/Solkey/TestSuite.lean` and `Solidity/TestSuite/`. |
| `SolidityCorpus.lean` | `Solidity/Corpus/`, generated by `scripts/solkey-port.mjs`. |
| `SolidityLean.lean` | The native entry point of the CLI (`docs/cli.md`). |
| `TrustAudit.lean` | The transitive axiom audit of the public proof bridges. |

Layering: types and names (`AST`), contexts (`Scope`), the grammar and the
contract (`Syntax`), the typed syntax (`Syntax/Scoped`) and its elaborator,
the typed state (`Semantics/State`; storage `Semantics/Storage`, heap
`Semantics/Heap`) and the typed run (`Semantics/Run`),
terms and updates (`Signature`: the typed signature), scoped formulas (`Calculus/Fml`),
the taclets (`Calculus/Rules`), then what is proved about them
(`docs/intrinsic.md` has how it got this shape). Imports are acyclic; a new
module goes in `Solidity.lean` (`scripts/check-orphans.mjs` fails on a module
nothing imports).

**Formulas read storage through the Theory.** Programs run in the typed run,
an update is read by where it lands (`Upd.Lands`), but `a = b` (`Fml.eq`)
compares `Tm.denote`s in the typed Theory (`Theory/Typed/Obs.lean`: a storage
its observations at typed locations, `Storage.obs`) by `=`, and is total as
KeY's `=` is; a program comparison `a == b` is the same equation.  There is
no definedness test: what may revert is evaluated by the program, and
`wt(storage)` is a predicate (`Fml.wellFormed`).  `Theory/Typed/Obs.lean` proves once that the
interpreter's reads and writes are the Theory's (`Storage.obs_write`, …), and
`Theory/Bridge/Denote.lean` lifts it to the symbols (`OpN.denote_eval`).  A
derivation rewrites terms by named term taclets (`Calculus/TermLib/Rules.lean`,
`Proves.rewrite`), each sound by its Theory law (`TermTaclet.sound`).  `sol_decide`
reads the same Theory symbolically (`Calculus/Decide.lean`), and memory by
the typed laws of `Theory/Typed/Mem.lean` (`Calculus/MemRead.lean`).

## The language

The separate dependency-bearing package (`Bridge/`, Mathlib, CSLib, Loom,
Velvet, EvmYul) is described in [bridge.md](bridge.md), with scope and
commands; it never enters the core import graph.

| Bridge module | What it defines |
|---|---|
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge.lean` | The bridge root: the core's notions as Mathlib's and CSLib's. |
| `Bridge/RuleCheck.lean` | `lake exe rulecheck`: the Plausible corpus of `PropertyChecks.lean`, `--seed`, `--samples`. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Checks.lean` | Boundary regressions and headline axiom audits for the package. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/PropertyChecks.lean` | Plausible checks of local rules: the emitted update against the typed statement (`checkUpdateWith`), 36 named cases. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Rewriting.lean` | The strategy as a reduction system: `Fml.Steps`, `Fml.Via`, `Fml.Leads` as CSLib/Mathlib closures. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ProofTheory.lean` | `Proves` and its soundness in CSLib's proof-theory vocabulary; the Lindenbaum Boolean algebra. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ModelTheory.lean` | The Theory as a first-order structure (Mathlib model theory); compactness (`FO.finite_support`, `FO.finite_storage_support`). |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ModelTheory/LowenheimSkolem.lean` | Relative compactness, upward/downward Löwenheim–Skolem, elementary-submodel preservation of closed first-order leaves. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Fresh.lean` | `Var` as CSLib's `HasFresh`. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Loop.lean` | `TLoop.run` as Mathlib's first-return map `PFun.fix`; invariant, variant and total-correctness rules independent of the core's. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/LoopFixpoint.lean` | `OrderHom.lfp` of one iteration equals `PFun.fix`; well-founded total correctness. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/WeakestPre.lean` | Predicate transformers of the actual modalities in Mathlib's set lattice; weakest sufficient precondition, guarded duality. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/WeakestPreLaws.lean` | Lattice laws of the actual wp and of the closer's `Modality.wp` helper (`rawWp`), their relation (`wp_eq_rawWp`), and why box does not preserve top (`wp_box_top_iff`). |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith.lean` | Umbrella of the arithmetic bridges below: every admitted operator at every width against `BitVec`/`ZMod`/`Int`. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/Basic.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/Checked.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/Wrapping.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/SignedWrapping.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/NarrowWrapping.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/Division.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/NarrowDivision.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/SignedDivision.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/Comparison.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/Bitwise.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/NarrowBitwise.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/Shifts.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/NarrowShifts.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/Casts.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/Widths.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/NarrowChecked.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/OverflowGuards.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/CheckedExecution.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/Modular.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/Signature.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/Lowering.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/Power.lean` | One operator family each (`docs/bridge.md`, "Arithmetic at every width"); `Arith/Power.lean` holds the checked-power counterexamples. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/WidthsSyntax.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/WidthsSignedShift.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/WidthsObstructions.lean` | Stable structural checker facts, the separate signed-right-shift path, and counterexamples to the old generic helper contracts. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/LoweringObstructions.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Arith/GuardRetentionExamples.lean` | Counterexample to the old guard-retention statement and checked flag/mode regressions for the corrected statement (`docs/bridge.md`). |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Evm.lean` | The EVM fragment as a CSLib `LTS` of instruction traces; trace soundness and completeness against the compiler theorem. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/CompilerSimulation.lean` | CSLib bisimulation, simulation and trace equivalence of successful program boundaries. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm.lean` | Numeric stack instructions against EVMYulLean's word operations and `execBinOp`. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Environment.lean` | SELFBALANCE, ADDRESS, CALLER, CALLVALUE, TIMESTAMP against EvmYul's account and transaction state. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/WordOps.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/SignedOps.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Power.lean` | Comparisons, shifts and unary words; signed comparison/division/remainder; exponentiation by squaring. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Dispatch.lean` | The core's instruction steps against `EvmYul.EVM.step` on numeric stacks. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/StackOps.lean` | Legal DUP1–16 and SWAP1–16. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Obstructions.lean` | Proved disagreements (CALL, REVERT, jumps, fixed memory, symbolic slots) that rule out a one-opcode identity theorem. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Memory.lean` | Actual memory dispatch and fixed/dynamic macros; single-read stack agreement, exact paired store updates, and active-extent counterexample. The primitive/macro layer for the bounded refinement below. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/MemoryBytes.lean` | Pure 32-byte encoding, conditional actual serialization and in-bounds byte write/readback/frame laws; explicit opaque allocator contract. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/MemoryRefinement.lean` | Persistent preallocated numeric-cell/byte relation, all four actual core/macro simulations, independently inhabited input, composed readback and alignment controls. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/MemoryComposition.lean` | Arbitrarily populated pure two-cell inputs; actual fixed/dynamic store/load program compositions, persistent postrelation, exact stack tails and nonzero-neighbor frame controls. |
| `Bridge/tests/MemoryObserve.lean` | Separate native core/upstream memory observations, driven by `scripts/check-external-memory.sh` with the verbatim pinned allocator; does not prove its contract. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/CodeLayout.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/LayoutChecks.lean` | Typed forward-label resolution, checked raw-byte layouts and offset/refusal controls; execution refinement remains separate. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/Control.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/Loop.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/ControlChecks.lean` | Actual local jump macros/fetched traces, paired observations, driver refusal controls and constant-loop counted prefixes; composed simulation is in the modules below. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/Composition.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/Structured.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/Placement.lean` | Typed source-boundary/frame and placed-code relations; general conditional sequence/branch/nested-loop fetched-run simulation and unbounded loop prefixes under explicit decoder/layout certificates. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/CompositionChecks.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/StackLoopChecks.lean` | Inhabited relocated branch and variable-length stack-consuming loop certificates, kernel simulation proofs, paired source/target and scanner/driver controls. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/Static.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/StaticLoop.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/Automatic.lean` | Finite typed layout checks and automatic recursive execution certificates for accepted placed control-flow images. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/Scanner.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/Scanned.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/Linked.lean` | Actual parse/next-PC scanner certificates, recursive target membership and linked checked images; no opaque D_J equality assumed. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/Driver.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/AutoChecks.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/LinkedChecks.lean` | Charged X prefix refinement under checked finite readiness, exact terminal JD/STOP relation, kernel-inhabited automatic loop/scanner/readiness and actual X observations/refusals. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/Termination.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/PureFetched.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/TerminationChecks.lean` | Checked final JD/STOP placement and gates, exact charged prefix endpoints, source-related X success and inhabited general proof-path controls. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/SafeTrace.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/SafeComposition.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/SafeLocal.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/SafeStructured.lean` | Actual scanner-safe fetched microsteps, source sequence/branch/loop composition and recursive consumption of stored ScannedCode memberships. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/ResourceReady.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/SafeFetched.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/ResourceChecks.lean` | Original driver gates from resource-only readiness plus compiler-derived destination safety; actual X success and finite safety/resource controls. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/NestedDivergence.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/SafeDivergence.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/NestedDivergenceChecks.lean`, `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/SafeDivergenceChecks.lean` | Source divergence witnesses and unbounded scanner-safe fetched prefixes through conditions, bodies, sequence prefixes and finite earlier outer turns; automatic nested certificate and later-entry controls. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/LayoutFacts.lean` | Uniqueness of typed image data and raw-validator acceptance of already proved images; no semantic admission completeness claim. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/ExternalEvm/Assembly/PopChecks.lean` | Automatic certified PUSH/POP/PUSH success, safe atomic POP witness and exact empty-stack source/fetched/driver underflow controls. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Adversary.lean` | An independent adversary LTS at a payment boundary; `holdsC_iff_all_policies`, `holdsC_iff_all_callees`. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/AdversaryPolicy.lean` | A fixed operational callee has exactly the outcomes of its reachable return policy. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/CallbackTrace.lean` | Completed callback outcomes as observable traces; weak/strong trace equivalence and bisimulation preserve the callback modality. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/CallbackModalTrace.lean` | Trace/bisimulation invariance of every framed callback modality (an adapter over `ExecP`/`ExecF`). |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/Loom.lean` | Loom's exception algebra with the actual modal halt policy, equality to `afterRunTR` and the existing bridge WP, and bind inherited from `wp_comp`. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/LoomRules.lean` | One translation and soundness theorem for all taclets, plus Loom generators from `Fml.Leads`, `Fml.Steps`, and the existing bounded strategy, with fixed deep posts. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/LoomVCGen.lean` | Deep-post annotations for the actual core run, one generic Loom specification registration, and the strategy-leaf/continuation-entailment VC equation. |
| `Bridge/SolidityBridge/LoomExamples.lean` | Loom VC generation for straight-line Solidity, a Solidity function inside a Lean bind/if program, halt-policy boundaries, and the existing invariant loop rule. |
| `Bridge/vendor/Loom/MonadAlgebras/WP/Tactic.lean` | The upstream Loom VC-generator import closure, with two explicit instance names for editor checks; provenance and checksums in `Bridge/vendor/Loom/VENDOR.md`. |
| `Bridge/vendor/CaseStudies/Velvet/Std.lean` | Velvet's front end (`method`, `prove_correct`, `loom_solve`), vendored verbatim from Loom's last Velvet commit with four of its examples; provenance and checksums in `Bridge/vendor/CaseStudies/VENDOR.md`. |
| `Bridge/SolidityVelvet/Contract.lean` | `contract C { … }`: a Solidity contract (storage, functions, `ensures`, loop `invariant`s) read as one Velvet method per function; `Mapping`, Solidity's reverts as `assume`s. |
| `Bridge/SolidityVelvet.lean` | Three of the core's Solidity programs as a contract (a counting loop, a largest-balance scan, ERC20's `transfer`), proved by `loom_solve` and pinned by `#eval`; not related to `Stmt.run`. |
| `Bridge/SolidityVelvet/TestSuite.lean` | solkey's `TestSuite.sol` in Velvet: 83 of its functions, under their names, in one contract, each `assert` proved (`prove_contract`). |
| `Bridge/SolidityVelvet/Bank.lean` | A bank contract: `deposit` (reverting past `2^256 - 1`), `withdraw`, `balance`. |
| `Bridge/SolidityVelvet/Token.lean` | An ERC20 token contract: `mint`, `transfer`, `approve`, `transferFrom` over mappings. |
| `Bridge/SolidityVelvet/Loops.lean` | A contract's loops over storage arrays: the total balance, a holder's index, the winning proposal. |

| Module | What it defines |
|---|---|
| `Frontend/Cli.lean` | JSON request adapter for source obligation, goal and ordinary proof-tree inspection, and typed concrete calls; the native launcher is `SolidityLean.lean`. |
| `Tools/SolCases.lean` | Closed typed primitive-root Solidity printer, named refusals, deterministic bounded program/storage cases and actual interpreter observations for the optional real-solc/EthereumJS harness. |
| `Examples/SolCases.lean` | Concrete kernel parser/printer round trip, typed literal division and explicit refusal regressions. |
| `KeySort.lean` | solkey's sort lattice as one type. Imports nothing. |
| `Language/Features.lean` | Pinned Solidity language catalog, scope/current-status records, finite structured exclusions and diagnostics, requirements/backlog lists, and a feature-parameterized syntax subset. Generated from `docs/solidity-features.json`. |
| `Language/FixedPoint.lean` | Reference semantics of `fixedMxN`/`ufixedMxN` (not wired into the AST, elaborator or compiler): intrinsic types `FxTy` and values `Fx` (scaled bounded integers), checked/unchecked arithmetic with `0x11`/`0x12` panics (`Fail`), conversions, literals, ABI words and their laws (`Fx.add_eq`, `Fx.ofWord_toWord`, `Fx.solc_lossy`). Imports nothing; design in `docs/feature-work/fixed-point-semantics.md`. |
| `Language/FixedPoint/Laws.lean` | Fixed-point laws: checked/unchecked agreement, `*`/`/` exactness and truncation bounds, lossless conversion round trips, `implicitOK = subsumes` and value preservation (`FxTy.implicitOK_iff`, `Fx.mul_trunc`, `Fx.div_trunc`, `Fx.explicit_implicit`). |
| `Language/FixedPoint/Literals.lean` | Fixed-point constants: exact rational folding with solc's compile errors (`CExpr.eval`), implicit/explicit constant conversions and their laws (`Fx.ofLiteral?_isSome_iff_solc`, `Fx.explicitConst?_eq_none_iff`), solc's mobile type (`mobileFx?`, `mobileFx?_inRange`) and the common type with a constant operand (`litCommon?`). |
| `Language/FixedPoint/Abi.lean` | Fixed-point ABI: 32-byte two's-complement words and bytes (`Fx.encode`, `Fx.abiEncode`), coder v2 decoding with dirty-upper-bit rejection (`Fx.decodeV2_eq_none_iff`, `Fx.decodeV2_isSome_iff`, `Fx.abiEncode_of_abiDecodeV2`), coder v1 cleaning (`Fx.decodeV1_of_decodeV2`), packed encoding and canonical selector names. |
| `Language/FixedPoint/Storage.lean` | Fixed-point values packed in storage slots: byte ranges of a word with read-after-write and frame laws (`field_setField`, `field_setField_below`/`_above`), `Fx.load`/`Fx.store` (`Fx.load_store`, `Fx.load_store_disjoint`), solc's packing of a sequence (`place_fits`, `place_pairwise`), slots of a contract with the per-variable frame of a layout (`Fx.layout_frame`) and mapping keys (`Fx.keyWord_injective`). |
| `Language/FixedPoint/Conformance.lean` | Kernel-checked verdicts of the decided fixed-point rules on the pinned upstream syntax tests (`decide +kernel` examples, one per test file), classified in `docs/feature-work/fixed-point-semantics.md` §6.5. |
| `Eqns.lean` | `attribute [sol_eqns l₁ … lₙ] f` (Mathlib's `@[eqns]`, named apart for `Bridge/`): hand-named equation lemmas for a definition written with a recursor (irreducible, so `simp only [f]` rewrites constructor applications only; the kernel still reduces it). |
| `AST.lean` | Types (`PrimTy`/`Ty`/`RefTy`, `RefTy.fixed`), the struct table (a contract's own struct a name spelling its members, `StructEnc`, kept by `structDefEnc`'s check; `structDef_rec`), operators, `Var`; the association lists (`lookupBy`, `setBy`, `nodupKeysB`) and their laws (`lookupBy_setBy_self`, …). |
| `TypeGraph.lean` | Separate finite declaration graph, typed member positions and inline-layout rank certificate; excludes direct/fixed cycles while allowing mapping/dynamic-array recursion. |
| `Semantics/RecursiveStorage.lean` | Graph-storage/copy/admission/legacy-compatibility umbrella; ordinary Contract, State and the run still use existing types. |
| `Semantics/RecursiveStorage/Values.lean` | Typed well-founded values with dependent field functions, bounded array extents and demand-driven slots. |
| `Semantics/RecursiveStorage/Defaults.lean` | Defaults by guarded layout rank, with lazy fields and guarded edges. |
| `Semantics/RecursiveStorage/Paths.lean` | Finite typed paths, bounds-sensitive read/save, printers and generic read-after-save/composition proofs. |
| `Semantics/RecursiveStorage/Clear.lean` | Mapping-barrier deletion, retained-tail frame, idempotence and preservation of defaults. |
| `Semantics/RecursiveStorage/Arrays.lean` | Empty push, pop, live/retained reads, typed observations and array preservation laws. |
| `Semantics/RecursiveStorage/Copy.lean` | Graph storage snapshot copy: destination mapping barriers, live-prefix overlay, retained shadows and copy read-back laws; standalone graph API, not ordinary recursive source support. |
| `Semantics/RecursiveStorage/Admission.lean` | Checked closed mapping-free graph markings, typed copy permits and source-facing graph assignment interfaces; not yet called by solc_import. |
| `Semantics/RecursiveStorage/Legacy.lean` | Finite intrinsic layout compatibility with ordinary SVal/SFields, arbitrary mapping fallbacks, typed subtree assignment and mapping-path correspondence proof scripts. |
| `Semantics/RecursiveStorage/Observations.lean` | Observational equivalence laws, appended-path observations, subtree descent and explicit/implicit default mapping equivalence proof scripts. |
| `Examples/RecursiveCopy.lean` | Recursive snapshot/shadow copies, copy permits, ordinary-value subtree assignment and struct/array/fixed/mapping compatibility regressions for the standalone graph API. |
| `Examples/RecursiveStorage.lean` | Kernel recursive mapping/array/mutual schemas, retained-slot and bounds regressions, and headline axiom output. |
| `Syntax.lean` | The grammar behind `sol[C]{…}` and `contract!{…}` (`sol_stmt`, `sol_expr`, `sol_ty`; the raw syntax `RawStmt`, `RawExpr`), `Contract`/`FunDecl`, expression scheduling (`EvalOrder`), the index types of the typed syntax (`ArrTy`, `IndexTy`), and the quoters' helpers (`rflTrue`, `rflSome`, …); the typed syntax is `Syntax/Scoped.lean`'s.  Split by topic into the pieces below (`docs/parallelism.md`), this module the umbrella. |
| `Syntax/Raw.lean` | The raw syntax `sol{ … }` reads (`RawStmt`), the contract (its storage types certified by `vars_ok`, its root names by `vars_nodup`; `Contract.root_okDeep`), narrow, byte and function types, index types, printing helpers. |
| `Syntax/Grammar.lean` | The grammar of `sol{ … }` and the taclets' schema forms, expanded. |
| `Syntax/Contract.lean` | `contract!{ … }`, the contracts, quoting. |
| `Scope.lean` | The scoped syntax's contexts: `LTy`, `Ctx := List LTy`, positions `Idx`, suffixes `Ext` (scope ends), thinnings `Thin` (weakening), `Binders`. |
| `Syntax/Scoped.lean` | The typed syntax scoped (M2 of `docs/intrinsic.md`): `Stmt C Γ Γ'`/`Prog C Γ Γ'` over `Idx`, scoped branches and blocks, calls closed over their frame (`Args`, `CallRet`), the printers (names beside the context) and `Stmt.wk`.  What every semantics, rule and proof reads; the elaborator emits it (`Syntax/Elab.lean`) and the printers read it (`Calculus/PrintStmt.lean`). |
| `Syntax/DispatchChain.lean` | The finite dispatch chain a call through an internal function value elaborates to (`DispatchArm`, `DispatchArm.chain`: one guarded arm per candidate, then the unassigned-value failure); `fnDispatch` emits it. |
| `Syntax/Checked.lean` | `checked { … }` and `ignoringOverflow { … }` (`docs/options.md`, O2, O5): the range guards of a statement's checked operations, in evaluation order (`Val.guard`, `Stmt.guard`, `Checked.rangeU`/`rangeI`), and `Prog.withOF`, each statement preceded by its guard's statement (`assert`, or `if (g) {} else { assume(false); }`), a loop's condition guarded again at the end of its body.; in a self-call's callee, each statement's checks with solc's `Panic` codes (`Val.panics`, `Stmt.panics`, `Prog.withPanics`: `0x11`, `0x12`, `0x31`, `0x32`). |
| `Syntax/CheckedExact.lean` | The guards are exact: each holds exactly when its operation stays in range (`add_uint_exact`, `mul_uint_exact`, `add_int_exact`, `sub_int_exact`, `neg_int_exact`, `div_int_exact`, `mul_int_exact`). |
| `Syntax/PushSource.lean` | Typed primitive path reads, complex-storage-read tests and memory-source classification/admission, shared by push-source schemas, dispatcher and fragment proofs. |
| `Syntax/Join.lean` | `Prog.join`, `P` then `Q`: the elaborator's and the block arms', below both (out of `Syntax/Elab.lean`, `docs/parallelism.md`). |
| `Syntax/Inlined.lean` | A scoped block printed with its calls inlined, for display (`Prog.inlinedStr`, `Prog.inlinedShow`): each call as KeY's `expand_function_body` leaves it, the arguments, return variables, body and result; a `{ … }` block's statements in place. |
| `Keccak.lean` | Keccak-256 over bytes, computed (`#guard`ed against known digests and SHA3-256's sponge): the selectors the elaborator folds as solc does (`Keccak.selector`); no proof reads it (`docs/semantic-gaps.md`, "Hashing and the ABI"). |
| `Hashes.lean` | SHA-256 and RIPEMD-160 over bytes, computed as `Keccak.lean` is (`#guard`ed against the standard vectors): `sha256` and `ripemd160` of a program (`sha256Bytes`, `ripemd160Bytes`, `Semantics/Heap.lean`; `docs/semantic-gaps.md`, "Hashing and the ABI"). |
| `Hash/Adaptive.lean` | Adaptive queries against a fixed random function: `eagerAdaptiveBound` proves `EagerAdaptiveBound`; repeated queries and early stopping (`two_mul_eagerCollides_le`, `two_mul_eager_stopped_le`, `keccak_eager_collision_prob_le`) and the region premise (`two_mul_eagerRegionsFail_le`, `keccak_eager_region_prob_le`), by a union bound over the first bad query ([hash-collision-bound.md](feature-work/hash-collision-bound.md)). |
| `Hash/CollisionBound.lean` | The approved hash premise (G01 Q3 / G04 Q1): per-run `CollisionFree f xs` with sublist/prefix/append lemmas, and the random-oracle birthday bound by counting (`collisions_le`, `keccak_collision_prob_le`, `keccak_premise_fails_prob_le`; adaptive queries under lazy sampling, `adaptive_lazy_collision_prob_le`); the eager adaptive case is stated as `EagerAdaptiveBound` and proved in `Hash/Adaptive.lean`; the second-round region premise `RegionsDisjoint` (`Near`, `Covers`) and its bound `keccak_region_prob_le`, `keccak_region_premise_fails_prob_le` ([hash-collision-bound.md](feature-work/hash-collision-bound.md)). |
| `Storage/HashedLayout.lean` | The hashed storage layout at word granularity (G27.05 preparation): slot terms `Loc` (root, mapping entry, array data) read as words through a hash parameter (`Loc.word`), types `STy` and access paths (`STy.locate`) with offsets kept inside their regions (`STy.locate_bounded`), and separation under the region premise (`Loc.word_inj`, `Loc.nodup_map_word`, `located_word_inj`) ([storage-hashed-layout.md](feature-work/storage-hashed-layout.md)). |
| `Storage/HashedLayoutEvm.lean` | Read-only correspondence of `Storage.Loc` with the EVM model's `Evm.Slot` (`ofSlot`, injective, `ofSlot_add`, `exists_ofSlot` for word keys, `lmax_eq_Lmax`) and separation read on slots (`slot_word_inj`). |
| `Storage/HashedKeccak.lean` | The hashed layout under keccak-256 (G46.05 preparation): the ABI preimage encoding `encode` (injective on words, `encode_inj`), the hash parameter `keccakH` (`keccakH_lt`), the premises read on the encoded `KECCAK256` inputs (`collisionFree_keccakH_iff`, `regionsDisjoint_keccakH_iff`), `keccak_word_inj`, and `#guard` vectors against solc's mapping/array slot formula. |
| `Storage/HashedSeparation.lean` | G27.01–G27.03 separation over the hashed layout: occupancy `Occ`, ancestry `Reach` (`occ_reach`, `reach_unique`), disjoint struct members, mapping entries, array elements and array header (`occ_fields_disjoint`, `occ_keys_disjoint`, `occ_sarr_disjoint`, `occ_darr_disjoint`, `occ_darr_len_elem`, `occ_map_slot_entry`), and their word-level reading (`occ_bounded`, `occ_word_ne`, `keys_word_ne`). |
| `ERC7201.lean` | Namespace formula over bytes with a bounded result and an alignment theorem; runtime/literal lowering uses the same operations. |
| `Secp256k1.lean` | Bounded modular arithmetic and checked curve points for computed address recovery; no general group-law or EVM-equivalence proof. |
| `Syntax/Elab.lean` | The elaborator: the raw passes that fix solc's evaluation order and the fresh names (`hoist`, `captureExpr`, `lowerLoops`, `lowerReturns`, `wrapMods`, `renameStmts`, `freshenBlocks`), then `synth`/`check` into the scoped syntax, a local by `NCtx.find`; statements typed where they are elaborated (`SStmt`) and chained into a block (`SProg.chain`, weakened past a flat block's locals); named contexts (`NCtx`, `NCtx.find`).  Split by topic into the pieces below (`docs/parallelism.md`), this module the umbrella. |
| `Syntax/Elab/Context.lean` | Named contexts (`NCtx`), elaborator bindings with attached width/ABI metadata (`LocalTy`, `ECtx`), and the raw passes the typing leaves alone: traversals, `calldata` parameters, block locals renamed apart, calldata slices, the recursion thunk. |
| `Syntax/Elab/Values.lean` | Memory objects, function values, selectors and external function values, narrow widths, byte types; implementation helpers have no source function values. |
| `Syntax/Elab/Lower.lean` | Inlining a call, loops lowered, `abi.*`/`string.concat`/`bytes.concat` and the ABI of nested types. |
| `Syntax/Elab/SliceCopy.lean` | Primitive and fixed primitive-row slice copies through existing typed allocation, loops and stores; fresh row identities and explicit unsupported-reference refusal. |
| `Syntax/Elab/Arms.lean` | `ElabRec` and the large arms that take it (`hoistWith`, `abiEncodeWith`, `elabCallRetWith`, `hoistStmtWith`, `elabStmtPWith`, `elabStmt1With`). |
| `Syntax/Elab/Stmt.lean` | The `mutual` block that ties the arms (`elabRec`): statements, calls, blocks. |
| `Syntax/Quote.lean` | The scoped syntax quoted (`Prog.quote`, `Idx` as `here`/`there`, proofs `Eq.refl`), contexts shared and the term hash-conses (`quoteShared`); `Prog.define` declares a block for the kernel to check. |
| `Syntax/LoopWarnings.lean` | AU03 literal-loop diagnostic classification and source spans; reachable callee/constructor/initializer paths, annotations suppress only the annotated loop; no change to unrolling. |
| `Syntax/Splice.lean` | The elaborator's entry points (`elabProgS`, `elabProgAtS`, `ctorProgS`), imported-source name provenance, `sol[C]{…}`/`sol{…}` (the scoped block, quoted and hash-consed), and their pins (a local out of scope or at another type an elaboration error). |
| `FreshNames.lean` | `FreshNames.ofTable`: the examples' names for the rules' fresh variables, one table per example; `FreshNames.clashes`. |
| `SpecSyntax.lean` | The specification language (`SolSpec.g4`: `SpecExpr`, `spec!(…)`), fixed unsigned literals `uint(n)`, and a function's clauses (`FunSpec`). |
| `Semantics.lean` | Typed primitives (`PrimTy.Denote`), typed values with their types (`CallValue`: call inputs, tool input and output), `ExtKey`, `sendKey`, external responses as ABI words (`Word`, `ExtResult`) and selector labels (`Selector`). The state is `Semantics/State.lean`'s. |
| `Semantics/NoPanic.lean` | Only an `assert` panics, over the typed run: `TRes.NoPanic` and the evaluators' no-panic lemmas (`Val.evalT_noPanic` and kin), `Stmt.asserts`/`Prog.asserts`, each step but `assert` does not panic (`TRun.*_noPanic`), so a program with no `assert` never does (`Prog.run_noPanic`, `Prog.run_ne_panic`), on every typed state. |
| `Calculus/SoundPush.lean` | Primitive push suffixes: two successive updates agree with the actual typed run, including source-first and receiver-once captures and failure in either stage. |
| `Semantics/Value.lean` | Typed primitive defaults, range-checked integers (`PrimTy.ofInt?`), ABI words (`PrimTy.toWord`, `ofWord?`), a typed value read at a type (`CallValue.as?`), mapping-key observations (`PrimTy.key`); `Ledger` has bounded address keys and unbounded signed amounts, with numeric observations for the EVM bridge (`Ledger.entries`, `lookup_entries`, `entries_setBy`). |
| `Semantics/Ops.lean` | The operators on typed values (`BinOp.evalT`, `UnOp.evalT`; arithmetic on `Int`, `BinOp.arithInt`, the range check `PrimTy.ofInt?`), and the `Int` helpers `Semantics.uintBound`, `intBound`, `uintBitwise`. |
| `Semantics/Storage.lean` | Typed storage (M4b): `SVal T` mutual with `SFields` over `structDef` (exact members), `SList`/`SVec`/`SMap`; `Fold`, the recursor of the five families (`SVal.fold`, `match` twin `foldM`), and the functions on a whole value as folds with `eqns` equations (`toList`, `SVec.set`, `defaultOf`, `strip`, `overlay`, `beq`), each recursor form with its `match` twin as `implemented_by` and a `_eq_…M` theorem; views computed from the index (`SVal.view`/`Shape`, `SFields.view`, `SVec.view`, `uncons`); `DecidableEq (SVal T)` and `(SFields fs)` by `beq`, reduced by `decide +kernel`; structural defaults `SVal.dflt` (fuel `rankBound`); typed segments `Seg T U`/`Segs T U` (composable endpoints), root segment sequences `RefSegments C U` (`SRef.segments`), typed paths `Path T U` (`Path.segments`, comparison/schema observations `Path.labels`); paths (`append` by `Path.rec`, `index` by `casesOn`), `SRef C U` (a key as hashed, `PrimTy.key`), `Storage C := SFields C.vars`; find/save by `Path.rec` over `SVal.view`, with `match` twins `findM`/`findLiveM`/`saveM` (and `SFields.getM`/`setM`) as `implemented_by`, `checkIndex` (`inBounds` by `casesOn`), `len`, `push`, `pushPlace`, `pop` through the view, `delete`, `write`, revert as `none`.; a save read back (`SVal.findM_saveM`) and the frame (`Path.Diverges`, `SVal.findM_saveM_diverges`).  Split by topic into the pieces below (`docs/parallelism.md`), this module the umbrella. |
| `Semantics/NumericLowering.lean` | Arithmetic proofs for signed shift saturation, word shifting, magnitude/parity powers and checked signed ranges; no runtime representation change. |
| `Semantics/Storage/Values.lean` | Storage values, folds, views, lists/vectors/maps as lists, members by name, defaults, paths. |
| `Semantics/Storage/Ops.lean` | Find and save with their `match` twins, delete and copy over, equality, the storage, the stores. |
| `Semantics/Storage/Anon.lean` | Modifies sets at the roots: `Storage.anon L old new` (KeY's `anon(heap, locset, anonHeap)`), `Storage.Framed`, their laws (`get_anon`, `find_anon`, `Framed.save`), the laws of `SFields.set` they need. |
| `Semantics/StorageWt.lean` | The test `wt(storage)` runs (`Storage.wtT`, `Signature.lean`), folds the kernel evaluates (Gate G0): `HeredB`, `SVal.allB`, the node tests `canonHB`, `tightHB`, `cleanTailHB` (`ordinaryDefaultBFold`, O6); `Storage.shapeB`, `wtB` (`Typing/CanonTest.lean` proves what they decide); `Storage.cleanTailB`, what `cleanTail(storage)` (`Op1.cleanTail`) tests. |
| `Semantics/Heap.lean` | Typed heap (M4c): a heap typing `HTy := List RefTy` (identity = position), `HRef H R`, `MVal H T`/`MObj H R` computed from the typing (no mapping object), `Heap H`; `get`/`set`/`alloc` (grows to `H ++ [R]`, weakening along `<+:`), member and element reads and writes; `SVal.copyStToM` (`Grown`, a fold), `MVal.copyMToSt` by rank and type (no cycle arm); computed byte hashes (`keccakBytes`, `sha256Bytes`, `ripemd160Bytes`) and secp256k1 recovery (`ecrecoverKey`), kept irreducible for symbolic proof cost; no collision or external-precompile equivalence assumption. |
| `Semantics/State.lean` | The typed state (M4): `LTy.TDenote C H` (a value of its type, an `SRef`, an `HRef H R`), `TEnv C H Γ` (computed, weakened along a heap extension, `TEnv.get_wk`), `State C Γ` (typed storage, heap typing `H` as a field, typed heap), its global part `State.glob` (from `Signature.lean`); `adopt` (a `Grown` heap, locals weakened), `alloc`, `copyStToM`, `allocDefault`, `copyMToSt`. |
| `Semantics/Run.lean` | The run, typed (M4), the only run of a statement: `SPath`/`Loc` to an `SRef`, `MLoc` to a heap address (`MAddr`: a struct member or an element of an `HRef`), `MPath` to the slot it holds (an `HRef` at a reference type), `Val.evalT` by `BinOp.evalT`/`UnOp.evalT`, `Stmt.run`/`Prog.run` on `State` (the recursor, `match` twin `runC`); halts only by `THalt` (`revert`, `panic`, `diverge`, `exhausted`: a push past the bound, decision 15), never stuck; loop induction (`TLoop.run_induct`).  Its example programs are `Examples/ScopedRuns.lean`'s. |
| `Semantics/Termination.lean` | Dependency-free well-founded loop measures, explicit finite iteration witnesses, lexicographic reset measures, and successful termination of actual scoped loops. |
| `Semantics/LexMetrics.lean` | Typed tuple evaluation, well-founded lexicographic natural ranks, and strict successful-loop certificates tied to the actual stored source metrics. |
| `Calculus/LexVariant.lean` | Typed tuple snapshots and lexicographic formulas that retain every component and its source order. |
| `Calculus/LexVariantSound.lean` | Snapshot definedness, canonical landing, state preservation and exact agreement of lexicographic formulas with natural-tuple order. |
| `Calculus/LexDiamond.lean` | Ordinary tuple-diamond verification conditions, conservative final-turn decrease and explicit dispatch boundaries. |
| `Calculus/LoopInvariantWk.lean` | Typed weakening of quantified loop clauses and complete invariants, including the scalar diamond's variant snapshot. |
| `Calculus/Sound/LoopLexDiamond.lean` | Soundness of tuple verification conditions for ordinary diamonds, preserving their accepted-exhaustion policy. |
| `Examples/LexVariant.lean` | Reset, equality, increase and undefined-later-component regressions for tuple snapshots and comparisons. |
| `Examples/LexDiamond.lean` | Actual source-loop verification conditions, successful exits, accepted exhaustion and strict-certificate distinctions. |
| `Examples/LexRuleSyntax.lean` | Exact typed replay of symbolic tuple rules, erasure disjointness and full rule-text coverage. |
| `Examples/LexTermSize.lean` | Explicit tuple-arity update budgets, the non-lex two-update bound and the unchanged global linear node bound. |
| `Examples/Tactics/LexDiamond.lean` | Automatic two/three-component diamonds, resets, scalar regression and rejected or unsupported tuple paths. |
| `Calculus/Preconditions.lean` | An entry-condition check indexed by its source formula, impossible/possible certificates, uniqueness, vacuous validity and mutual exclusion. |
| `Calculus/FiniteMembership.lean` | Typed finite equality-membership and its general denotation theorem against Theory equality. |
| `Calculus/FilteredMembership.lean` | Typed finite literal-domain filters with an outer key, a genuine typed guard binder and an exact scoped denotation theorem. |
| `Tools/Preconditions.lean` | Bounded kernel-checked entry-condition audits, the reusable runner API and `#audit_preconditions`. |
| `Tools/SourceCounterexample.lean` | Exact-goal refutation proofs coupled to the same serialized typed model prestate; primitive storage and input binders, bounded initial search and shrinking. |
| `Examples/Termination.lean` | Lexicographic reset proofs for generic and source-elaborated Solidity loops, with actual divergent/exhausted/reverting negative cases. |
| `Examples/LexMetrics.lean` | An actual tuple-annotated source program proved to return successfully; equal, increasing, undefined and exhausted-body certificates rejected. |
| `Calculus/Sound/LoopLexErase.lean` | Ordinary execution is unchanged by replacing a proof annotation; the tuple erasure rule uses this equality without claiming metric decrease. |
| `Examples/Tactics/LexAnnotations.lean` | Ordinary box proofs after tuple annotation erasure, Lean-only rule classification and printer output. |
| `Examples/Preconditions.lean` | Possible and impossible source preconditions, a vacuous specification and unknown-shape reporting, with axiom pins. |
| `Examples/SourceCounterexample.lean` | Exact-goal source refutations tied to the displayed candidate, rejected unsupported/open/mismatched goals, full-width values and certificate axiom pins. |
| `Examples/Tactics/FiniteSets.lean` | Typed finite membership in specifications: checked source proofs, malformed operands, snapshot traversal and parser/printer roundtrips. |
| `Examples/Tactics/FilteredSets.lean` | Finite literal filters: typed binder positions, key definedness, snapshot and name traversal, malformed empty domains and printer roundtrips. |
| `Semantics/RevertMemory.lean` | Current-heap computed/raw revert operands, unset sources and exact statement data; bounded non-divergent halts and successful runs lifted to unbounded execution; exact self-call halt bytes transported into a typed catch allocation from the original caller state. |
| `Semantics/RevertData.lean` | `Panic(uint256)`'s code decoded as a word (`decodePanic`); the byte layouts are `RevertBytes.lean`'s. |
| `Semantics/AbiEntry.lean` | solc's ABI coder v2 entry decoder over typed shapes and values (`Shape`, `Val`, `Seq`, `Vals`): eager memory decoding with allocation bounds (`mdec`), calldata entry checks (`cref`) and lazy access (`cval`, `tailRef`), `entry`, `verdict`, canonical `encodeArgs`. Imports nothing. |
| `Semantics/AbiEntryProofs.lean` | Eager and lazy decoding agree (`mdec_cval`, `eager_cref`); certified reverts are entry failures (`verdict_revert_iff`, `verdict_run_entry`). |
| `Semantics/AbiEntryChecks.lean` | Kernel-evaluated controls of `AbiEntry.verdict` against pinned solc's probe and fixture vectors (`tests/abi-0.8.38/LazyEntry.vectors.json`). |
| `Semantics/AbiAccess.lean` | Access-time failures of calldata elements: the recording lazy reader `cpart` (failing access paths, substitute values) agrees with `cval` (`cpart_agree`, `cpart_of_cval`); one access along a path `cat` reverts exactly below a recorded path (`cat_cpart`); `verdictA` with access-time parameters, conservative over `verdict` (`verdictA_off`). |
| `Semantics/AbiAccessChecks.lean` | Kernel-evaluated controls of `cpart`, `cat` and `verdictA` against pinned solc (`tests/abi-0.8.38/AccessEntry.vectors.json`, `calldata_nested_array_static_reencode_v2.sol`). |
| `Semantics/AllocBytes.lean` | A byte string allocated is a fresh object of its bytes: `litBytes`' run (`new bytes(n)`, a write per byte) ends in `State.allocPush` at `bytesObj` (`TRun.litBytesT_alloc`), the state `Fml.allocAll` reads; the copies stated against abstract grown heaps (`Grown.prims_cons`, `State.adopt_push_alloc`). |
| `RevertBytes.lean` | Revert data as bytes, below the syntax: `Error(string)`'s and `Panic(uint256)`'s selectors and encodings, `decodeError`, ABI words, the uninterpreted `customSelector`. |
| `Semantics/Deploy.lean` | The deployment over the typed run alone: `State.deploy` (the typed deployment state: the initial storage, a ledger holding the deployer's payment, the value sent as the contract's funds) and `Contract.deployT` (refusing value to a constructor that is not `payable`, else the program run from `State.deploy`), shared by `#deploy` (`Tools/Run.lean`) and the constructor obligation (`Calculus/Deploy.lean`). |
| `Semantics/Transient.lean` | Explicit typed transient effects composed with closed `Prog.run` fragments; zero-value self-call sharing, atomic rollback, static restrictions, fresh transaction/reset and lifetime-runner compatibility. Ordinary source uses certified regions of the same logical root product. |
| `Calculus/Transient.lean` | The composed interpreter read with the existing modal halt policy; ordinary `Spec` box/diamond proof bridges and bind soundness. No extension of scoped `Fml` or taclets. |
| `Examples/Tactics/TransientStorage.lean` | Kernel regression declarations for actual ordinary persistent effects composed with transient reentry, rollback/reset, static restrictions, negative witnesses and an ordinary calculus proof bridge; ordinary source, transaction reset and typed call/rollback controls. |
| `Examples/Tactics/TransientSourceImport.lean` | Pinned 0.8.38 source import; declaration-class identity, mandatory successful self-call/rollback execution and a false-assertion panic. |
| `Semantics/Lifetime.lean` | Persistent `Lifetime.World`, explicit `System` interface, constructor-rooted `Init`, commit/rollback/wait `Step`, labelled `Trace`, `Reachable`, invariant induction, `AG`/`EF`/`AGEF` and successful execution witnesses (`docs/lifetime.md`). |
| `Semantics/ContractWorld.lean` | Catalog-indexed instances, explicit global funding, atomic constructor/call/library frames and successful-run witnesses; explicit body/environment obligations, no general source-world execution (`docs/feature-work/contract-world.md`). |
| `Semantics/ContractWorldCertificates.lean` | Exact source return erasure, paid empty-frame correspondence, bare creation-catch laws and funded reachability certificates. |
| `Semantics/TransientWorld.lean` | Opt-in transaction entry/exit over existing typed owner/library operation trees; per-owner reset, commit witness and full failure rollback. Typed frame compositions also have an explicit result-preserving outer boundary, owner normalization and persistent-path laws; raw frames stay unchanged. |
| `Semantics/Ends.lean` | Typed results that do not diverge (`TRes.NoDiv`) and that end (`TRes.Ends`: neither diverge nor are exhausted); every evaluator, and every write but a push, can only halt by a revert (`TRes.RevertOnly`, `Val.evalT_revertOnly` and kin), so it ends (`Val.evalT_ends`). |
| `Semantics/RevertOnly.lean` | Exchanging pure argument evaluation (`TRes.RevertOnly`: values, reference paths and call arguments return or revert) preserves exact failure priority. |
| `Examples/ContractTypes.lean` | Contract declaration certificates: rejection of duplicate roots and unknown struct types in raw records, nested types, record updates and `contract!`; valid nested structs, metadata/root updates, symbolic `ofStruct`, and generic well-formedness/reachability clients. |
| `Examples/ScopedRuns.lean` | Scoped programs written in `sol[C]{ … }` run on typed states by the kernel (`guardLowCore` …, `callsCore`'s calls); out of `Semantics/Run.lean`, which is in the core (`docs/parallelism.md`). |
| `Examples/Tactics/ReferenceParameters.lean` | Storage and memory arguments, shared aliases, copies, failure order and reference contracts. |
| `Examples/Tactics/ArrayShapeCopy.lean` | Fixed-to-dynamic and shorter-to-longer fixed storage snapshots, cleared tails, retained destination histories, composed typed-term and source-printer roundtrips. |
| `Examples/Tactics/OwnStructs.lean` | A contract's own structs (`contract!{ struct S { … } }`): nested, with a mapping member, in an array, built by literals; their names in the table; refused duplicates and self-containment. |
| `Examples/Tactics/Inheritance.lean` | Kernel-checked diamond initialization with virtual dispatch, base constructors in solc's legacy order, `super` chains, and rejection of malformed C3 metadata. |
| `Calculus/RefInputs.lean` | Initialized reference inputs as nonallocating diamond identity updates; source obligations exclude unset hidden references. |
| `Semantics/RunWk.lean` | A weakened program runs as the program, typed (`Stmt.run_wk`, `Prog.run_wk`): `τ.lift h d` (`TEnv.put`/`off`/`thin`), the background weakened along the heap a run grows (`TEnv.wk`, `offWk`), `TWkRel`; per-step lemmas (`TRun.*_wk`); frames run (`Frames.run`); loops on `TLoop`: `run_map`, `run_unfold`, `run_variant`. |
| `Semantics/Dispatch.lean` | Which arm a dispatch chain runs (`DispatchArm.chain_select`, `chain_none`), and per-tag selection (`chain_tag`): with guards `tag == tᵢ`, a run whose tag is `tᵢ` runs arm `i`; that the elaborator's guards and arm bodies are those is by construction (`docs/feature-work/finite-dispatch.md`). |
| `Semantics/RunFrame.lean` | A body within a mutability runs within its frame, typed (`Stmt.run_frame`, `Prog.run_frame`): the heap and its typing kept (`TGFrame`, heaps compared by `HEq`), the locals below kept but where `writesAt d` lists them (`TLocAgree`); `State.anon`, `State.havoc`, and a state in a loop body's frame is one `{anon(xs)}`/`{havoc}` covers (`State.eq_anonHavoc`); the frame theorem of the modifies sets (`Stmt.run_keeps`, `Prog.run_keeps`: a run ends in a storage `Storage.Framed` by `Stmt.storeAt`, the ledger and the balance kept, `FrameKeeps`), and a loop body's (`Prog.frame_keeps`). |
| `Semantics/DeferredSource.lean` | Deferred typed operand prefixes, weakening and snapshot evaluation, and ordinary-run agreement with an actual getter, alias capture and scoped push program; connected to native syntax by `Semantics/NativeDeferred.lean`. |
| `Semantics/DeferredCallback.lean` | Exact callback composition of an explicit getter/capture/prefix/push program through `ExecP`, including halts and violations; supplies the actual-program decomposition used by `Semantics/DeferredCorrespondence.lean`. |
| `Semantics/DeferredCorrespondence.lean` | Independent direct receiver/operand callback semantics and exact agreement with the actual two-alias capture program (`captureProgram_direct_iff`, `callProgram_direct_iff`), preserving prefix effects, source snapshots and heap growth. |
| `Semantics/NativeDeferred.lean` | Ordinary execution agreement of native effectful receiver statements with their actual named capture program (`capture_run`), used by global statement soundness. |
| `Semantics/NativeDeferredCallback.lean` | Exact native/captured callback correspondence (`capture_iff`), including fresh-name transport; used by `ProvesC.unfoldReceiver`. |
| `Semantics/RunFuel.lean` | The typed run with each loop entered at most `n` iterations (`Stmt.runF`, `Prog.runF`, `Frames.runF`; `loopF` on `TLoop.iterN`), its compiled twin (`Stmt.runF_eq`), and its agreement with the typed run but where it says `diverge` (`TRes.Approx`, `Prog.runF_approx`): the counterexample search and its kernel certificates run it. |
| `Semantics/RunAccel.lean` | Counting loops in closed form: a body that only adds literals to `uint` locals (`Prog.bumps?`: `x = x + d`, `x += d`, `x++`, each local once, its run `Prog.BumpsAt`), a condition `x < e`/`x <= e` either way round (`Val.guard?`) over a term the body leaves alone (`Val.rigid?`), run to the iteration the condition fails or the first overflow (`TRun.accel?`, the bounds `guardBound`, `ovfBound`; the head states `State.bumpK`), sound against the typed loop (`TRun.accel?_sound`, by `TLoop.run_of_steps`); `Prog.runA`, `Prog.runF` with such loops accelerated (`TRun.loopA`), its compiled twin (`Prog.runA_eq`) and `Prog.runA_approx`.  The recognizers return their proofs (`match`-based, G0 condition 1's deviation). |
| `Update.lean` | The modalities (`Modality.onHalt`: how a halt reads under each), `IntOp` (the ledger updates' arithmetic).  The typed memory read is `Heap.readAddr` (`Semantics/Run.lean`), the halt policy `Modality.afterRunTR`, `State.havoc`/`State.anon` `Semantics/RunFrame.lean`'s. |
| `Signature.lean` | KeY's signature, typed: `Srt` (`val p`, `path T`, `st`, `sv T`, `ident R`, `addr T`, `mem`, `mv T`, `sts s`/`spath s T` under `select`), `Op0`…`Op3` over the contract (`netOf` is a local's, `Tm.netOf`), `Srt.Den`, `OpN.eval` on the typed global part (`State.glob`, a revert `none`; `casesOn` with `evalM` twins), identities checked on read (`HRef.ofId?`, `MAddrT`, `MvT`); the typed laws `Storage.findOnSave`, `findOnSaveFrameRoot` (the frame below one root is `Storage.find_save_frame`, `Calculus/TermLaws.lean`), `Storage.find_selectT` (`SRef.under`).  `Srt.kint`: KeY's unbounded `int`, apart from `val .int`: the ledger (`Op1.net`, total), `Op1.toInt` (a `uint` widened, total), `Op0.zlit`, `Op2.zbin`/`Op2.zcmp` (`BinOp.evalZ`, `BinOp.cmpZ`). |
| `Theorems.lean` | The headline theorems in notation, over the scoped calculus and the typed run, their axioms pinned: soundness, the rule table against the strategy (`rule_exists`, `rule_of_step`), the solkey fragment at the rule level (`solkey_rule_exists`, `solkey_rule_lt_calculus`, `solkey_step_taclet`) and at the judgement level (`solkey_eq_calculus`, `solkey_lt_calculus`), the compiler (`σ ≈[C, L, b, F] m`: `Evm.Sim` of the typed state). |

## The calculus

| Module | What it defines |
|---|---|
| `Calculus/RuleSyntax.lean` | The `dl{ … }` notation: schemas (taclets, whose formulas are written scoped, `fmlC`; update schemas `{u ‖ {u}u2}`; terms written by their symbols' KeY names and turned into the scoped `Tms` symbols by `coreNames`), the syntax of `tm{ … }` (read by `Calculus/TermLib/Rules.lean`, printed by `Calculus/TermLib.lean`), `⊨ᶠ`/`⊧` (`Fml.Valid`/`holds` of a scoped formula: `elabValidHolds`), and the printers' builders for taclets, premises, goals, terms (`pp.sol.key`: KeY's long forms).  Split by topic into the pieces below (`docs/parallelism.md`), this module the umbrella. |
| `Calculus/RuleSyntax/Grammar.lean` | The grammar of `dl{ … }`/`tm{ … }`, the side conditions' syntax, the first schema macros. |
| `Calculus/RuleSyntax/Schemas.lean` | The schema macros, continued: terms and scoped taclets; `Schema`, the side conditions' elaborator. |
| `Calculus/RuleSyntax/Print.lean` | The delaborators that print the notation back. |
| `Calculus/RuleKit.lean` | What the printers keep of a rule: solkey's branch labels (`Taclet.branchLabels`, `labelledBy`, read off a scoped statement's head) and `delabTacletBinders`. |
| `Calculus/Fml.lean` | Scoped terms, updates and formulas: `Tm C Γ s` at a typed sort (`Tm C Γ (.val p)`, …), `Rhs`, `UpdElem` (an amount and an address a `uint`), `Upd C Γ Δ` (assign, declare, pop), `Frames` (`seq`, `pop .block`/`.capture`, `drop` by a `Thin`), `Fml C Γ` (`eq` at one `PrimTy`, the predicate `wellFormed`) with `modal m P κ φ`, the hidden binder `bind` and `allocAll` (every fresh memory object of any contents, Lean only), `Premise C Γ Γ'` (no cover goal; sequential `updates U V` through an intermediate context, `branches`, `cases`, `inv`, `inline`, `allocBranches` with `Fml.allocGoal`) and `Premise.fml`, `Frames.exit`. |
| `Calculus/Lower.lean` | Scoped program expressions lowered to typed terms (`Val.lower`, …, `Src`/`MSrc.lower`; the root and member proofs passed through; `Simple.lower`, `Tm.castV`, `Tm.one`, `Ty.mpathSrt`), and the update elements the arms build (`UpdElem.save`, `writeMem`, `clearMem`, `push`, …, `Upd.allocCopy`/`allocFresh`/`pushBind`, `OpLoc.cur`/`set`/`op`, `Tm.bumped`). |
| `Calculus/LowerRun.lean` | A lowered expression's term, read on the typed state (`Tm.eval`), against the typed evaluator, nothing erased (`TRes.AgreesO`: the typed value, `none` for a revert, no panic; `.cases`-style binds): `Simple.lower_evalT`, `Simple.denote_lowerT`, `SPath`/`Loc.lower_resolveT`, `MPath.lower_mvalT`, `MLoc.lower_addrT` (`MAddr.toT`), `Val.lower_evalT`, `Src.lower_valueT`, `MSrc.lower_mvalT` (`MVal.toT`); by induction on the syntax. |
| `Calculus/Print.lean` | `Fml.show`: a scoped formula printed in `Calculus/RuleSyntax.lean`'s notation (`}` at a block's end, pops and hidden binders unprinted), its terms, updates and blocks quoted and printed by `Calculus/PrintTerm.lean`'s and `Calculus/PrintStmt.lean`'s printers (`Tm.show`, `Upd.show`, `ppProgS`), and pinned. |
| `Calculus/Shift.lean` | A rule's schema variables shifted under its fresh locals (`Val.up` and its kin, `*.up_eq`: `wk up` without a `Thin`), and a call's body read under its caller (`Thin.atop`, `Idx.under`). |
| `Calculus/Arms.lean` | The arms of `Stmt.step`, the rule of each statement: holes (`SHole`, `MHole`, `VHole`), reads, writes, copies, deletes, the conditional, `&&`/`||`, compound assignments, `++`, push/pop, payments, memory, `if`/`require`/`assert`; each arm names its taclet. |
| `Calculus/SyntaxSize.lean` | The nodes of a term (`Tm.size`) and of a statement (`Stmt.size`, `Prog.size`, the expressions'), below the rules: what `Calculus/TermSize.lean` bounds a rule's output by, and a call summary's gate (`Tm.sumGate`). |
| `Calculus/CallSummary.lean` | A call's summary (`internalCallSummary`, Lean only): the symbolic frame (`SEnv`, each value local a term over the frame it started from and whether its definedness is owed), terms read through it (`Tm.substE`, `Tm.strictReads`), the symbolic run of a body (`Stmt.sym`, `Prog.sym`, by the recursors, `…M` twins), a callee's summary (`CallRet.sumOf`), its gate (`Prog.hasCall`, `Tm.valOnly`, `Tm.sumGate`) and the call's update (`CallRet.summary?`, `CallRet.sumOK`, `CallRet.summary?_isSome`). |
| `Calculus/Blocks.lean` | The rules of calls (inlined, the frame dropped under exported returns by `Premise.inline`), `try` (`Premise.branches`) and loops (`whileUnwind`, `loopExit`, the invariant rules, `Premise.inv`), and the loop frame (`Stmt.within`, `Prog.frame`, the modifies sets `Stmt.storeAt`); revert data a program lists (`Stmt.revertDatas`: literal data and data known in part, `Pat`, `DSet`) and `catchDataStep` (`Pat.fml`, the any-bytes goal). |
| `Calculus/Step.lean` | The strategy on scoped formulas: `Stmt.step` (one `casesOn`, every statement a rule; total, a `Premise`, decision 76), `Fml.maxIdx`/`fresh`, `Fml.stepR` by `Fml.rec` with its compiled twin `Fml.stepAt` (`stepR_eq`), `symex`. |
| `Calculus/JoinSequence.lean` | Intrinsic straight-write decoding/reconstruction and snapshot-guarded assignment programs (AU03; integrated dispatcher soundness in `Calculus/Sound/JoinSequence.lean`). |
| `Calculus/JoinSequenceSize.lean` | Exact emitted program size and linear bound, separately from walked leaf costs. |
| `Calculus/Sound/JoinSequence.lean` | Exact state/halt agreement after snapshot pop and soundness under both modalities. |
| `Examples/Tactics/JoinSigned.lean` | Producer-certified negative literal and dependent signed intervals, forced sequential/conjunctive closure, scoped countermodels, unchanged 64-case cap and stale-target rejection. |
| `Examples/Tactics/JoinSequence.lean` | Inhabited changed-guard, dependent-write, untaken/selected failure, refusal and emitted-size controls. |
| `Examples/Tactics/JoinWalk.lean` | Production automatic source proofs and separate walk/leaf measurements for dependent assignment sequences and long two-base joins. |
| `Examples/Tactics/JoinIntervals.lean` | Kernel interval certificates, fixed Boolean-case cap, current-context/stale-bound and inhabited countermodel controls. |
| `Calculus/TermSize.lean` | The size of what a rule emits: nodes of updates and formulas (`Upd.length`, `Upd.tmSize`, `Fml.tmSize`; of terms and statements, `Tm.size` and `Stmt.size`, in `Calculus/SyntaxSize.lean`, no type counted); what a premise writes (`Premise.upds`, `Premise.fmls`, `Premise.Bounded`); lowering at most triples the nodes (`Val.lower_size`, …); one lemma per arm, and `Stmt.step_upds_le` (dispatch budget: two, or tuple arity plus its condition capture), `Stmt.step_upds_le_two` (the retained non-lexicographic bound), `Stmt.step_tmSize_le` (every emitted term at most `16 * s.size + 16`). |
| `Calculus/Lands.lean` | Terms and updates read in a typed state, and where an update lands, nothing erased: `Tm.denote` (a term in the Theory, the symbols' `OpN.denote`) and the term bridge `Tm.denote_eval`; `Rhs.eval` (`LTy.VDen`: a memory local's identity bare, `State.getV`); `Held`, `Subst`, `Upd.held`; `Upd.globT` (the typed global part `GlobT`, its `Grown` heap carried), `Held.OK`, `Upd.Lands` (`.prefix`), `Upd.afterT` (`holds`' update clause), `afterT_imp`/`_and`/`_intro`/`.of_lands`/`.of_none`, `Upd.afterT_of_lands`, `Upd.lands_of`; the locals as values (`LocsV`, `State.locV`), an update computed (`Upd.applyV`, `Upd.applyV_ok`, `Upd.applyV_of_globT`, `Upd.lands_iff_applyV`, `Upd.afterT_applyV`); landing by constructor (`Upd.lands_nil`, `lands_pop_nil`, `lands_drop_nil`, `lands_decl`, `lands_snoc_local`) and the typed state's reads by position (`State.getV_*`, `State.locV_*`, `State.glob_*`). |
| `Calculus/TermWk.lean` | A term read with more locals around it (`Tm.wk`, `Tm.eval_wk`, `Tm.denote_wk`), and a lowered value weakened as its term (`Val.lower_wk`, `Val.lower_denote_up`). |
| `Calculus/TermEval.lean` | A term read on the typed state (`Tm.eval`, `Tm.rec` with an `evalM` twin); the laws on terms `Tm.findOnSave`, `Tm.findOnSaveFrameRoot`, `Tm.findOnSaveIn`; literals (`Tm.aliceAgeRead`, `Tm.aliceSelectRead`) read by the kernel. |
| `Calculus/Logic.lean` | What a scoped formula means: `holds`/`Fml.Valid` over the typed state (`State`) and the typed run (`Prog.runFrames`, `Modality.afterRunTR`): an equation in the Theory (`Tm.denote`, `holds_eq`; `holds_eq_of_eval` where both sides return), `wt(s)` by the storage's test (`holds_wellFormed`), an update by where it lands (`Upd.afterT`); typed binders (`LTy.tdenote_nonempty`); `State.anon`. |
| `Calculus/Correct.lean` | The minimal `⊨`: a correctness statement (`Spec`: equations, `wt`, connectives, typed `∀`, frameless `[P]`/`⟨P⟩`) read by `Prog.run` alone (`Spec.sat`, `Spec.Valid`); `Spec.toFml` the formula the calculus proves. |
| `Calculus/Lifetime.lean` | Successful-outcome bridges from `Spec` and internal formula boxes to lifetime invariant preservation; `Lifetime.Invariant.ofSpecs` composes function correctness into whole-contract safety. |
| `Calculus/ContractWorld.lean` | Spec/formula boxes at actual successful paid world-frame outcomes; no success inferred from exhaustion. |
| `Calculus/CorrectCallback.lean` | Direct correctness with callbacks (`Spec.satC`, `Spec.ValidC`, `⊨[I]`); exact translation and agreement without callback sites. |
| `Calculus/SoundKit.lean` | What a premise asks of its statement's typed run (`Premise.Runs`: an update's end as `Upd.applyP`, a block's run up to the halt, a hidden binder's value, a split's branch with and without callbacks; any other kind its formula reading), which gives `Premise.Sound` (`Premise.Runs.sound`); the premise shapes (`Premise.sound_update` … `sound_done`) on the states the typed runs end in (`Stmt.runSt`, `Prog.runSt`, `Frames.runSt`); the per-arm statement (`Stmt.StepSound`, arms named `…_sound`), a push never diverging (`TRun.push_nd`), runs alike up to the halt (`TRes.Sim`, `sound_unfoldSim`, `sound_captureGen`), an update against its statement's typed step (`sound_updateSim_agrees`, `sound_update_typed`, `sound_updateSim_typed`, `sound_cases_typed`: the step agrees, `TRes.AgreesO`, with what the update computes, `Upd.applyP`; `Upd.lands_of_applyP`, `Upd.globT_none_of_applyV`), the typed shift lemmas (`Val.evalT_up` and kin). |
| `Calculus/WkOver.lean` | The syntax the runs' weakening and frame lemmas read, apart from any run: a scope's end on both sides (`Thin.Over`, `Stmt.wk_over`, `Prog.wk_over`), a loop body's frame as its mutability, its modifies set and the locals it writes (`Prog.frame_some`, `Prog.frame_within`). |
| `Calculus/SoundSummary.lean` | A call's summary is its run: a symbolic frame agrees with a typed one (`SEnv.Ev`) and what it owes returns (`SEnv.D`); terms read through it (`Tm.eval_substE`, `Tm.strictReads_eval`); every statement's symbolic run is sound for its typed run (`Stmt.sym_sound`, `Prog.sym_sound`, `SymRuns`, `SymMono`); the callee's summary (`CallRet.sumOf_sound`), the call (`CallRet.sym_call`), the update against the run (`CallRet.summary_agrees`) and the arm (`Premise.sound_callSummary`). |
| `Calculus/SoundCalls.lean` | The rules of calls and `try` (`Stmt.sound_call`, `Stmt.sound_tryCall`): the inlined body run as the body in its frame (`Stmt.run_block_wk`, from `Prog.run_wk`), the parameters and returns declared (`Args.runSt_declsThen`, `Binders.runSt_declsThen`), a captured argument (`Args.evalT_capture`), each `try` clause for every typed value its outcome binds. |
| `Calculus/SoundCatch.lean` | `catchDataBox` sound (`Premise.sound_catchDataBox`, `Stmt.sound_catchData`): a self-call's datum is one its body lists (literal, or matching a `Pat`, `Pat.fml_holds`), the clause run after its allocation; any bytes past `require`s (`catch_alloc_sound`, from `TRun.litBytesT_alloc`). |
| `Calculus/DataIn.lean` | The data a run reverts with is one the program lists (`TRes.DataS` over `DSet.Mem`, `Pat.Matches`, `Stmt.run_dataS`/`Prog.run_dataS`, `Prog.asserts_of_revertDatas`), step by step as `Semantics/NoPanic.lean`. |
| `Calculus/SoundLoops.lean` | The rules of loops (`Stmt.sound_loop`): one turn (`Stmt.runSt_loop_unfold`) for `whileUnwind`/`loopExit`, the invariant rules by induction on the heads (`TLoop.run_induct`) and a variant (`TLoop.run_variant`; under the diamond `TLoop.run_variant_or`, the body exhausted), the body's frame anonymised (`holds_anonHavoc`), a condition read by its update (`{ b := cond }`, landing where the typed value returns: `Val.lower_eval_ok`/`Val.lower_eval_error`), an invariant and a variant in the Theory (`holds_isBool_lower`, under the variant's local `Val.lower_denote_up`). |
| `Calculus/SoundArms.lean` | The update arms: each statement's typed step against what its update computes (`*_agrees`, `TRes.AgreesO` against `Upd.applyP`, following the run's binds, `TRes.AgreesO.cases`), an element computed (`applyP_storage1`, `applyP_memory1`, `applyP_local1`, `applyP_snoc`) and a symbol read (`Tm.eval_save`, `Tm.eval_compound`, `Tm.eval_bump`, …) on the typed operations the run calls, nothing erased; carried to the typed run (`sound_setLocal` … `sound_revert`, `Premise.sound_update_typed`), none with a condition. |
| `Calculus/SoundCapture.lean` | Captures and holes: a hole filled is the read, then the hole's typed continuation (`SHole.contT`, `MHole.contT`, `VHole.contT`), shifted under a local it runs lifted (`*.fill_pushT`, `runSt_pushed`); the read steps and the value holes (`SHole.readStep_sound`, `MHole.readStep_sound`, `VHole.step_sound`, `ternaryStep_sound`); the typed push lemmas (`TRun.*_pushT`, `OpLoc.AgreeT`, `pushedT`). |
| `Calculus/SoundLocal.lean` | The arms on locals (`localStep_sound` with the short circuits, `rebindStep_sound`, `opStep_sound`, `incStep_sound`, `OpLoc.unfoldRecv_sound`; `binopUnfoldLeft`, `unopCapture`). |
| `Calculus/SoundControl.lean` | Payments, control flow, declarations and blocks (`transferStep_sound`, `sendStep_sound`, `iteStep_sound` by `Prog.run_wk_up`, `requireStep_sound`, `assertStep_sound`, `holds_isBool`, the `…DeclInitDrop` binders, `sound_block`); `memoryLocalDeclInitDrop` from an unset local (decision 17'). |
| `Calculus/SoundStore.lean` | The storage arms (`assignStep_sound`, `copyStep_sound`, `assignFromMemStep_sound`, `deleteStep_sound`, `pushStep_sound`, `popStep_sound`), blocks of captures by `Prog.runSt_declLocal_cons` and kin. |
| `Calculus/SoundMem.lean` | The memory arms (`rebindMemStep_sound`, `memRefStep_sound`, `assignMemStep_sound`, `deleteMemStep_sound`, `sound_assignNew`). |
| `Calculus/SoundStep.lean` | `Stmt.stepSound_arms`: every arm of `Stmt.step` but the blocks' (`Stmt.isBlockArm`) sound. |
| `Calculus/FirstOrder.lean` | `Fml.FO`, a formula with no update, hidden binder or modality; `Idx.nameR` and `Thin.namesR`, a position's and a thinning's names by the recursor (`Thin.namesR_eq`), which the reduction's state reads. |
| `Calculus/Quote.lean` | Scoped formulas, terms, updates, frames and premises quoted back into `Expr` (`Fml.quote`, `Tm.quote`, `Upd.quote`, `Frames.quote`, `Premise.quote`), hash-consed; a typed symbol with its implicit types and its proofs (`Eq.refl`, an `IndexTy`/`ArrTy` constructor), a literal as `PrimTy.ofLit` (`qPrimVal`); an explicit `∀`'s mark (`Fml.markExplicit`, `Fml.isExplicitAll`, an `Expr.mdata`, decision 28); a block alone is `Syntax/Quote.lean`'s. |
| `Calculus/StmtSound.lean` | `Stmt.sound`: every rule of `Stmt.step` sound over the typed state, at every contract (decision 17'), the arms and the blocks assembled; `Stmt.runs`.  Below the sequent's rules, so it needs none of the laws. |
| `Calculus/Sound.lean` | `Proves.sound`, `Proves.fmlValid`: the scoped sequent sound, with no hypothesis (axioms: Lean's three), from `Stmt.sound` and `Proves.sound_of`. |
| `Calculus/Rules.lean` | The rule table: `Taclet` and `LeanTaclet`, one `dl{ … }` line a constructor, read under `open scoped Solidity.Schema` into scoped taclets (`RuleSyntax.schemaTacletCore`); `blockEmpty` and `functionCallArgCapture` stated by hand (`scripts/check-rule-text.sh` checks the rest are lines); the term symbols the lines write (`Tms`), the side conditions (`Loc.isTarget`, the holes' `isTarget`, …), `Prog.up`, a call's inlining `Premise.expandCall` and argument capture `Premise.argCapture`; `Rule`. |
| `Calculus/Taclet.lean` | Each scoped taclet is the rule `Stmt.step` fires (`Taclet.toStep`, alias `step_eq`; `LeanTaclet.toStep`, `Rule.toStep`, `Rule.premise_unique`): one lemma per constructor (`Taclet.<rule>.toStep`), most replayed by `#replay_toStep` (`to_step_replay`, the plans `#derive_toStep?` found with `to_step_tac`), the rest by hand. |
| `Calculus/Completeness.lean` | The rule a scoped statement fires, by name (`ruleOf?`, `#rule_of m s`): the constructor whose line matches (a hole's statement by the hole and part read off it, `holeCandidates`) and whose side conditions evaluate to true, its premise checked to be `Stmt.step`'s; for `#step`, `sol_derive?` and the proof tree. |
| `Calculus/RuleExists.lean` | Every scoped statement has a rule and the strategy fires only rules (`Stmt.rule_exists`, `Rule.ofStep`, `Rule.iff_step`; the converse of `Rule.toStep`): one lemma per `Stmt` constructor (`Stmt.<c>.ruleExists`), `Stmt.step` split where it is blocked (`blockerOf`), each leaf closed by its rule; searched once by `#derive_ruleExists?`, replayed from the `#replay_ruleExists` table (no search). |
| `Calculus/SolkeyFragment.lean` | `Stmt.inSolkey m` on scoped statements; on the fragment the fired rule is solkey's (`Stmt.step_taclet`, `Stmt.solkey_rule_exists`, `LeanTaclet.not_inSolkey`); a zero-bound loop uses Lean's exit rule (`Taclet.not_unwindZero`, `Stmt.solkey_lt_calculus`); a step of `⊢ₖ` fires a `Taclet` (`Stmt.taclet_of_admits`), and solkey's rules keep the fragment (`Fml.inSolkey`, `Taclet.premise_inSolkey`). |
| `Calculus/SolkeyProves.lean` | solkey's rules and the judgement (decision 36): `⊢ₖ` is `⊢` on the fragment (`Proves.toAll`, `Proves.toSolkey`, `Proves.solkey_iff`), and less off it (`Proves.solkey_blocked`, `Proves.solkey_lt_calculus`: `[ while (false) { } ] true`). |
| `Calculus/DelabLean.lean` | The printer of `LeanTaclet`: `dl[LeanTaclet C k]{ … }` as the rule lines print, by `Calculus/PrintStmt.lean`'s printer (`tacletLine?`; Lean-only loop fallbacks; `functionCallArgCapture`'s calls as terms, `‹Stmt.call …›`). |
| `Calculus/DelabErase.lean` | The printers' name stack, read off an `Expr` with its schema variables free: the names a statement, a call or a frame end leaves (`stmtOutNames`, `thinNames`, `extDepth`), parts under `up`s (`isUp`, `stripUps`, `wkUp?`), `lowerFns`, `blockEndName`, `isScopedFamily` (for `#check_rule_text`); over `Rules` and `RuleKit` only, below the delaborators (decision 53). |
| `Calculus/PrintTerm.lean` | The printers of scoped terms and updates read from their `Expr` at a name stack: `viewTmS` (a term as a local's name, a schema variable or a symbol `Op2.find` on its terms, `up`s and lowerings read through), `ppTermS` … `ppMValS` in `Calculus/RuleSyntax.lean`'s term grammar (also `tm{ … }`'s printer, `Calculus/TermLib.lean`), `pp.sol.tmField`, `cmpPartsS`, `eqTmS` (the same text), `updPiecesS`/`ppUpdS`/`updElemS` (pops and drops print as nothing); `idxName`. |
| `Calculus/PrintStmt.lean` | The printer of the scoped syntax read from its `Expr`, schema variables and all: `ppStmtS`, `ppProgS`, `ppBlockS`, `ppExprS`, `ppPremiseS`, `ppFmlS`, in `Calculus/RuleSyntax.lean`'s grammar, by its builders (terms and updates by `Calculus/PrintTerm.lean`); the name-stack walker (`idxName`, `SPiece`, `progPiecesS`, `framePiecesS`: positions as their binders' names, a schema variable its own, block ends as `}`), the API a reader of the scoped syntax uses; `withPrinter`; the `where` clause's sources (`Wheres`). |
| `Calculus/DelabRules.lean` | Scoped taclets, premises and formulas printed from their `Expr`, schema variables and all, by `Calculus/PrintStmt.lean`'s printer (`blockEmpty` as `{body;}` ⇝ `body;`, a call without targets `ic`); `}` at a block's end (`core_item`, printing only); the delaborators for `Taclet`, `Premise`, `Fml` (calls and `try` as the statements they come from; a frame end by `Thin` as nothing) and a taclet's side conditions (`delabTacletSide`); a formula's `where` clause for a memory local a copy out of storage binds under a hidden binder (`ppFmlE`).  Below the judgement.
| `Calculus/Delab.lean` | The delaborator for `Proves`, a sequent `dl{ ..H ⟹ φ }` (`hypsPartsS`), over `Calculus/DelabRules.lean`. |
| `Calculus/Graft.lean` | Two updates as one (`Upd.seqPar`, KeY's `sequentialToParallel`): `V` read in `U`'s state (`Upd.graft`, by `Calculus/Lands.lean`'s `Upd.held`), `storage` the storage `U` last wrote (`Upd.lastSt`, `Upd.lastSt_eval`), sound as `holds_upd_seqPar`/`holds_upd_graft` over `Upd.Lands` (`Tm.eval_substH`, `Upd.globT_graft`, `Upd.held_graft`); two readings over two heap typings (`Srt.SameO`, `Srt.forget`: a memory by its grown heap), the symbols free of the global part (`Op0.globFree` …, `Op1.eval_same` …); an update moved under hidden binders (`Upd.lift?`, `Upd.liftBinds?`, `holds_liftBinds`). |
| `Calculus/SeqRewrite.lean` | A term taclet (`TermTaclet`) or Theory equation (`Tm.Theq`) of closed terms of one type at the empty scope on a scoped formula: a closed term read there (`Tm.toNil?`, `Tm.closedAs`, `Tm.ofNil`, `State.toNil` the global part, `Tm.eval_toNilS`, `Tm.denote_toNilS`), in every equation of that type (`Tm.sideC`, `Fml.rwEqC`: through connectives, updates, quantifiers, hidden binders and an open modality's postcondition, sound as `Fml.rwEqC_holds`; the scoped `Proves.rewrite`'s, decision 90) and in an update's right-hand sides (`Upd.rwC`, `holds_upd_rwC`, `Tm.EvalRefines` over `OLe`; `Upd.rwBy_lands`, `Upd.rwBy_globT`); the closed terms of a formula found (`Tm.closedNilVals`). |
| `Calculus/Merge.lean` | `sequentialToParallel` on a scoped line while the program is open (`Fml.mergeRun`, the chains' `~[sequentialToParallel]~>`): the update spine's merges, through quantifiers and hidden binders; sound as `Fml.mergeRun_leads`, by `Calculus/Graft.lean`'s lemmas. |
| `Calculus/RewriteScoped.lean` | Rewrites of a scoped line, most while the program is open (`FmlRw`, the chains' `~[n]~>` there, `Fml.RwBy`): a literal law in an update's right-hand sides (`FmlRw.lit`, any modality: exact, `holds_upd_rwC_iff`), a term taclet in a box update's (`FmlRw.lawUpd`), `applyOnRigid` along the skeleton of the formula under an update that cannot halt (`Upd.totalS`; storage writes at a root or a member, `Tm.totalW`, `Storage.write_fieldsB`) or under the box (`FmlRw.applyOnRigid`, `Fml.pushS`; through the scope ends under it, `FmlRw.applyOnRigidPops`), a rewrite under a line's premises (`FmlRw.inPath`), `concrete` (`FmlRw.concrete`), `applyStorageBox` (`FmlRw.applyStorageBox`), the laws in the equations, `applyOnPV`, `simplifyUpdate`, the merges under a branch and over a state write; where on the line (`Fml.belowS`, `Fml.atUpd`) and the skeleton the elaborator reads (`Fml.skelS`, `Fml.spineSkels`). |
| `Calculus/RewritePast.lean` | Rewrites of a scoped line past the program: `sequentialToParallel` across hidden binders (`FmlRw.mergePast`: a binder absorbed into the update that sets it, `Upd.absorb`, else the update moved under it, else every binder absorbed, `Fml.absorbAllMergeP`, which declares a late-set local first), a shadowed state write dropped (`Upd.dropSh`); each over `Upd.Lands` (`State.ThinOf`, `Upd.absorb_globT`/`_held`, `Upd.dropSh_rel`, `Upd.globT_thinIO`/`held_thinIO`); `simplifyUpdate` dropping the locals nothing reads with their writes and binders (`FmlRw.simplifyPast`, `Upd.thinIO`, `Fml.thinS?`); `applyStorageBox` on a lone storage write (`FmlRw.applyStorageBoxP`: `Fml.substSt` by the storage merge's `Tm.substP`, equal by `Tm.substP_denote` over the symbols' congruences `Op0.denote_congrP` … `Op2.denote_congrP`).  Split by topic into the pieces below (`docs/parallelism.md`), this module the umbrella. |
| `Calculus/RewritePast/Scope.lean` | Fewer locals, out of the top local's scope, a hidden binder absorbed. |
| `Calculus/RewritePast/Merge.lean` | A shadowed state write dropped, two updates merged. |
| `Calculus/RewritePast/Apply.lean` | A storage write applied to a first-order line, out of a thinning's scope, `simplifyUpdate` past the program. |
| `Calculus/RewriteLaws.lean` | A law in an update's right-hand sides past the program, under any modality: a term taclet (`TermTaclet`), a memory law (`EvalLaw`) or a literal law at the update's scope (`LawPair`, `LawPair.pick`), what it covers (`LawCov`, `Calculus/TermLaws.lean`), `holds_upd_rwG`, `FmlRw.lawUpdS`, `Fml.spineScopes` |
| `Calculus/ExpandLaws.lean` | A whole value's write as its words past the program, Lean only (`docs/automation.md`, M2): `Tm.expand?` (`delAt`, a copy `save(s, p, find(s, q))` at a type `Ty.expandable` accepts, a push and a pop of a word), exact (`Tm.eval_expand?`) and out of gas where the term is (`Tm.exhB_expandK?`), `holds_upd_expand_iff`, `FmlRw.expandPast` (the links `delAtExpand`, `copyExpand`, `pushExpand`, `popExpand`), at most `expandBound` more `save`s (`Tm.spineSaves_expand?`). |
| `Calculus/TermLaws.lean` | The laws past the program, scoped and typed: the literal laws (`LitLaw`, KeY's `*_literals`, side conditions by `BinOp.evalT`), `Tm.EvalRefinesAt`; the typed frame laws (`Storage.find_save_frame`, `delete`/`push`/`pop` as saves, `len_save_frame`) and `TermTaclet.RefLaw` at the eval level, covered by `Upd.coversRef`; the member-wise reads of a write (`SRef.underAt`, `Tm.unshift`, `Tm.base`, `Tm.base?`, `Tm.sameBase`) and `Upd.coversLit`; the memory laws (`EvalLaw`: `readOnWrite`, `readOnWriteFrame[Identity]`, `readOnWriteIdentity` on the typed heap's get/set; `readOnAddM`, `readOnAddMFrame[Identity]` on the typed heap an allocation keeps (`Heap.Keeps`) and its defaults (`Heap.Dflt`, `Srt.FreshRead`, shared with `Calculus/MemRead.lean`), `Tm.freshPath?`, `Tm.boundedIn`; `findOnCopy[In]`, `readFromCopyToStorage` on the typed copies, `SVal.find_overlay_field`), exact past their anchor (`LawAnchor`, `Tm.lawAnchor?`), covered by `Upd.coversEval`; and `LawCov`, the evidence (its `back` where the update's global part returns) `FmlRw.lawUpdS` asks: `lit`, `findOnSave`, `findMemberCons`, `covered`, `ref`, `eqv`, `eval`.
