A read by members below a delete the update holds is covered (`Tm.findDelBelowAt`, `Upd.coversLit`); `Tm.KindFreeAt.ofNil` at any storage sort.  Split by topic into the pieces below (`docs/parallelism.md`), this module the umbrella. |
| `Calculus/TermLaws/Storage.lean` | Refinement, the literal laws, update elements, the storage's frame and delete laws in a run. |
| `Calculus/TermLaws/Reads.lean` | Reads member-wise, below a delete, of a word written; the memory read laws. |
| `Calculus/TermLaws/Fresh.lean` | Fresh allocations, copies between storage and memory, the laws bundled, the pins. |
| `Calculus/StateFacts.lean` | The shapes of the paths a law's side condition reads (`SegT`), and a member type's prefix (`Ty.memberTy_snoc_some`); the typed symbols monotone in their arguments (`OLe`, `Op1.eval_le` …). |
| `Calculus/ChainKit.lean` | The relations of a chain on scoped formulas: `φ ~> ψ` (`Fml.OneStep`), `φ ~[r]~> ψ` (`Fml.StepBy`, labelled by a `Rule` derivation on the statement the step fires on, `Focus`), `~*>` (`Fml.Steps`), a chain (`Fml.Via`, a link a scoped merge too, `Link.merge`, or a scoped rewrite, `Link.rw`, past the program too); the strategy's step taken apart along a line's connectives (`stepFocusR_*`, `maxIdx_*`), and each relation sound (`Fml.Leads`, `….valid`); `~~>` past the program to the line written (`Fml.LeadsK`: some line the chain reaches whose erasure is the line written read as its erasure; `Via.leadsK`, `Leads.leadsK`, `LeadsK.valid`), segments (`Leads.via`), `calc` (`Trans` instances, `SoundRel`), `StepBy.unique` (the rule and the line; `StepRule.eq_of_focus`), `Steps.eq_of_length`, `Steps.eq_of_measure`, `Steps.ofSymex`. |
| `Calculus/ChainRules.lean` | Every step has its rule (`Fml.OneStep.stepBy`, by `Stmt.rule_exists`; `Focus.rule`). |
| `Calculus/Chains.lean` | Chains over `cdl!{ … }`: the strategy run as compiled code (`Fml.chainLines`, postconditions in slots), each written line matched against the computed one by its key (`Fml.lineKey`: terms and updates as text in names, `Tm.keyStr` (`Calculus/NamedTerm.lean`'s), `Upd.keyStrs`, a modality's program as its text, `Prog.keyStrs`; the text read open, `elabDlOpen`; `matchWritten`; a block's `}` read as written), each step proved by the kernel (`rfl`, `Modality.casesOn` for a variable modality), the rewrite links on the scoped line, while the program is open (`~[sequentialToParallel]~>` by `Calculus/Merge.lean`, the rewrites of `Calculus/RewriteScoped.lean`) and past it (`Calculus/RewritePast.lean`, `Calculus/RewriteLaws.lean`: `lawUpdCands`; read through the premises a line begins with, `premisePath`), every arrow's computed at once (`scopedRwCandsMany`), the strategy going on after them while the program is open; a rewrite past the program that gives no line written says why (`pastRefusal`); `A ~~> B` read by the first line's kind (past the program `Fml.LeadsK`, the written line read by name, `elabKeyQ`, `writtenFml`; `leads%`; while the program is open up to `openRwDepth` merges and rewrites past a line, `openRwPath`, a bounded breadth-first search), a `calc` step's line computed when its step is proved (`pendingLines`); `sol_chain` and `#derivation` dispatch on the scoped head, and the delaborators print a chain's statement.  Split by topic into the pieces below (`docs/parallelism.md`), this module the umbrella. |
| `Calculus/Chains/Strategy.lean` | The strategy as compiled code, a line's key, printing a line, running the strategy (its caches). |
| `Calculus/Chains/Proofs.lean` | The steps' proofs, naming the rule, written lines, past the program. |
| `Calculus/Chains/Elab.lean` | The chain elaborated, `φ ~~> ψ`, `sol_chain`, printing. |
| `Calculus/ChainGen.lean` | `#chain φ` and `#chain_rest c` for a scoped `φ`: the strategy's links grouped and named by the rule fired, then the rewrites of the scoped line to a last line (`pastSteps`, `pastSearch`: depth first, each arrow at the first place it applies, the smallest last line), each line in the first wrapper (`cdl![m]{}`, `cdl!{}`, `cdl![.box]{}`, `cdl![.diamond]{}`) that reads back as it; `sol_chain?` and `sol_rws`/`sol_rws?` on `A ~~> B` (`sol_chain?` writes the rewrites `openRwPath` finds while the program is open as links, `openRwFrom`), `sol_chain` on a scoped one (past the program it refuses to search, and names `sol_rws`); past the program `sol_rws` replays the named rewrites on the scoped line (`pastLeads`). |

| `Calculus/Proves.lean` | The scoped sequent: `Hyps C Γ₀ Γ` (a telescope: updates, `∀` and the hidden binder extend the scope), `Hyps.wrap`/`fresh`/`boxOnly`/`anonHavoc`, and `Proves` with one rule per premise kind (`update`, `unfold`, `bind`, `split` with KeY's two goals, `check`, `done`, `branches`, `cases`, `inv`, `inline`), each firing `Stmt.step`'s rule where the rule set admits it (`hR : R.admits m s = true`, default `rule_admits`; `RuleSet.admits .solkey` is `Stmt.keyStep`, false exactly where a `LeanTaclet` fires: `LeanTaclet.keyStep`); `Stmt.stepIn R`, the walk's step (`step_of_stepIn`, `admits_of_stepIn`, `stepIn_of_step`); and `intro`, `allIntro`, `updIntro`, `bindIntro`, `empty`, `closeTrue`, `closeFalse`, `close` (by the erasure); the rules on the context's updates, `merge` (`sequentialToParallel`, `mergeStorage`, past hidden binders: `Hyps.binds`) and `updRw`; `rewrite` (a Theory equation in every equation of the sequent, a modality open or not: `Hyps.rwEqC`, `Fml.rwEqC`, decision 90); and `closeHolds` (no modality left, `Fml.modalFree`). |

| `Calculus/UpdRuleScoped.lean` | KeY's update simplification on `Fml C Γ` (M6c 6c), one way over the typed state (`UpdRuleS.sound`: what a rule rewrites to gives what it rewrote): `sequentialToParallel` (`Upd.seqPar`), `simplifyUpdate` (`Upd.simpS`: `Upd.simp` under the box, the shadowed writes under either modality, `Upd.shadow`), `applySkip` (a formula with no modality, `holds_congr_reads` over `State.ReadsAs`), `applyOnRigid` (`Fml.substU`; under the diamond for `Upd.totalB`, aliases to a root's fields included), and, Lean only, `mergeHidden` (`Fml.mergeB`, through hidden binders), `absorbPops`, `trans`; at the positive positions only (`Fml.rwAtS`, `Fml.updAtS`, `Fml.simpUpdsS`, `Fml.Valid.of_simpUpdsS`), the walks by recursor with `match` twins (`Fml.rwAtSM`, …); `holds_upd_mono`; each rule over `Upd.Lands` (`Upd.shadow_globT`/`_held`, `Upd.totalB_globT`). |

| `Calculus/RewriteSyntax.lean` | The closer's steps after the program, spelled the same on a scoped goal (`open scoped Solidity.Scoped`): `refine merge rfl ?_`/`Proves.mergeStorage` (`core_merge`: scope ends taken in, one merge past the hidden binders), `refine simplify ?_`, `eqRefl`, `eqClose`, `sol_apply_upd`, `rw`/`sol_rw` (`core_rw`: a scoped term taclet or the Theory's laws on closed terms read at the empty scope, `theoryRewriteS`, its terms reified by their typed readings `Tm.denote_*S`); results computed and quoted back (`Upd.quote`, `Fml.quote`). |

A `uint` checked or paired against an `int` is widened (`NTm.widen`, `resolvePair`). |

| `Calculus/RunFrameMem.lean` | A body that allocates runs within a grown frame (`Stmt.withinM`, `TGFrameM`: the heap typing extended, the caller's objects kept, `Heap.Keeps`). |
| `Calculus/RunFrameOwn.lean` | A body that writes only into its own objects (`Stmt.withinW`, the invariant `Own b`): the caller's objects kept. |
| `Calculus/CallbackFrameMem.lean` | `ExecS.frame` for a body that allocates: a callback run grows the heap keeping every older object. |
| `Calculus/MemContract.lean` | A memory return by contract for a callee that allocates and writes only its own objects: its "post" reads the call where it ends. |
| `Calculus/Elementary.lean` | Individual capture and raw update laws under either modality, used by `Tools/ProgramTrace.lean`. |
| `Calculus/Symex.lean` | `sol_step`/`sol_symex` on a scoped goal `Fml.Valid φ`: one step sound (`Fml.stepR_sound`, from `Stmt.sound`, the rules' soundness), `symex_sound`, `symex_valid`; `Symex.normValid` (the compiled strategy quoted back), `evaluable` (an obligation with no code read by its value). |
| `Calculus/Derive.lean` | `Derive.fuel`, the steps `sol_derive`'s walk is given (`sol_prove`'s walk is bounded by `Seq.fuel`, `Derive.budget`); `sol_derive`/`sol_derive?` on `Proves`, one `apply` per rule cited by its taclet (`Taclet.step_eq .rule`, `ruleOf?`), a precondition, a quantifier and an empty modality by solkey's names (`impRight`, `allRight`, `emptyModality`); `sol_derive?` closes a leaf at the empty outer scope (`Walk.closeLeaf`), and under `open scoped Solidity.Scoped` writes the short walk (`Walk.surfaceOn`, `shortTac`: `apply Proves.fmlValid`, `apply impRight`, `apply update .r`, `sol_leaf; sol_close` at a leaf, the silent steps unwritten); `Derive.searchLeaf` (`Derive.leafTacs`: `sol_leaf` and a scoped closer), `Derive.replayFits`, `Derive.replayLines`.  `sol_close` on a scoped leaf is `Calculus/CloseScoped.lean`'s |

| `Calculus/AllLeft.lean` | KeY's `allLeftHide`: a `∀` premise of a leaf's context replaced by its instances at literals (`Hyps.instAll`, `Hyps.instAll_valid`; an instance is the body under `{x := v}`, `Fml.instLit`, by `Fml.substU`); `sol_leaf` offers the literals the leaf indexes with (`Seq.allLeftLits`). |

A 512-node cap on each input condition/update prevents exponential substitution. |
| `Calculus/ProvesDerive.lean` | `sol_prove` on a scoped sequent `H ⊢ φ` (`Proves C R [] H φ`, `⊢ φ` under the flip), and on `Fml.Valid φ` by `Proves.fmlValid` then the same walk (`evalSolProveValid`, decision 89 extended): the walk of `Proves`' rules (`Seq.run`, generic in the leaf; `Seq.runK` carries the context's largest index, `Hyps.maxIdxH`, for the fresh index `Seq.freshK`, `Hyps.fresh_eq_freshK`) as one kernel evaluation (`Seq.residue R H φ = some ls`, `Proves.of_residue`; the walk steps with `Stmt.stepIn R`, so under `⊢ₖ` it fires solkey's rules only, and `#solkey_scan` walks `.all`, `Seq.scanLeaves`, `Hyps.storageWrites`); a split's branch whose ground condition the closer refutes closes by `closeFalse` (`Hyps.groundS`, `Seq.refuteLanded`, preserving the live sibling); a leaf goes to the scoped closer (`Seq.leafCheckS`, `Calculus/LeafDerive.lean`); the parameters are counted by `Seq.paramCount` (the context's outermost `∀`s and hidden binders, then the formula's, up to an explicit `∀`, decision 28), the count `np` marking a leaf left (`Seq.residueQuoteWithin`); one goal `leafᵢ` per leaf the closers leave, the walk's own scoped sequent (`Seq.SLeaf`, `Seq.leafS`, quoted by `Seq.SLeaf.quote`/`Hyps.quote`, decision 89); `sol_leaf` (`sol_leaf_wt`) leaves it `⊨ᶠ ψ`, the sequent wrapped (`Proves.closeValid`, `Seq.leafValid`), its `∀` premises instantiated first (`allLeftHide`, `Calculus/AllLeft.lean`) `Seq.goalFits`, the closer's bounds on a leaf (`Seq.leafFitsS`), for `sol_prove?`. |
| `Calculus/ProveChunked.lean` | `sol_prove (chunk := n)`: the walk `n` steps per kernel check (`Seq.cutK`, `runK` with its spent step budget a leaf, sound by `Seq.cutK_sound`; `Seq.cutWithin`, `Proves.of_cutWithin`), each chunk and each leaf's `sol_leaf; sol_close` under a heartbeat budget of its own (`Seq.proveChunked`). |

| `Calculus/DecideEval/Memory.lean` | Reads of memory, a memory object read as the stored value it copies to, the elimination keeping what a term reads. |
| `Calculus/ElimWriteFree.lean` | The elimination leaves no storage operation (M3): `LTm.StoFree` (no subterm at a storage or stored-value sort, no `next`/checked `at`; the start read only by `obsW`/`obsL`/`obsH`), `LTm.Elimable`/`LFml.Elimable` (no memory sort, `mtSt`, `wt`, `cleanTail`, `select`, `copyMem`; `orElse`/`kite`/`err` only at a word; observed paths storage-free), free readers (`VRd.Free`, `SRd.Free`, `SFun.Free`, `ElimM.Free`) kept by every builder (`relSegs_free`, `writeAt_free`, `delRS_free`, `ovRS_free`, `pushRd_free`, `popRd_free`, …); `LTm.elim_stoFree`, `LFml.elim_stoFree`; save normal form (`LTm.SaveNF`, `LTm.SaveNF.find_stoFree`). |
| `Calculus/StepFragment.lean` | On a storage fragment a rule writes only save-like updates (M3, Lean only): `Tm.SaveLike` (`save`/`pushSlot`/`shrink` on `storage`, or what `Tm.expand?` turns into those), `Tm.SaveNF` (`LTm.SaveNF`'s shape on scoped terms), `Tm.SaveLike.saveNF_expand`; the fragment `Stmt.Frag`/`Prog.Frag` (memory-free `Val.MemFree`, kept by weakening, `Prog.frag_wk`); `Premise.SaveLike` (updates save-like, programs it runs in the fragment), one lemma per arm, `Stmt.step_saveLike`, `Stmt.step_saveNF`; storage aliases declared with an initializer. |
| `Calculus/SymexFragment.lean` | The step fragment lifted over the strategy (M3, Lean only): `Tm.ElimOk` (`LTm.Elimable`'s symbols on scoped terms), `SymS.Ok`/`SymS.OkNF`; pushed in, `Tm.ElimOk.toL` (elimable), `Tm.SaveNF.toL` (the bridge to `LTm.SaveNF`), `Upd.ElimOk.toLS`, `Upd.toLS_okNF` (chains of saves compose); `Val.lower_elimOk`, `Tm.expand?_elimOk`, `Premise.ElimOk` one lemma per arm, `Stmt.step_elimOk`, `Stmt.step_toL_saveNF`; `Fml.Frag`, `Fml.stepR_frag`, `symex_frag`, `Fml.Frag.toLS`, `symex_reduceS_stoFree`; every expansion at once (`Tm.pickExpandAll`, `holds_upd_expandAll_iff`, `Upd.rwBy_expandAll_nf`, `Upd.SaveLike.toLS_expandAll_okNF`). |
| `Calculus/ElimFacts.lean` | The elimination under its premises (`LFml.elimF`): the equations an eliminated premise states (`LFml.factsOf`) rewrite every word and storage reading built after it (`ElimM.rwF`, by `Facts.rw`), so the readers below compare what the premises say; sound by `LFml.elimF_holds`. |
| `Calculus/DecideScoped.lean` | The reduction on a scoped leaf: updates pushed in by position (`SymS`, `BEnv`, `Tm.toL`, `Upd.toLS`, `Fml.toLS`); ledger writes kept and a ledger read past them (`NetW`, `NetW.read`), the balance a payment leaves (`payBal`); bounded early storage literals and non-growing memory read/length/identity reductions (`LTm.literalRead`, `foldMem2`, `foldRead2`); checked indices lowered when their checks read true (`foldAt`, `foldNext`, `foldRead3`); push exhaustion (`Upd.exhL`, `guardM`); `Fml.reduceS`, then `LFml.elim`; recursors and compiled match twins. |
| `Calculus/ReadBounds.lean` | Shared input limits for demanded read elimination and the closer: 24,000 nodes and depth 12, or 96,000 nodes and depth 40 where every storage history is concrete (`LTm.known?`: updates at literal array paths below a deleted root, values not read from memory, reads below known roots, no `pop`/`shrink`/`extend`); over an unknown history at most `exposeBound` (5) exposing updates for early reads and refutations (`LTm.exposeDepth`, `LFml.exposeDepth`); the `LTm`/`LFml` tree counters (`fits`, `stDepth`) and compiled-link proofs. |
| `Calculus/DecideTyped.lean` | The scoped reduction sound over the typed state: the reduction read in the leaf's starting state, the leaf in the state its updates reach, related by `Agrees`; a term pushed in reads what it reads (`Tm.toL_drel`, a memory up to the heap it grew from, `Srt.DRel`), an update lands where it does (`Upd.toLS_sound`, `Upd.exhL_sound`; a ledger read past the writes, `NetW.read_eval`, `NetW.read_op`/`read_pay`/`read_mt`, `payBal_eval`), a formula at its polarity (`Fml.toLS_sound`); `Fml.Valid.of_toLS`, `Fml.Valid.of_reduceS`. |
| `Calculus/DecideCleanTail.lean` | The closer under clean tails (O6, Lean only): a leaf that assumes `cleanTail(storage) = true` (`Fml.assumesCT`, vacuous elsewhere: `Fml.assumesCT_holds`) has each starting-storage read past a live end, along no mapping key, read as the default (`LTm.ctGuards`, `lenOf`, `guardWith`, `LTm.ctFold`, `LTm.ctFold_eval`), then closed (`LFml.decideMemCT`). |
| `Calculus/DecideComplete.lean` | `sol_decide` on a scoped leaf: the reduction (`Fml.toLS`), the elimination under premises and the memory reader and closer (`LFml.decideMem`), within the closer's bounds (`Seq.checkCore`, `Seq.closeSize`, `Seq.stackSize`, `Seq.fitsCore`, `LFml.boundsOk`), evaluated once by the kernel (`Seq.checkCore_valid`); else fails; `sol_reduce?` shows the reduction (`sol_reduce`, `Fml.Valid.of_reduceS`); `sol_decide_close`. |
| `Calculus/Closer.lean` | `LFml.close`: the closer, KeY's first-order and arithmetic taclets as clauses of one `Bool` over typed terms, sound by `LFml.close_valid`.  What it knows is a `Facts` (rewrites, pairs apart, terms that return, ranges); a term is read in its normal form (`Facts.nf`: literals folded, `Facts.fold2` with comparisons of terms apart (`Facts.apart`), `(a + b) - b` and `(a - b) + b` cancelled (`Facts.cancel`, `Facts.cancelAdd`), `2 * a` as `a + a` (`isTwo`, `evalT_two_mul`), `a < a + c` true (`Facts.ltSucc`, Lean only), `kiteBool?`, keys compared (`Facts.keyCmp`, `Facts.kiteN`)); a premise adds facts (`Facts.addEqn`, `Facts.addNeq`, `Facts.addBool`; a guard `kite(c, true, a, err)` read as an assert, `kiteAssert`, `assertArg?`; the facts read again, `Facts.rebuild`); ranges (`Facts.range`, `ltDec`, `Facts.cmpFold`), `has` below a length (`Facts.hasRange`); a key split (`LTm.pick`, `Facts.proveEqN`); the size count `LFml.fits`.  Split by topic into the pieces below (`docs/parallelism.md`), this module the umbrella. |
| `Calculus/Closer/Facts.lean` | What the symbols read, `Facts`, ranges, what returns, locations, an index below the length. |
| `Calculus/Closer/Normal.lean` | Guarded arithmetic normal forms, including cancellation of either summand of a checked sum (`Facts.cancel`, `cancel_eval`); the following module completes `Facts.nf`. |
| `Calculus/Closer/Bytes.lean` | Normal forms, continued: bytes reassembled into words (Lean only) and the rest. |
| `Calculus/Closer/Leaf.lean` | What a premise tells, and closing a leaf (`LFml.close`, `LFml.verdict_sound`). |
| `Calculus/MemRead.lean` | Memory read as solkey reads it (`memoryRules.key`, `structMemoryRules.key`), proved on the typed heap: a read walked down the memory term's writes and allocations (`LTm.memRes`, `memReadN`, `memIReadN`: `readOnWrite`, `readOnWriteFrame` by `LTm.apart?`, `readOnAddMFrame` by `LTm.boundedIn`, `readOnAddM` at a fresh path, `readFromCopyToStorage` by `copyFieldRd?`), an array's length (`lenAtN`), identities apart (`identApart?`, `freshOver?`), the guards a memory term returns under (`MemQ`, `guardQ`, `guardL`); `LFml.nfE`, `LFml.closeMem`, `LFml.decideMem` (sound: `LFml.decideMem_valid`); a copy's root formed over its memory without the writes above its newest allocation by `addM` (`LTm.stripW`, `copyRes`, heap-independent identities `SVal.copyStToM_H'`, `SVal.copyStToM_id_eq`), read back at its node (`LTm.onSpineS`, `LTm.unstripAddr`); a copy's objects below its root (`copyObjN`, `copyPathRd?`, fresh by `LTm.copyFresh?`) and an address's guards out (`LTm.peel`, `LTm.hoistA`, `wrapG`, `framesCond?`); `LFml.memElim`.  Split by topic into the pieces below (`docs/parallelism.md`), this module the umbrella. |
| `Calculus/MemRead/Spine.lean` | What an allocation mints, the spine, older objects, the fresh root, views, guards. |
| `Calculus/MemRead/Laws.lean` | A copy's objects, their views, the laws of a read. |
| `Calculus/MemRead/Ok.lean` | Array lengths walked down a memory, memory terms that return. |
| `Calculus/MemRead/Pass.lean` | The walk down a memory, the keccak of bytes read off it, the pass, the fold. |
| `Calculus/Problem.lean` | solkey's obligation forms, scoped (`Fml C []`): `Problem.fml` (`∀x̄. wt(storage) → [f] true` or `⟨f⟩ true`, the program in the parameters' context `paramsN`), the constructor's `Problem.ctorFml` from `Problem.deployUpd`, `Problem.ctor` (`problem!{constructor}`), `Fml.wt`, `holds_wt` (`σ.storage.wtB`, the test itself), `shape_iff_reachable`, `wt_iff_reachable` (over `Storage C`), `initStorage_wt` (at `State.init C`); `Problem.parts` (by position: `Problem.isWt`, `Problem.isDeploy`), `Problem.text` in solkey's syntax. |
| `Calculus/TailMode.lean` | Clean array tails as an option (O6): `Fml.cleanTail` (`cleanTail(storage) = true`, `holds_cleanTail`), `TailMode` (`solc`, `clean`), `ValidTail` (`validTail_solc`, `validTail_clean`, `ValidTail.holds_of_clean`, `holds_init`, `clean_of_solc`); `ValidTail.holds_reachable` (every state a contract that exposes nothing reaches). |
| `Calculus/CleanTailBoundary.lean` | Why clean tails are an assumption on the starting storage and not kept by every program (O6; kept where nothing is exposed: `Typing/CleanTailRun.lean`): the boundary conditions `Prog.CleanTailBoundary`, `CleanTailCallbackBoundary` (`StateInv.cleanTail`), refuted in `Examples/Tactics/CleanTail.lean`. |
| `Calculus/SurfaceSpec.lean` | Specifications compiled to dynamic logic as solkey's `SpecCompiler` does: `specPieces` (the clauses as `NRead`s; the call elaborated scoped, `SProg`), which `Calculus/Spec.lean` puts together; the syntax `spec[C]{f}`, `spec!{f}` (`Calculus/Spec.lean`'s `cspec[C]{f}`); `sol_spec`. |
| `Calculus/SurfaceNotation.lean` | `dl[C]{ … }` and `dl!{ … }`: the raw reader of concrete formulas (`expandFml`), read scoped by `Calculus/Notation.lean`; the term readers (`readers`, `tVal` …, `elabUpd`) to terms by name (`NTm`, `Calculus/NamedTerm.lean`); `dl![m]{ … }`, `⟨[ ]⟩` at a modality `m`; a Lean formula where a formula stands; `Γ ⟹ φ` lines |
| `Calculus/TreeData.lean` | The proof tree as data (`NodeInfo`, `Tree`: open/solved leaves and rule nodes), solkey's rows and branch labels (`Tree.rows`, `branchLabel`), `Tree.toJson`, `Tree.display`, the walk it writes (`Tree.script`, `suggest`) and the helpers of a tactic writing a proof out (`runOn`, `short`, `qualified`, `tacLines`, `termLines`, `Chain.lastName`). |
| `Calculus/RewriteChains.lean` | The chain arrows' syntax (`~>`, `~*>`, `~[r]~>`, `~=>`, `~~>`) and what an arrow names past the strategy (`rwArrow?`: an update rule of `rwTable`, a `TermTaclet`, an `EvalLaw`, a `LitLaw`), a law's instances in a line's closed terms (`lawInstances`, `matchLaw`, a law's twins in a storage of members `lawTwins`, the spine of a storage of members `spinePositions`), `~=>`'s order (`rwAnyArrows`); `sol_chain` on a goal no chain elaborator takes.  A law's side conditions are closed by their shape (`lawAt`: a closed `Bool`/`Option` test by `rfl` alone, `KindFreeAt` by a hypothesis). |
| `Calculus/RewriteChainGen.lean` | The pieces a chain is written out with: the rewrites a chain takes (`chainRewrite`), the search's bound and measure (`genNodes`, `lineSize`), fresh spellings, the strategy's steps grouped as the worked examples print them (`groupSteps`); the syntax of `#last_line`, `#chain`, `#chain_rest`, `sol_rws`, `sol_rws?`. |
| `Calculus/TermLib.lean` | The term taclets' printers (`delabTmS`, by `Calculus/PrintTerm.lean`'s) and pins, above `Calculus/TermLib/Rules.lean`, which it imports. |
| `Calculus/TermLib/Rules.lean` | In the core, below every printer: the term taclets on scoped, typed terms: `TermTaclet` on `Tm C Γ (.val P)`, each constructor a solkey taclet (`select` read in the struct's frame: `findIn`, `saveIn`, `delAtIn`; a push and a pop as their symbols), and `TermTaclet.sound` over the scoped `Tm.denote`, the side conditions on scoped paths (`Tm.hasSeg`, `Tm.diverges`, `Tm.sameSegs`, `Tm.shift?` to a `Shifted`, …, `SCtxS` over storages of members; `findMemberConsIn`, `findOnSaveIn` and `selectOn{Save,DelAt}{Member,Frame}At` one member further down, `Tm.shiftIn?`), generic in the sort with the symbol read by its own `match` (`Op1.shapeStep` …); `tm{ … }` writes the scoped term (printed back by `Calculus/TermLib.lean`).  `findOnDelAtBelowIn` with its member-wise side conditions (`Tm.findLitIn?`, `Tm.extendsIn`, `Tm.divergesU`); `Tm.KindFreeAt` at a storage or a storage of members (`Srt.pathAt`).; the side conditions read on a path's location (`SLoc.labels`, `SLoc.strip?_none_of_diverges`, `Tm.diverges_denote`: two locations neither below the other) in the typed Theory. |
| `Calculus/TermEq.lean` | Terms compared: equal in the Theory (`Tm.Theq`, the rewrites' premise), structurally across sorts (`Tm.beqR`, recursor with `match` twin, the typed symbols by their codes `Op1Code` …, `IndexTy.eq`, `ArrTy.eq`), and the locals a literal value term reads (`Tm.litVarsS`). |

## The data-structure theories

solkey's `find`/`save`/`read`/`write` are uninterpreted symbols whose meaning is
a taclet set. These modules are that theory on typed values, each taclet a
law stated under its solkey name.
The map from taclet to theorem is `docs/lean-key-rule-map.md`.

| Module | What it defines |
|---|---|
| `Theory/Typed/Obs.lean` | The typed Theory: a value is its observations (`Obs L`: word, live length, presence, at the locations `L`), `TVal T`, `TSto C` over `SLoc C` (a state variable's path or a dangling one); `find`/`save`/`delAt`/`copyTo`/`push`/`extend`/`pop`/`select` stated once over any location family (`ObsLoc`), a delete/strip/copy computed from the type (`TVal.del`, `strip`, `overlay`, one `Comp`onent at a time); the bridge from the typed storage (`SVal.obs`, `Storage.obs`, `obs_save`, `obs_write`, `obs_delete`, `obs_push`, `obs_pop`, …).  Split by topic into the pieces below (`docs/parallelism.md`), this module the umbrella. |
| `Theory/Typed/Obs/Paths.lean` | Typed paths, observations, storage locations, typed values read below a path. |
| `Theory/Typed/Obs/Values.lean` | Values and storages as their observations, a read step by step, the bridge of delete/strip/copy. |
| `Theory/Typed/Obs/Ops.lean` | Under a `select`, the storage operations, their bridge and laws. |
| `Theory/Typed/Mem.lean` | The Theory of memory, typed: solkey's memory laws on the typed heap (`Grown H`, `MAddrT T`): a write read back and its frame (`Grown.writeT_facts`), a grown heap keeps the older objects (`Heap.Keeps`, `SVal.keeps_copyStToM`, `Heap.Keeps.readAddr`, `Grown.read_then_old`, `Grown.readId_then_old`) and an allocation's freshness (`Grown.alloc_id_ge`), a fresh object's defaults and lengths (`Heap.Dflt`, `Heap.dflt_allocDefault`, `Heap.allocDefault_array_len`, `newArrT_copy_len`, the defaults' equations from `Typing/Values.lean`), copies between storage and memory (`SVal.copyStToM_field`, `MVal.copyMToSt_field`, `MVal.copyBelow_eq` by `MVal.copyAt_congr`); no erasure is read; the section "The laws by solkey's taclets" restates each under its taclet's name, a `\else` as its `Frame` (`Grown.readOnWrite`, `Grown.readOnWriteFrame`, `Grown.readOnAddMFrame`, `Heap.initMember`, `Heap.initSize`, `SVal.readFromCopyToStorage`, `Grown.readFromCopyToStorageFrame`, `MVal.findOnCopy`; Lean only, `Grown.alloc_fresh`). |
| `Theory/Typed/MemMirror.lean` | A copy into memory below its root: `SVal.Mirrors` (an object holds what the storage value held, member by member, past a heap), kept by allocations (`SVal.Mirrors.wk`) and made by `SVal.copyStToM` (`SVal.mirrors_copyStToM`); read at a member and an element (`SFields.Mirrors.get`, `SVec.Mirrors.getElem`). |
| `Theory/Typed/Leaves.lean` | The leaves of a type (`Ty.leaves`: words by members and in-range fixed indices, fuel by `rankBound`; `Ty.leafCount`; `Ty.mem_leaves` by `Path.direct`/`Path.out`; disjoint, `Ty.leaves_strip?_ne`, `Leaf.diverges`), finite shapes (`Ty.Finite`, its test `Ty.finiteShape`), and the expansions they give: in the Theory under `TVal.Shaped` (`SVal.obs_shaped`, `Storage.obs_shaped`) a delete and a copy are a word written per leaf (`Obs.delAt_expand`, `Obs.copyTo_find_expand`); in the interpreter unconditionally at a shape with a leaf (`Storage.delete_expand`, `Storage.write_expand`, `Storage.write_find_expand`); a push and a pop of a word (`Obs.push_prim`, `Obs.pop_prim`, `Storage.push_prim`, `Storage.pop_prim`). |
| `Theory/Segs.lean` | Two paths of segments that part (`Theory.diverges`) and the segment a length is read at (`Theory.lengthSeg`), what the frame rules' side conditions read; list functions on segments. |
| `Theory/Bridge/Denote.lean` | What a typed symbol denotes in the typed Theory (`Srt.TDen C`: a word an `Option`, a path an `SLoc`, a storage a `TSto`, a stored value a `TVal`; `Op0.denote` … `Op3.denote` over `Storage.obs`) and the bridge to its typed evaluation (`Srt.Bridge`, `Op0.denote_eval` … `Op3.denote_eval`: the value read as its observations, literally). The only Theory module that imports the signature directly; `Theory/Typed/Mem.lean` reads it (`MAddrT`, `Grown.writeT`) through `Calculus/StateFacts.lean`. |

## Typing and sort faithfulness

| Module | What it defines |
|---|---|
| `Typing/Values.lean` | Typed storage values, hereditarily: `Hered` (a condition per node kind) and `SVal.All P`, a fold with `eqns`; what every such predicate shares: reads (`SVal.findM_all`), defaults (`SVal.dflt_all`, the defaults' equations `SVal.dflt_struct` … by fuel independence, `SVal.dfltBelow_irrel`, which `Theory/Typed/Mem.lean` reads too), saves along keys a program writes and live word slots (`SVal.saveM_all`, `Path.Keys`, `SVal.Live`), `strip`/`defaultOf`/`overlay` (`Hered.StripOK`, `DelOK`, `OvOK`), copies out of memory (`SVal.Plain`, `MVal.copyMToSt_plain`), implications (`SVal.all_mono`, `Hered.Mono`); the save algebra (`SVal.saveM_idem`, `SVal.saveM_saveM`); a test that decides a predicate (`HeredB.Decides`, `SVal.allB_iff`). |
| `Typing/Soundness.lean` | `RunInv`, the shape of a run invariant: a part on the storage (`Storage C`), a part on each local at its type, kept as the heap grows (`loc_wk`). |
| `Typing/TypedRunInv.lean` | A `RunInv` read of a typed state (`RunInv.HoldsT`: `σ.storage`, and `TEnv.All` over the locals at their types) and carried along the typed run (`RunInv.TKeeps`): the scopes keep any (`tkeeps_seq` … `tkeeps_tryCall`, a loop by `TLoop.run_induct`; a call's and a `try`'s locals by `RunInv.ValMemOk`). |
| `Typing/HeredRun.lean` | A hereditary predicate the typed run keeps, statement by statement (`Stmt.keeps_all`, `Prog.run_all`): `HeredRun P G`, what each storage operation asks of the node conditions (`G` a property of the contract's types, `Ty.okDeep` for tightness); `heredInv` (the storage's `All P`); the storage operations (`Storage.save_all` … `pop_all`), resolved references live (`Loc.resolveT_live`); each leaf on `TRun.*`, once for the three predicates. |
| `Typing/Sorts.lean` | Runtime sorts of typed values (`SVal.keySort`, `MVal.keySort`: a word's by its type, a storage node `Struct`, a memory reference `Identity`), read by `SortCheck/Faithfulness.lean` and `Counterexamples/StaticRuntimeSort.lean`. |
| `Typing/Reachability.lean` | Every reachable storage is canonical (`canonH`: a mapping's keys distinct, its default the type's, at every node; `Prog.run_canon`, `reachable_canon`); `Reachable` is a scoped program's typed run from `State.init`; `map_default_not_reachable`, `map_dup_key_not_reachable`. |
| `Typing/Tight.lean` | Tight storage (`tightH`: no slot of an array of elements without a well-formed default, cleared words past an array's end, no entry of a reference-keyed mapping), kept for every contract (`Prog.run_tight`, `reachable_tight`, using `SRef.okDeep` from its declaration certificates); `SVal.Fits` (`fitsH`), what the builder writes with literals. |
| `Typing/Fits.lean` | Decision 77's conditional form: under `FixedFits` (every fixed size at most `2^256`, solc's "type too large for storage") every reachable storage fits (`reachable_fits`): the bounded run invariant (`bndH`: a dynamic array's slots below `2^256`, `reachable_bnd`) and the fixed sizes from the types (`Storage.fits_of_bnd`). |
| `Typing/Constructibility.lean` | The converse: canonical and tight is reachable for keys and slots that fit (`canon_reachable`; `reachable_iff`, `reachable_iff_fits`, `no_hidden_invariant`): the builder `Build.rootsProg`/`fill` runs by the typed run (`TRuns`, `run_push` … `run_bound`) to the storage it is given (`fill_ok`), tracked as saves at one path (`Wrote`); `dirty_word_not_reachable`, `huge_reachable_not_fits`, `bool_key_reachable`, `dangling_slot_reachable`. |
| `Typing/CanonTest.lean` | What the tests of `wt(storage)` (`Semantics/StorageWt.lean`) decide, by `SVal.allB_iff` (`Typing/Values.lean`): canonical and tight (`SVal.canonB_iff`, `tightB_iff`), `Storage.shapeB_iff`. |
| `Typing/CleanTail.lean` | Clean array tails (O6): `SVal.OrdinaryDefault` (a word at its default, a dynamic array of length `0`, a mapping anything), `Storage.CleanTail` (`cleanTailH`: every shadow one), what keeps it (`Storage.init_cleanTail`, `SVal.defaultOf_cleanTail`, `SVal.emptyPush_ordinaryDefault`, `SVal.find_cleanTail`), `Storage.cleanTailB_iff`; as the observations show it (`Path.KeyFree`, `SRef.Shadowed`, `TSto.CleanTails`, `Storage.cleanTails_obs`), the Theory's reading of `cleanTail(s)`. |
| `Typing/CleanTailRun.lean` | Clean tails as a contract's invariant (O6): `SVal.LiveAll`, `Storage.Grows`, the storage operations at live slots keep clean tails (`SVal.saveM_ct`, `Storage.push_ct` …), the invariant `CTInv` (clean storage, aliases live), the check `Stmt.ctSafe`/`Prog.exposesNone` and its soundness `Stmt.keeps_ct`, `ReachableBy.cleanTail`. |
| `SortCheck/Annotations.lean` | The taclets' read-sort annotations, transcribed from the `.key` file. |
| `SortCheck/Parser.lean` | Token-level `.key` scanner and the `conforms` cross-check. |
| `SortCheck/Faithfulness.lean` | Each annotation holds of what the typed run's evaluators read in every typed state, at the value's runtime sort (`Read.SortOk`, `Read.sortOk`, `Typing/Sorts.lean`), at every statement its `Taclet` rule fires on (`faithful_theorem`, the statement read off the constructor's type). |
| `SolkeyCheck.lean` (root) | `lake exe solkeycheck`. |
| `SolCases.lean` (root) | `lake exe solcases`: deterministic bounded cases as JSONL; the seed rotates the boundary pool. |
| `Counterexamples/*.lean` | Three refutation modules pinning design decisions: `DeleteFamilyGenericOverlap`, `StaticRuntimeSort`, `PreFixSortAnnotations`. |
| `Examples/Tactics/SignedCasts.lean` | Casts between `uint` and `int` and signed `unchecked`, through `int(u)`/`uint(i)`: solkey's `SolcTypesOpen`/`SolcArithmeticOpen` cases. |
| `Examples/Tactics/SolcOpen.lean` | What solkey's `solc/open` ports needed of the elaborator: `E(x)` asserts a member, a call's value has its callee's return width under `unchecked`. |
| `Examples/Tactics/StorageDangling.lean` | solkey's dangling-storage suite: an empty push saves only the length, so a popped alias's writes are exposed again; the four obligations derived in every state by `sol_prove`. |

## The EVM compiler

`docs/compiler-verification.md` has the theorem, the fragment and what is out.

| Module | What it defines |
|---|---|
| `Evm/Machine.lean` | A straight-line EVM: slots as terms, wrapping words, a bounded `WordStore` (`Slot → Fin (2^256)`, numeric reads, normalized writes), relative forward jumps, the accounts' balances; memory addressed by level (`Nat`). |
| `Evm/Memory.lean` | Literal-base and runtime-pointer primitive-array access code and a bounded representation of actual typed heap objects; exact reads, writes and out-of-bounds reversion. |
| `Evm/MemoryStruct.lean` | One-word member offsets for memory structs, typed primitive-field representations with no-wrap bounds and no length header, exact runtime-pointer read/write instructions and same-object field preservation. |
| `Evm/MemoryStructHeap.lean` | Global struct identity bases, struct/local/array interval separation, and both directions of struct/array write framing. |
| `Evm/MemoryStructAlloc.lean` | Allocator ownership of live struct intervals in addition to arrays; cursor, zero-tail, separation and both layouts preserved by local, struct-field and array-element writes. |
| `Evm/MemoryStructGrow.lean` | Combined struct/array ownership across typed allocations; exact headerless struct allocation, primitive default fields, fresh/older interval separation, retained old addresses and cursor/zero-tail growth. |

Loop bodies retain the existing finite certificate. |

| `Evm/MemoryCompileExamples.lean` | Populated source aliases and an independent array; compiled reads/writes, alias declarations/rebinding, lengths, defaults, composed RHS/index reads, public-entry dispatch, capacity and exact-revert regressions. |
