| `Evm/MemoryExamples.lean` | A populated typed-heap representation witness, checked reads/writes, shared-base visibility, frame/length preservation, boundary reversion and address-wrap rejection. |
| `Evm/Layout.lean` | The storage layout (`size`, `offset`, `rootSlot`), its injectivity (`Occ`, `occ_*_disjoint`), what `delete` clears (`leaves`), the fragment's operators (`binInFrag`, `unInFrag`) and static types (`static`), and solc's guard and code sequences (`uTail`, `sTail`, `expCode`, `boundsCheck`, `pushSlotCode`, …). |
| `Evm/StorageLemmas.lean` | The typed storage read along a path, for the simulation: a live read below a path (`findLiveM_append`), a live read is a read (`findM_of_findLiveM`), the reads and index checks of a reference's member, key and element (`Sto.findLive_field` …, `Sto.checkIndex_dyn` …), a default's members below a rank (`get_dfltBelow`; at full rank `SFields.dflt_get`, `Theory/Typed/Obs.lean`). |
| `Evm/Repr.lean` | The representation of the typed storage: `ReprV` (a word represents a typed primitive), `ReprAt` over `SVal T`, `ReprStore`, `PathAt`/`PathSlot` over typed paths and `SRef`s; `find_repr`, `save_repr`, `zero_repr`, `pop_repr`, `move_repr`, `overlay_repr`, `push_repr`, `live_mono` (`OccAt`, `LenSlot`, `PrimSlot`), `initStorage_repr`. |
| `Evm/Signed.lean` | The signed guard sequences, exact on two's complement. |
| `Evm/Exp.lean` | `**`: solc's `checked_exp_unsigned`, unrolled. |
| `Evm/SimLemmas.lean` | The machine lemmas of the simulation: the guard sequences computed (`add_tail`, …), the operators' tails on the typed operators (`tail_simT` from `utail_simT` and its signed half `stail_simT`, `neg_simT`, `bnot_simT`, `compound_simT`, `bump_simT`: `BinOp.evalT`'s integer result and `PrimTy.ofInt?`'s range check against the guard's word), `ValOutT`, the slot sequences (`boundsCheck_run`, `elemSlot_run`), `EnvSim` over `TTx`, `netAt`, the store sequences (`zeroCode_run`, `popCode_run`, `storeLeaves_run`, `pushSlotCode_run`), `push_eq`, `write_ref`. |
| `Evm/Compile.lean` | The compiler over the scoped syntax (M5, `docs/intrinsic.md`): a local at its level (`cell`, `lvl`), a call's body in its own frame, the fragment `inFrag` over `Frag Γ` (alias flags only), `Stmt.pushes`, `Stmt.pays`. |
| `Evm/Examples.lean` | Programs of `StandardExample`, `CallsExample` and `TestSuite` (`Evm.Examples.setAge` …), scoped blocks compiled by `Evm/Compile.lean`, run by `decide +kernel`. |
| `Evm/Sim.lean` | The simulation over the scoped syntax: `SimL` of a global part (`State C []`) and the locals by position, typed (`Look C Γ`, `Seen`: a value, an alias's `SRef`; `Look.ofTEnv`), declarations as `Look.cons` (`SimL.consVal`/`consAlias`/`consFragile`/`consDead`), a scope's end as `SimL.drop`, the fragment's flags (`SimL.flags`, `meet`, `storeDrop`), a call's frame (`SimL.enter`, `SimL.return`); `Sim C L b F σ m` of a typed state; compiled code at base `b` stores to no level below `b` (`storesFrom_compileStmt`, `storesFrom_callFrame`, `exec_mem`). |
| `Evm/Expr.lean` | The expression lemmas over the typed run: `LocOutT` over the typed storage, `simple_sim`, `spath_sim`, `loc_sim`, `val_sim`. |
| `Evm/Stmt.lean` | One lemma per statement of the fragment over the typed run, a storage write the typed save (`save_repr`): `StmtOut` (with `Keeps`: cells below the frame, array lengths), `assign_sim` … `transfer_sim`. |
| `Evm/Correct.lean` | `stmt_sim`/`prog_sim` over the scoped syntax and `Prog.run` (within `maxHeartbeats 11000`) (one lemma per family, `ite_sim`, `block_sim`, `callNone_sim`/`callVal_sim`), then `compile_correct`, `compile_exact`, `compile_box`, `compile_net`, `compile_storage`; no `not_stuck` (no stuck outcome). |
| `Evm/Interpreter.lean` | The compiler's interpreter theorems over `Prog.run` (`setAge_interpreter`, `overflow_interpreter`, `payOwner_interpreter`, `callTwice_interpreter`), from `Evm/Examples.lean`'s machine runs. |
| `Backend/Opcode.lean` | The backend's target (G45.05, [backend-assembler.md](feature-work/backend-assembler.md)): every Osaka opcode (`Op`, with `CLZ`, `TLOAD`/`TSTORE`, `MCOPY`; `DUP`/`SWAP`/`LOG` indexed by `Fin`), `Instr` with `PUSHn` immediates of exactly `n` bytes, `encode`/`decode` (`decode_encode`, `encode_injective`, `encode_decode`). |
| `Backend/Jumpdest.lean` | Jump-destination analysis on any bytes (`starts`, `jumpdests`, `validJumpdest`, `InPushData`): valid iff `JUMPDEST` outside push data (`validJumpdest_iff`, `mem_starts`), and on encoded programs iff a `JUMPDEST` instruction sits at that offset (`validJumpdest_encode_iff`, `starts_encode_append`). |
| `Backend/Assembler.lean` | The assembler (G45.04): solc-style items (`tag`, `pushTag`, `pushTagRel`, `pushLibraryAddress`), big-endian immediates (`toBytesBE`, `ofBytesBE`, `byteWidth`), per-label `PUSH` widths by a terminating fixpoint (`fixWidths`), `assemble` at a base offset with link references; every resolved jump reaches its label's `JUMPDEST` (`assemble_pushTag`), success (`assemble_isSome`), linking (`link_patch`). |
| `Backend/Control.lean` | G45.03 preparation: structured blocks (`code`, `seq`, `ite`, `loop`) expanded into assembler items with fresh `Nat` labels in solc's legacy shapes; each label defined once and every reference defined (`tags_nodup`, `refs_defined`), so assembly succeeds (`expand_assemble_isSome`); wherever a loop or `if` is placed, its backedge, exit and branch jumps push the offsets of its own labels (`loop_jumps`, `ite_jumps`). |
| `Backend/EvmVectors.lean` | Generated by `scripts/check-backend-vectors.mjs`: EthereumJS's Osaka opcode table, the vendored EVMYulLean `parseInstr`/`argOnNBytesOfInstr` table, 300 randomized jump-destination scans and 200 randomized encoded programs (EthereumJS's scan and `PUSH` values, transliterations of EVMYulLean's `D_J_aux` and `decode` immediate), each `#guard`ed against `byteName`, `immWidth`, `decode`/`encode` and `jumpdests`. |
| `Backend/Checks.lean` | Byte-encoding and assembler vectors (`#guard`) and the axiom pins of the encoder, decoder, jump-destination and assembler theorems. |

## Tools

Commands for people; they prove nothing beyond the certificates they check.
`Examples/Tools.lean` and `Examples/Verify.lean` pin their output.

| Module | What it defines |
|---|---|
| `Tools/Show.lean` | Printers over the typed state: `SVal.fmt` (a fold, `fmtFold`), `fmtStorage`/`fmtStorageLine`/`fmtRoot`, `fmtTx`, `fmtHalt`/`fmtTRes` (a `THalt`; `exhausted` prints as `diverge`), a `Repr` of `State C Γ` for `#eval`; the clauses as written (`SpecExpr.fmt`). |
| `Tools/Common.lean` | Shared plumbing: the typed generator (`TGen`, `TGen.sval`/`storage`, canonical typed storages; `#difftest`'s and `#counterexample`'s), resolving `C` / `C.f`, evaluating terms, report layout; imports `Syntax`, not the closer's `Update`. |
| `Tools/Run.lean` | `#run C.f(args) [from σ] [with msg.sender := n, …]`: the typed run from a typed state (`State C []`; fresh, `freshState`, by default), `State.withTx`/`withRoots`; `#deploy C(args) [with …]`, a deployment by `Contract.deployT` (`Semantics/Deploy.lean`); `Prog.runT`/`localAfterT`, a scoped block run typed from a typed state (`State.exampleStore`, `State.testSuiteStore`: the examples' `#eval`s). |
| `Tools/Upstream.lean` | `#upstream_batch`: bounded, checkpointed replay of scalar/canonical-prefix typed ABI vectors, raw-calldata ABI coder v2 entries through the typed lazy/eager entry decoder (`Tools/UpstreamEntry.lean`), primitive v2 decoder checks and exact external-entry failure bytes through the typed importer/interpreter; separate working, broken and blocked evidence ([solidity-upstream-tests.md](solidity-upstream-tests.md)). |
| `Tools/UpstreamEntry.lean` | The upstream replay's bridge to `Semantics/AbiEntry.lean`: parameter shapes and locations from a declaration, and the replay plan for one raw v2 vector (`revert`, `run` on canonical bytes, `blocked`, `legacy`); access-time calldata reads guarded in the replayed body (`accessOnly`, `guardDecl`, `accessGuard__calldata`). |
| `Tools/Inspect.lean` | `#wp φ`, `#step φ` on a scoped formula (`Fml C Γ`): the scoped strategy compiled, its result quoted and printed by `Delab`; the reduction (`Fml.reduceS`) and its printer (`LFml.ppWith`, a position printed by its binder's name: `Fml.keyNames`, `keyedNames`).  The module a file inspecting the calculus imports: it brings `Tools/ProofTree.lean` and `Tools/Taclet.lean`. |
| `Tools/Taclet.lean` | `#taclet r`: a scoped rule (`Taclet`, `LeanTaclet`, `CallbackTaclet`), its solkey taclets, printed rule and soundness (`toStep`), from the tables (`RuleShapes`, `PrintedRules`); `#check_rule_text`, that every rule of the three prints as a rule line. |
| `Tools/DiffTest.lean` | `#difftest C[.f]`: the typed run against `Evm/Compile.lean`'s code on random typed storages (`diffGen`, a `TGen`); the layout as a fold over the typed value (`encodeFold`, `ReprAt` read as a function; `mapKeysFold`). |
| `Tools/Counterexample.lean` | `Fml.eval3`, a scoped formula evaluated over the typed state and the typed run with fuel (`eval3_sound` against `holds`; by the recursor, its compiled twin `eval3C`), its leading parameters bound to a witness's arguments (`Fml.peel`), a typed candidate (`Cand C`, its state `Cand.tstate`) drawn by a typed generator (`TGen.sval`) and shrunk by folds (`intsFold`, `renameFold`), an update's result (`Upd.applyV`) lifted to a typed state it lands on (`State.lift?` over a global part and the locals' values, `TEnv.liftV?`), witness search and shrinking, certificates of `¬ ⊨ᶠ` of the scoped formula at the candidate's typed state, quoted (`quoteFold`, `Cand.quote`), `#counterexample`; programs run by `Prog.runA` (counting loops in closed form); long-array candidates (`longCands`: a dynamic-array root `2^255` or `2^256 - 1` long, `lazyArray`), certified by the kernel alone (`certifyLong`: `wt(storage)` proved, `proveMembersAllB`, the body evaluated) when the ordinary search finds nothing (`refuteLong`). |
| `Tools/LazyStorage.lean` | Storages too large to write down: `SVec.rep v n` (`n` copies, unfolded only as far as read), `wt(storage)` of one proved, not evaluated (`SVec.allB_rep`, `SVal.allB_arrayOf_rep`, `SFields.allB_cons_true`, `holds_wt_of`). |
| `Tools/Verify.lean` | `#verify C[.f]`: each spec'd function proved, refuted or given up on (`gaveUp`). |
| `Tools/ProofTree.lean` | `#proof_size` measures walk/leaf sizes (moved from the PolySize example); `#proof_tree φ`, `#proof_node n φ`, `#proof_tree_json φ` on a scoped formula: solkey's view of the tree of `⊢ φ` (`ProofTree.ofFormula`); `elabCoreFormula?`, the formula a command reads. |
| `Tools/LatexTerm.lean` | Scoped terms, updates and formulas rendered from their `Expr` as LaTeX: `Options`, the `plain` and `macros` backends, the symbol table `symbols` and its check `uncovered`, `definitionKeys`, `Doc` and its line breaking (`docs/latex.md`). |
| `Tools/Latex.lean` | The scoped calculus registered with vendored LeanTeX; formulas, sequents, chains, derivations (`derivationLatex`) and declaration statements to LaTeX strings/files; the options (`latexOpt`), `#latex_definition_targets`, `#latex_chains_files` (`scripts/latex-chains.sh`). |
| `Tools/LatexSolkey.lean` | `#latex_derivation` and `#latex_obligation`: a generated derivation, a `solc_import`ed function's obligation (`scripts/latex-sol.sh`). |
| `Tools/Elementary.lean` | Bounded capture/update traces built from `Calculus/Elementary.lean`'s laws. |
| `Tools/ProgramTrace.lean` | Checked formula/chain/obligation traces with ordered read continuations: `#latex_program`, `#latex_program_file`, `#program_trace_json_file`, `ProgramTrace.generate`. |

## Roadmap statements

| Module | What it defines |
|---|---|
| `Roadmap/Contracts.lean` | Roadmap G01: the six final source-to-bytecode contracts, observations, resourced prefix families and compositional pass certificates as statements only (`Cert.comp`, `PublicCorrectness`); no final theorem is claimed ([roadmap-g01-interfaces.md](feature-work/roadmap-g01-interfaces.md)). |
| `Roadmap/Frontend.lean` | Roadmap G05: checked frontend certificate interfaces as statements only: raw input, parse, resolution and metadata checkers (`Chain.checked`), digests as identity (`Digests.identity`, `Digests.only_invariant`), admitted settings: solc's two pipelines (three order/coder pairs) and the empty link map ([roadmap-g05-frontend.md](feature-work/roadmap-g05-frontend.md)). |
| `Roadmap/Evm.lean` | Roadmap G06: the pinned Osaka EVM interface as statements only: the pin and its idealizations (`pinned`), the Osaka precompile table, the shared fixed functions with the per-run hash premise and `CREATE` addresses (`Fixed`), resources as gas schedules, fuel and source failure points (`GasRes`, `FailurePoints`), frame observation (`Halt.observe`) and the core/Bridge `Certificate` ([roadmap-g06-evm.md](feature-work/roadmap-g06-evm.md)). |
| `Roadmap/TypedRun.lean` | `typedRunPrefixes`: the fuelled typed run (`Prog.runF`) read as a G01 prefix family, kept apart from `Roadmap/Contracts.lean` so that a source semantics importing the contracts does not reach the intrinsic run. |
| `Roadmap/Controls.lean` | Roadmap G07: inhabited and negative controls for the G01 contracts: a toy source, intrinsic layer, EVM and compiler meeting `PublicCorrectness` (`toy_publicCorrectness`), and refutations of an always-reverting, a byte-altering, a data-erasing and a malformed-source-accepting compiler; `PublicCorrectness.observes`, `PublicCorrectness.rejects`, `SourceSpec.not_nonVacuous_of_no_parse`. |
| `Roadmap/V16/SSCP2/NewAllocation.lean` | Actual dynamic-new data validity and MemOK preservation, with a closed local-length consumer. |
| `Roadmap/V16/SSCP2/NewAllocationControls.lean` | Live caller references, both pipelines, exact allocation-size/timeout/refusal phases and wrong-reference controls. |

## Independent source definitions

| Module | What it defines |
|---|---|
| `Source/Syntax.lean` | S00: the pinned solc 0.8.38 grammar as intrinsic syntax `Syn : Cat → Type`, indexed by category and by statement context (`SCtx`: loop, `unchecked`), excluded syntax (assembly/Yul, `layout at`, `>>>`, `years`) included. |
| `Source/Ctor.lean` | S00: the constructor index `Ctor` (`Ctor.mem_all`), `Syn.nodes`/`Syn.children`, the syntax-level exclusions `Excl` and precise rejection (`Syn.rejections_of_sub`). |
| `Source/Upstream.lean` | S00: the explicit mapping from grammar rules/labels and manifest features to constructors (`grammarCtors`, `featureCtors`; `Ctor.grammar_covers`), checked by `scripts/check-source-grammar.mjs`. |
| `Source/Decls.lean` | S00: the typed declaration graph (`Decl`, `Syn.decls`, `Decl.refs`, `Syn.decls_ctor`). |
| `Source/Examples.lean` | S00 controls: an in-profile counter and rejected assembly, `layout at`, `>>>` and `years` programs. |
| `Source/Const.lean` | S01: compile-time constants: exact rationals `Q`, literal values and units, `Syn.const` (folding `+ - * / % **`, shifts and two's-complement bitwise operations), the implicit and explicit conversions of constants to integer and fixed-point types. |
| `Source/Types.lean` | S01: static types `STy`, elaboration of elementary types, implicit and explicit conversions (fixed point per the second-round decisions) with `implicit_int_range` and `FixTy.subsumes_inRange`. |
| `Source/Resolve.lean` | S01: name resolution: C3 linearization, scoped lookup, builtins classified against the profile (`Builtin.status`, member and call-option status), the `Resolves` judgment with `resolve_iff` and `Resolves.unique`. |
| `Source/Typing.lean` | S01: type-name elaboration (`Syn.tys`), typing contexts, expression typing as data (`Typed`, `HasType`) and statement typing in a fixed body context (`StmtD`/`ItemsD`, `StmtOK`/`ItemsOK`). |
| `Source/Resolved.lean` | S01: the intrinsic resolved form (`RExpr`/`RStmt` over local types, de Bruijn `Idx`, explicit implicit-conversion evidence) and the elaboration of derivations into it (`Typed.toR`, `StmtD.toR`). |
| `Source/WellFormed.lean` | S01: settings over solc's two pipelines (`Settings.coder`), resolutions, body frames (`Decl.frame?`), and well-formedness of a resolved unit (bases, clashes and overloads, pragmas, declared types, placement, constants, bodies). |
| `Source/TypingExamples.lean` | S01 controls: a derivation per typed expression constructor (`exprCoverage_typed`, `exprCoverage_total`, `stmtCoverage_total`), the counter well formed, C3 on a diamond, refuted ill-typed, `msg.value`, clashing, same-signature, interface-body and unknown-pragma programs. |
| `Source/State.lean` | S04.01: source values `Val` over a declaration `Layout` (ranges, enum bounds and reference locations intrinsic), contents `Data` (recursive aggregates, content-keyed mappings), typed `memory` (`Heap`, copy and alias laws), read-only `Calldata`, `Storage` typed by its layout, defaults. |
| `Source/Observe.lean` | S04.02: ABI words and bytes of values (`Val.word`, `Val.word_int`), selectors, `Panic`/`Error` revert data (`panicData_code`, `panic_distinct`) and `revertOutcome`. |
| `Source/World.lean` | S04.03: instance accounts, the world, transient storage cleared per transaction, transaction configuration, a journal of operations, call frames with journal rollback (`rollback_run`), value transfers. |
| `Source/Units.lean` | G12: source units of a compilation input (`UnitSet`), solc's import path resolution and remappings (`Path.absolute`, `Remapping.apply`), declaration identities (`DeclId`), the file-level scope across imports (`Sees`) and G12's judgment `UnitsOK`. |
| `Source/Overloads.lean` | G12: overload sets across a contract's bases (`InheritedFn`: hidden only by a more derived function with the same parameter types). |
| `Source/ConstCheck.lean` | G13: compile-time constants: purity of constant initializers (`PureS`, `ConstInitsPure`, solc error 8349), solc's typed constant evaluation (`TEval`, `NamedConst`, `ConstVal`), array lengths through it (`ArrayLenOK`) and acyclic constants (`ConstsAcyclic`). |
| `Source/UserOps.lean` | G13: user-defined value types: operator bindings of global `using {f as op} for T` directives (`opBindings`, well formed: `UsingOpsOK`), and the typing of operators, `wrap` and `unwrap` (`UdvtTyped`). |
| `Source/Inheritance.lean` | G14: solc's contract-level checks as relations: overrides (`FnOverrideOK`, `OverridesOK`), required overrides (`MustOverride`), implementations (`ImplOK`), base-constructor arguments (`CtorArgsOK`), modifier invocations (`ModInvOK`), `using for` libraries (`UsingLibOK`), constructor order and callable identity; `InheritanceOK`. |
| `Source/StateExamples.lean` | S04.01–S04.03 controls: nested memory aliasing, an independent memory copy, storage round trips, distinct panic outcomes, transfers, a reverted call undoing its writes. |
| `Frontend/Checked/Token.lean` | G08: the tokens of the checked frontend (`Tok`, keywords, punctuation, Yul keywords), pieces with their exact bytes (`Piece`), located tokens and errors. |
| `Frontend/Checked/Tables.lean` | G08: byte-literal keyword, word and punctuation tables, generated by `scripts/checked-lexer-tables.py`. |
| `Frontend/Checked/Lexer.lean` | G08: the checked lexer over bytes: per-lexeme classification (`classify`, the lexical classes of `SolidityLexer.g4`), maximal-munch splitting with modes, the certificate `Lexed` (`lex_sound`, `lex_reprint`, `lex_locate`, `lex_error_pos`). |
| `Frontend/Checked/LexExamples.lean` | G08 controls: every lexer rule's sample classified (`lexRules_classified`, `lexRules_cover`), tables against spellings, accepted inputs and located refusals. |
| `Frontend/Checked/Print.lean` | G09–G11: the token yield `Syn.yield` of S00's syntax under a layout (attribute orders, string fragments): the grammar derivation `Syn.Derives`. |
| `Frontend/Checked/Prec.lean` | G10/G11: the precedence, associativity and dangling-`else` predicate `Syn.prec`, with refusal where the grammar and solc disagree. |
| `Frontend/Checked/Parser.lean` | G09–G11: the untrusted recursive-descent parser from located tokens to `Syn .unit` and its layout (`parseTokens`), fuel-structural. |
| `Frontend/Checked/Check.lean` | G11: whole-source parsing checked against the yield and precedence (`parse`, `parse_sound`, `parse_derives`). |
| `Frontend/Checked/Render.lean` | G09: canonical token spellings and the source printer `Syn.print`. |
| `Frontend/Checked/TypeExamples.lean` | G09 controls: type-name trees, attribute layouts, recursive declaration references, printing round trips, type and declaration constructor coverage (`declCtors_covered`), refusals. |
| `Frontend/Checked/ExprExamples.lean` | G10 controls: precedence and associativity lemmas over `Syn.precNode`, every operator, expression trees, refused ambiguous forms, expression constructor coverage (`exprCtors_covered`). |
| `Frontend/Checked/Certificate.lean` | G11: G05's parse-stage instance: `Units`, `Parses`, `parseChecker` (`checkUnits_sound`), the producer (`produce_accepted`), `FrontendSpec.ofParse`. |
| `Frontend/Checked/UnitExamples.lean` | G11 controls: statements, definitions and units, every S00 constructor covered (`allCtors_covered`, `profileCtors_covered`), out-of-profile refusals, the G05 checker on a two-unit input. |
| `Source/Value.lean` | S02: run-time values `RVal`, `Halt`, decidable `STy` equality, wrapping (`wrapBits_mem`), checked/unchecked integer and fixed-point arithmetic, shifts, powers, comparisons, value conversions. |
| `Source/Elaborated.lean` | S02 prerequisite: resolved bodies, modifier invocations, initializers and base arguments (`Program`), and `Elaborated` (a resolution carrying its program, every part tied to a derivation). |
| `Source/Unique.lean` | Q1: uniqueness of meaning (`Typed.unique`, `StmtD.unique`, `ItemsD.unique`, `RBody.ofDerivation_unique`), solc's literal typing (`Typed.constShape`), `0.length` refused, literal-gap controls. |
| `Source/Machine.lean` | S02: the logical storage layout (`Env.cellTy`), `Locals`, the machine state `St`, frames `Fr`, `Glob`, the monad `EM`, storage/memory reads, writes, copies and deletion. |
| `Source/Eval.lean` | S02/S03: the interpreter of resolved expressions, lvalues, statements, modifiers, internal and external calls, dispatch and environment moves, at a horizon. |
| `Source/Semantics.lean` | S03.02/S03.04/S04.04/S05: constructor order per pipeline, deployment, transactions, least-horizon completion (`firstDone_mono`), sessions as G01 prefixes (`sessionAt_consistent`, `sourceLang`). |
| `Source/Ledger.lean` | S05: the semantic status of every expression and statement constructor against S01's coverage (`semExprCoverage_of_typed`, `semExprGaps_spec`). |
| `Source/SemanticsExamples.lean` | S02–S05 controls: overflow, division by zero, evaluation order per pipeline, short circuit, two placeholders, constructor order, revert rollback of storage and transient storage, a reentrant callback. |
| `Source/Infer.lean` | S02 item 1b: type inference computing derivations (`Typed.infer`, `StmtD.infer`, `ItemsD.infer`), sound by construction. |
| `Source/Elaborate.lean` | S02 item 1b: the elaborator `elaborate : Resolution → Option Elaborated` (bodies, modifiers, initializers, constants, base arguments), coherent by construction. |
| `Source/ElaborateExamples.lean` | Controls of the elaborator: a unit with inheritance, a base argument, a modifier and an initializer elaborated from syntax and run. |
| `Source/Horizon.lean` | S05 item 2: the interpreter is monotone in its horizon (`belowAt`, horizon independence `runTx_stable`, `firstDone_runTx`), only appends events (`growAt`), and a timed-out run's events are a prefix of a larger horizon's (`approxAt`, `runTx_events_mono`). |
| `Source/HorizonDeploy.lean` | S05 item 2: a deployment not completed at a horizon shows a prefix of its events at every larger one (`deploy_events_mono`). |
| `Source/Session.lean`, `Source/SessionExamples.lean` | Sessions showing the events of unfinished transactions (`sessionAtEvents`, `sourceLangEvents`), consistent, and their controls. |
| `Source/Hoare.lean` | V04b: Hoare triples over the interpreter monad `EM` (`Hoare`, `NotStuck`), with a rule per combinator (`bind`, `sub'`, `attempt'`, `lift'`, …). |
| `Source/WellTyped.lean` | V04b preservation: references agree with the heap typing and the layout (`Val.RefOK`, `DataOK`, `RValOK`, `LocalsOK`, `HeapOK`, `StOK`, `EMOK`); memory operations keep it (`alloc_ok`, `allocRef_ok`, `heapPut_ok`, `heapAt_ok`). |
| `Source/Decode.lean` | FU2-B `abi.decode`: the decoder keeps `MemOK` and is never stuck at the types it reads (`decodeArg_ok`, `decodeArgs_ok`, `abiDecodeData_ok`); the evaluation arm relative to its data (`evalE_abiDecode_ok`, `evalE_abiDecode_lit_ok`). |
| `Source/DecodeExamples.lean` | FU2-B controls: `abi.decode` elaborated from syntax and run, malformed payloads reverting as solc's decoder (empty revert, Panic `0x41`), named refusals. |
| `Source/Calldata.lean` | FU2-B `calldata` references: entry decoding, element/byte reads, slices and copies are never stuck and keep `MemOK` (`decodeArgs_entry_ok`, `readLoc_cd_ok`, `sliceR_ok`, `cdToMem_ok`); the `x.length`, `x[j]`, `x[s:e]` arms on locals (`evalE_cdLength_ok`, `evalE_cdIndex_ok`, `evalE_cdSlice_ok`). |
| `Source/CalldataExamples.lean` | FU2-B controls: `calldata` parameters elaborated and run, elements validated at the read, entry checks, slices and copies reverting as solc's. |
| `Source/InlineExamples.lean` | FU2-B controls: inline arrays elaborated and run at solc's common type, an index past the length panicking, refusals without a common type or with reference elements; `new T[](n)`, `new bytes(n)`, `new string(n)` zeroed, panicking `0x41` at `2^64`. |
| `Source/TryExamples.lean` | FU2-B controls: `try`/`catch` between two deployed contracts, success with returns bound, a caught revert (plain and `bytes memory` clauses), no code at the target not caught. |
| `Source/ValueProgress.lean` | V04b progress, value layer: `binVal`/`cmpVal`/`shiftVal`/`unaryVal`/`incDecVal`/`convVal`/`constVal` are never stuck in their domains (`NotStuckR`); every elaborated term is in them (`RExpr.OpsOK`, `Typed.toR_opsOK`, `Elaborated.body_opsOK`). |
| `Source/ExprProgress.lean` | V04b progress, expressions: `NS`/`NSP`; the value fragment (`RExpr.ValFrag`, `RExpr.MapRef`, `RLVal.IsSimple`: locals, value-type state variables, mappings) is never stuck (`exprNSAt`, `evalE_valFrag_ns`; `readAt_ns`, `writeAt_ns`, `keyStep_nsp`, `convRV_ns`); the fragment parameterized by allowed callees (`RExpr.ValFragC`, `CallNS`, `exprNSAt_step`) |
| `Source/StmtProgress.lean` | V04b progress, statements: the statement value fragment in a body frame is never stuck (`stmtNSAt`, `stmtNS`, `itemsNS`, `ItemsD.toR_ns`; `RStmt.ext`, `Locals.restore_ok`, `BodyFrame`); calls inside expressions by the joint induction `FragAt`/`fragAt` under `CallsOK` (`evalE_callFrag_ns`); elaborated frames (`Elaborated.body_locals`, `Glob.dispatchFn_params`) |
| `Source/SoundnessExamples.lean` | V04b controls: value-layer witnesses and stuck negative controls; fragment witnesses evaluated in the kernel (a loop, a storage update), an unframed `return` stuck; call witnesses (`callF_ns`, `recF_ns`, `callF_badFrame_stuck`) |

## Precompiles

| Module | What it defines |
|---|---|
| `Precompiles/Field.lean` | Shared arithmetic of the computed precompiles: `buffer_read` (`readPad`, `readNat`), big-endian words, `powMod`, `FieldOps` with `Fin p` and `Fp2 p`, affine short Weierstrass curves (`Curve`, `Pt`), `chunks`, `ofHex`, `mapM_eq_none_iff` ([precompiles.md](feature-work/precompiles.md)). |
| `Precompiles/Pairing.lean` | `Fp12` and the optimal ate pairing check generic over BN254 and BLS12-381 (`PairingCurve.check`). |
| `Precompiles/Basic.lean` | `ECRECOVER`, `SHA256`, `RIPEMD160`, `IDENTITY` (0x01–0x04) over the approved `Hashes`/`Secp256k1` functions, with Osaka gas, never failing; length-monotonic gas. |
| `Precompiles/Modexp.lean` | `MODEXP` (0x05) with EIP-7823 bounds and EIP-7883 gas; `modexp_eq_none_iff`, `modexp_length`. |
| `Precompiles/Bn254.lean` | BN254 `ECADD`, `ECMUL`, pairing check (0x06–0x08); decoding laws (`decodeG1_eq_none_iff`) and failure cases. |
| `Precompiles/Blake2f.lean` | `BLAKE2F` (0x09), EIP-152's compression on `UInt64`; `blake2f_eq_none_iff`. |
| `Precompiles/P256.lean` | `P256VERIFY` (0x100), EIP-7951's ECDSA check on secp256r1, never failing. |
| `Precompiles/Bls12381Iso.lean` | RFC 9380's BLS12-381 isogeny constants and `G2`'s effective cofactor, as data. |
| `Precompiles/Bls12381.lean` | EIP-2537 (0x0b–0x11): `G1`/`G2` addition and MSM, pairing check, SSWU maps; failure laws and MSM gas monotonicity. |
| `Precompiles/PointEval.lean` | EIP-4844 point evaluation (0x0a): zcash `G1` decompression, the trusted setup's `[τ]G₂`, the KZG check. |
| `Precompiles/Osaka.lean` | G06's `Fixed` instantiated (`Osaka.fixed`): dispatch `run`/`gas` over `Precompile`, failure exactly on `Malformed` input (`run_eq_none_iff`), length-monotonic gas (`gas_mono`), the stipend facts. |
| `Stipend/Rule.lean` | The stipend cost rule (G06 Q3): which `receive`/fallback bodies fit 2300 gas under cold access costs (`Stipend.rule`, `progCost`, `fits_iff`), its examples and non-examples, and the compiler obligation `Obligation` (G43) ([stipend-cost-rule.md](feature-work/stipend-cost-rule.md)). |

## The solkey corpus

`SolidityCorpus` (`lake build SolidityCorpus`): each function of solkey's `.sol`
suites, generated by `scripts/solkey-port.mjs` into `Solidity/Corpus/`, stated
at the contract's initial store and decided by the kernel; `TestSuite`'s rows
are read off `TestSuite/Report.lean` and its derived obligations restated as
corollaries.  `./scripts/check-corpus.sh` checks the verdicts against
`tests/solkey/expected.tsv`, `./scripts/check-testsuite.sh` audits the
TestSuite rows without Lean; `docs/corpus-parity.md` is the scoreboard.

| Module | What it defines |
|---|---|
| `Corpus/Basic.lean` | The corpus's obligation `Diamond σ P` over the typed run (`P : Prog C [] Γ'`, `Runs`: the typed reading of `⟨ P ⟩ true` at `σ`), `corpus_decide` (`runOk`), `outcome` (how the typed run ends). |
| `Corpus/Imported.lean` | `testSuite_agrees` (the hand-written `TestSuite`'s roots are the imported one's); a scoped `⊢` obligation of no parameters at the initial state, with its source input premise proved before the program's typed run (`Obligation.Diamond`/`Box`, `runs_of_proved`, `diamond_of_proved`, `box_of_proved`). |
| `Corpus/TestSuite.lean` | Generated: each derived `TestSuite` obligation with no parameters at `Solkey.TestSuite.initState`. |
| `Corpus/SolcArrays.lean`, `Corpus/SolcControlFlow.lean`, `Corpus/SolcExpressions.lean`, `Corpus/SolcMappings.lean`, `Corpus/SolcMemory.lean`, `Corpus/SolcStructs.lean` | Generated: solkey's `solc/*.sol` suites, one `Diamond (State.init C) sol[C]{ body }` per function (`Corpus/Basic.lean`). |

## The solc front end

`SolkeyTestSuite` (`lake build SolkeyTestSuite`): solkey's `TestSuite.sol` from
the AST the pinned soljson writes (`scripts/solc-ast.mjs` makes
`tests/solc/TestSuite.ast.json`; `./scripts/check-solc-ast.sh` re-derives and
diffs it).  `docs/testsuite-proofs.md` has the counts and timings.

| Module | What it defines |
|---|---|
| `Frontend/SolcJson.lean` | solc's JSON AST (`Lean.Json`) printed as `sol` text per function: `readContract`, `Gap`, `Tag`; a struct the table's when its members are, else the contract's own, declared (`readStructs`, `ownStructMembers`), struct constructors printed; the functions called by name as `contract!` members, callees first; the constructor (`ctor`, a root of the calls) and the state variables' initializers (`initMembers`, `initGap`).  Split by topic into the pieces below (`docs/parallelism.md`), this module the umbrella. |
| `Frontend/SolcJson/Body.lean` | What is left out, reading the JSON, types, expressions and statements; declaration-ID metadata and lazy selector receiver helpers. |
| `Frontend/SolcJson/Contract.lean` | JSON contract/dependency reading; callable-only source/native finite-dispatch signatures with source identity; inherited empty-data receive/payable fallback registration with direct missing-identity refusal; inherited constructor initialization follows verified legacy/viaIR provenance. |
| `Frontend/SolcJson/Getters.lean` | Source ABI identities and record-paired, nonempty getter plans, lowered through ordinary function members; primitive/UDVT/enum/contract values, nominal mapping keys, conservative nominal dispatch signatures, reference/function-result refusal and empty-data array bounds. |
| `Frontend/SourceUnits.lean` | Original compilation-unit ASTs, per-source provenance and checked compiler import identities; explicit specification/mixed-legacy-ABI refusals. |
| `Frontend/Static/Closure.lean` | G12–G14: reachability by saturation over a finite graph (`iter_sound`, `iter_complete`), shared by the static checkers. |
| `Frontend/Static/Units.lean` | G12: the import resolver `UnitSet.globals` (sound and complete for `Sees`, through cycles), the program check `UnitSet.unitsOK` (`unitsOK_iff`), identities (`Sees.decl_exists`) and agreement with S01's lookup (`globals_lookup`). |
| `Frontend/Static/Overloads.lean` | G12: the inherited overload set `Env.inheritedFns` (`mem_inheritedFns`, extends S01's lookup) and overload selection (`selectOverload`). |
| `Frontend/Static/UnitsExamples.lean` | G12 controls: an import cycle, a diamond (one identity), a clash, aliases, a missing unit, remappings, shadowing, an ambiguous overload and overloads across bases. |
| `Frontend/Static/Infer.lean` | G13: type inference `infer` over every category, returning S01 derivations (`Typed`, `LVal`, `TypedArgs`, `TypedSlots`): sound by construction. |
| `Frontend/Static/InferComplete.lean` | G13: completeness of `infer` for every S01 expression rule (`typed_complete`, `lval_complete`, `args_complete`, `slots_complete`). |
| `Frontend/Static/InferStmt.lean` | G13: statement and block checking `inferS`, returning `StmtD`/`ItemsD` derivations. |
| `Frontend/Static/InferStmtComplete.lean` | G13: completeness of `inferS` (`stmt_complete`, `items_complete`); `stmtOK_iff`, `itemsOK_iff`. |
| `Frontend/Static/WellFormedCheck.lean` | G13: `wellFormedB` decides S01's `WellFormed` (`wellFormed_iff`), every body and initializer typed by the checkers. |
| `Frontend/Static/Consts.lean` | G13: purity checker (`pureB_iff`, `constInitsB_iff`), typed constant evaluation (`tevalP`, `nconstP`; `teval_complete`, `nconst_complete`), `evalConst`, `arrayLenB`, the cycle check (`acyclic_iff`). |
| `Frontend/Static/UserOps.lean` | G13: the user-defined value type checker `udvtTys` (`udvt_iff`) and the binding check (`usingOpsB_iff`). |
| `Frontend/Static/Standalone.lean` | G13, backfill item 5: the type a constant stands alone at (`STy.standalone`: a fractional constant at its mobile fixed-point type), the relations `StandaloneTyped`/`AbiArgTyped` decided by `standaloneTys` (`standalone_iff`, `abiArg_iff`), and the value from `Fx.mobileConst?`/`Fx.abiConst?` in `Language/FixedPoint/Literals.lean` (`standalone_value`). |
| `Frontend/Static/InferExamples.lean` | G13 controls: S01's examples checked by computation, ill-typed and ill-formed refusals, named constants, a cyclic constant, a non-compile-time constant, UDVT operators and conversions. |
| `Frontend/Static/Inheritance.lean` | G14: checkers deciding each inheritance relation (`fnOverrideB_iff` … `usingLibB_iff`, `inheritance_iff`) and callable identity (`callable_unique`). |
| `Frontend/Static/C3.lean` | G14: the C3 specification `C3Lin`, the merge laws and soundness of S01's `Env.linearize` (`linearize_sound`); constructor order (`ctorOrder_spec`). |
| `Frontend/Static/InheritanceExamples.lean` | G14 controls transcribing solc 0.8.38's `inheritance/` and `nameAndTypeResolution/` syntax tests, with their verdicts. |
| `Frontend/Static/Certificates.lean` | The static checkers as G05 `Checker` values (`unitsChecker`, `metadataChecker`, `strictMetadataChecker`, `inheritanceChecker`) and the program's library declarations. |
| `Frontend/Source.lean` | Compiler/pragma/settings validation, derived compilation-mode observations, static compiler-code artifacts and an admission result certifying its extracted feature list. |
| `Frontend/SolcJson/Admission.lean` | Bounded AST scope classification and declaration-header closure over resolved execution roots; structured policy, upstream and implementation diagnostics. |
| `Examples/Tactics/SourceUnitsImport.lean` | Both pinned compilers and pipelines: module calls, lexical error aliases, exact entry error bytes, receiver/condition effects and nonvacuity controls. |
| `Examples/Tactics/SourceUnitsMetadata.lean` | Missing, duplicate and foreign source metadata, lexical-specification refusal and real compiler remapping controls. |
| `Examples/Tactics/SourceLiteralsImport.lean` | Pinned literal, byte, address and constant execution, universal remainder correctness and a false-property counterexample. |
| `Examples/Tactics/SourceIdentifiersImport.lean` | Raw source names, builtin shadowing, address fields and specifications with dollar names. |
| `Examples/Tactics/SourceAdmissionImport.lean` | Scope roots, metadata-only references, ABI modes, malformed AST metadata and provenance regressions. |
| `Examples/Tactics/OperatorAliasesImport.lean` | Modern resolved UDVT aliases: all unary/binary signatures, nominal validation, lazy branches, both pipeline schedules, callee arithmetic scope and concrete false properties. |
| `Examples/Tactics/DeclarationMetadata.lean` | Compiled declaration metadata: interface IDs, receiver evaluation/laziness and rejection of malformed or anonymous selectors. |
| `Examples/Tactics/PublicGetters.lean` | Imported primitive scalar/mapping/array getters, interface overrides, struct omissions, ABI identities, bounds/helper freshness; box safety plus concrete success/panic controls at self address 7, with external-try diamond automation still limited. |
| `Frontend/Testing.lean` | `#solkey_test`: kernel-checked function and specification tests with checkpointed JSON results, imported-closure axiom-policy memoization and opt-in phase timers ([measurements](browser-simple-benchmarks.md)); `scripts/test-solidity.mjs` compiles and tests `.sol` files/directories ([solidity-testing.md](solidity-testing.md)). |
| `Frontend/Import.lean` | `solc_import "f.json" hash 0x… as N renaming A => B`: `N : Contract`, `N.f : Prog N Γ Γ'` per function (read in its parameters' context, quoted hash-consed and checked by the kernel), `N.constructor : Prog N Γ Γ'` (a deployment, `constructor(x̄);`), `N.report : List ImportRow`; native evaluation of the imported contract and the legacy `EvalOrder` override (two `evalExpr` sites), with the selected order quoted back as data. Large contract tables compile in list segments, with a kernel-checked equality to the original literal. |
| `Frontend/WorldImport.lean` | Strict hash-checked source creation/creation-try and linked mutation commands with explicit premises, executed operation certificates and funded reachability bridges. |
| `Solkey/TestSuite.lean` | `Solkey.TestSuite`, its source function rows and pinned import report (counts in the module and `TestSuite/Report.lean`). |
| `Solkey/ComputedRevert.lean` | Pinned solc computed reasons/raw rethrows, with public self-call bodies retained; rejected assembly shapes and word-array buffers. |
| `Solkey/Constructors.lean` | solkey's `Counter.sol` and `EtherWallet.sol` imported (`tests/solc/Counter.ast.json`, `tests/solc/EtherWallet.ast.json`): their constructors and initializers, and `EtherWallet`'s constructor obligation derived; `tests/solc/CtorGap.sol` and `tests/solc/InitGap.sol` pin what the import leaves out. |
| `Solkey/ScopedFlip.lean` | `tests/solc/ScopedFlip.sol` (one box function) through the TestSuite's commands, pinned: `solc_scoped_problems … decl`, `#solkey_problem`, `#solkey_derive?` and `#solkey_obligations`. |
| `Frontend/Problems.lean` | `obligation` (a function's statement written on its program `N.f`: `∀x̄. wt(storage) → [m] N.f`, a constructor's from `Problem.deployUpd`); `solc_scoped_problems N [decl]` (`N.f.problem : Fml N []` per program, the entries of one kernel-checked table `N.problems`), `#solkey_problem`, `#solkey_scan` (`N.f.problem`, or the statement `obligation` writes), `#solkey_derive?` (`scopedSearch` on `⊢ N.f.problem`; the replays to paste, when they fit `maxHeartbeats`), `#solkey_obligations` (derived, checked against `⊢ N.f.problem` and Lean's three axioms / pending). |
| `TestSuite/Problems.lean` | Two of `Solkey.TestSuite`'s obligations pinned in solkey's syntax (`#solkey_problem`), `initState_wt`. |
| `TestSuite/ScopedProblems.lean` | The pinned scoped obligations, `Solkey.TestSuite.f.problem : Fml Solkey.TestSuite []`, by `solc_scoped_problems … decl` (entries of one table, declared, not compiled), each on its program `Solkey.TestSuite.f`; what the `Derived` modules prove. |
| `TestSuite/Derived1.lean`, `TestSuite/Derived2.lean`, `TestSuite/Derived3.lean`, `TestSuite/Derived4.lean`, `TestSuite/Derived5.lean`, `TestSuite/Derived6.lean`, `TestSuite/Derived7.lean`, `TestSuite/Derived8.lean`, `TestSuite/Derived9.lean`, `TestSuite/Derived10.lean`, `TestSuite/Derived11.lean`, `TestSuite/Derived12.lean` | `Solkey.TestSuite.f.proved : ⊢ Solkey.TestSuite.f.problem`, the scoped sequent (`open scoped Solidity.Scoped`, `Calculus/ProvesDerive.lean`), 40, 40, 40, 40, 40, 37, 41, 23, 40, 40, 11 and 20 (412 in all), by `sol_prove` and explicit leaf tactics; 7 and 8 are what pushes, pops and storage copies added, 9 to 11 what memory added, 12 what copies between memory and storage added. |
| `TestSuite/Derived13.lean` | `testArrayCopyClearsOldElements`, by `sol_prove`; four resurrection claims, upstream's `solc/StorageDangling.sol`, are proved in `Examples/Tactics/StorageDangling.lean`. |
| `TestSuite/Derived14.lean` | The twenty obligations added at solkey `1b4341a303` (`send`, internal calls, `return`, tuples), each by `sol_prove`; ground splits are pruned (`Seq.refuteS`, closed by `Proves.closeFalse`). |
| `TestSuite/Derived15.lean` | 53 migration derivations for returns, overloads, modifiers, helpers, storage shapes, fresh arrays and push operands; the report counts only kernel-checked declarations. |
| `TestSuite/Derived16.lean` | Native push compound, DIV/MOD, increment and signed three-push derivations; demanded primitive reads close the signed case within the existing bounds. |
| `Calculus/MemoryLoopReplay.lean` | Typed symbolic aliases and kernel-checked one-layer equations for the unchanged 1024-step walk; untrusted native proposals, exact callback/result accounting, fail-closed checkpoints and empty-residue-only refusal boundary. |
| `TestSuite/MemoryLoopReplay/*.lean`, `TestSuite/Derived17MemoryLoop.lean` | Twenty shared replay stages for the original nested-return theorem; incomplete, missing-state, wrong-root and residual-leaf refusal controls. |
| `TestSuite/Derived17.lean` | Unannotated upstream loop derivations, including storage-dependent bounds, break/continue and nested returns; fixed-budget kernel-checked replay. |
| `TestSuite/Derived18.lean` | The imported native modular arithmetic obligation, proved with explicit Lean-only assignment rules. |
| `TestSuite/Derived19.lean` | Obligations new at solkey `473da8944c`: `&`'s operand order, a `continue` under an invariant, modifier continuations, the four dangling-storage claims (a length-only push) and shifts, by `sol_prove`. |
| `TestSuite/Derived20.lean` | Obligations whose leaves close each but not together in one declaration's heartbeats, and the quantified-prefix invariant: `sol_auto_theorem` (`Calculus/Closers.lean`). |
| `TestSuite/Derived21.lean` | The three `memoryIndexWrite*` obligations: memory read through a storage copy below its root, by `sol_prove`. |
| `TestSuite/Refuted.lean` | The obligations false in the model, `Solkey.TestSuite.f.refuted : ¬ (⊢ Solkey.TestSuite.f.problem)`, each by `sol_refute` (a certified counterexample, `Tools/Counterexample.lean`). |
| `Calculus/KeyValues.lean` | Recursive exclusion of native modular arithmetic from upstream-only programs, including path indices; weakening preserves the check. |
| `Examples/Tactics/ModularArithmetic.lean` | Kernel-checked modular arithmetic, intermediate overflow, dynamic zero, short circuiting, argument effects, function shadowing and fragment boundaries. |
| `Examples/Tactics/EvaluationOrder.lean` | Legacy and via-IR expression schedules: captures, inlined calls, modular arguments, failure priority, conditional evaluation and import configuration. |
| `Examples/Tactics/AuditRepairs.lean` | Strict Spec equations and proof bridges; constant arithmetic, unchecked parentheses, argument and receiver capture regressions. |
| `Examples/Tactics/RunnerAudit.lean` | Imported bare and specified external-call rows retain the callback reading and cannot prove storage preservation. |
| `TestSuite/Report.lean` | The pinned count: derived, pending (named), and rows without statements (excluded, skipped, unsupported and internal helpers). |
| `TestSuite/Suggestions.lean` | `#solkey_derive?` suggestions pinned by name, off the `Derived` modules' import path; `returnEarly` and `tupleReturnDiscardsComponents` pinned as derived since ground splits are pruned. |

## Exact verification

| Module | What it defines |
|---|---|
| `Calculus/DeclarationFacts.lean` | Generated scalar, struct and mapping ranges certified by total declaration reads (`declaration_facts[C]`, `sol_decl_facts`). |
| `Calculus/ExactReduction.lean` | Structurally agreeing positive/negative reductions, their per-state equivalence and exact read-elimination transport. |
| `Calculus/TheoryRead.lean` | An array element as a formula reads it (every slot, the default past them) pushed in exactly: `Tm.toLT`, `orElse(find(S, q[i]), 0)` over the storage the updates left, `Tm.toLT_sound` (M7 step 6). |
| `Calculus/ExactUpdates.lean` | Constructive landing states without memory locals, exact push-free update guards, compositional scoped reductions and original-source certificates (`sourceReduction?`); an equation `directional?` refuses taken as the Theory reads it (`ExactAt.eqT?`). |
| `Calculus/ExactRun.lean` | Exact update/run agreement with termination and exhaustion conditions; statement certificates for local/storage assignment, deletion and primitive declarations. |
| `Calculus/ProgramReduction.lean` | Exact bounded normalization of supported source programs, primitive-result calls, scope frames and primitive binders; control-flow failure/panic distinctions and original-program arithmetic proofs/counterstates (`programReduction?`). |
| `Calculus/ReadRealization.lean` | Alias-consistent finite typed reads realized by actual saves (`Assignment.realizable_iff`). |
| `Calculus/ReadQuery.lean` | Finite computations with read-dependent locations and trace-preserving reconstruction (`Query.run_realize`). |
| `Calculus/ReadTrace.lean` | Typed occurrence replay, actual-length composition, alias-consistent storage realization and bounded/no-loss trace witnesses. |
| `Calculus/QueryReduction.lean` | Recognition of existing typed leaf expressions and universal-prefix formulas, source-to-query equivalence (`ReadReduction.valid_iff`) and certified counterstates from supplied assignments. |
| `Calculus/QueryReadBounds.lean` | Syntax-derived bounds for every typed leaf-query occurrence stream and selected branch. |
| `Calculus/TraceReduction.lean` | Original-source validity over consistent dependent-read traces and actual counterstates from supplied finite occurrence values. |
| `Calculus/QueryArithmetic.lean` | Exact finite typed occurrence layouts, complete arithmetic validity and actual counterstore extraction from alias-aware query certificates. |
| `Calculus/SymbolicPaths.lean` | Bounded recognition of existing rooted path terms as the closer's symbolic paths, preserving optional key/path evaluation exactly. |
| `Calculus/AliasConstraints.lean` | Existing path-comparison trees compiled to alias formulas/arithmetic, with successful-path side conditions and exact finite read-matrix consistency. |
| `Calculus/PathGuards.lean` | Exact optional definedness guards and declaration proofs for root/field/mapping paths, retaining failed keys. |
| `Calculus/FlattenReads.lean` | Shared-budget source term/formula flattening into fresh typed read occurrences, with local evaluation equivalence and atom-bound agreement. |
| `Calculus/ReadFree.lean` | Executable zero-read query certificates proving term/formula storage independence. |
| `Calculus/OccurrenceLayout.lean` | Checked causal fresh slots, actual-state filling, active assignment extraction and consistent-storage reconstruction. |
| `Calculus/ReadFlattening.lean` | Exact source-leaf validity over consistent fresh occurrences, with actual original-leaf counterstates. |
| `Calculus/ActiveReadConstraints.lean` | Guarded symbolic alias formulas exactly equivalent to active occurrence consistency, including failed paths. |
| `Calculus/LeafArithmetic.lean` | Automatic typed input collection and complete arithmetic decisions/counterstates for flattened leaves, with a pair-count budget. |
| `Calculus/LeafInputs.lean` | Term/formula agreement at exactly their typed atom inputs and universal-prefix assignment agreement. |
| `Calculus/CondLift.lean` | The elimination's conditionals (`kite`, `ite`) folded where trivial (`LTm.condSimp`, `LFml.condSimp_holds`) and lifted out of equations into three-way case splits preserving definedness (`LTm.condSplit`, `CondGuard.split`, `LFml.liftCond`, `LFml.liftCond_holds`); recursor forms with `match` twins. Also lifted to the formula's top (`LFml.findCond`, `LFml.liftTop`, `LFml.liftTop_holds`: the guard out of negations and premises, KeY's `ifthenelse_split`), `LFml.decideLift`'s last try. |
| `Calculus/DependentReduction.lean` | Automatic dependent-read original-source proofs/counterstates through exact normalization, folded conditionals, consequent quantifiers prenexed (`LFml.prenex`), lifted conditionals (lifted first within a node budget: `LFml.liftCondB`, `liftBudget`, `LFml.liftCondB_eq`), covered universal parameters and guarded arithmetic. |
| `Calculus/BitArith.lean` | The identities the cut writes literal operators through: `x & c` bit by bit, `\|` and `^` through `&`, `*%` by doubling, truncating division by its sign cases (M7). |
| `Calculus/QuotientCut.lean` | Operators cut out of a leaf into fresh atoms (M7): `LFml.cutAll` replaces `int(·)`, `uint(·)`, `/`, `%`, `>>`, `<<`, low masks, `+%`, `-%` by atoms defined in the antecedent (`LFml.defn`, one atom a node by `quotAtom`), and writes `&`, `\|`, `^`, `*%`, `**` by a literal and `/`, `%` at `int` through them (`bandGo`, `mulWGo`, `litCutR`), exact in a universal reading (`LFml.cutAll_valid_iff`, `LFml.holds_cutAll`; `Defs.Good`, `CutTm.Good`). |
| `Calculus/CutTotality.lean` | The cut maps the source fragment into the one flattening and the compilers take (`LTm.cut_src`, `LFml.cutAll_src`), and `flattenedArithmetic?_isSome` on cut leaves. |
| `Calculus/LeafTotality.lean` | The arithmetic compiler never refuses its fragment (M4): `LTm.Num`, `LTm.BoolV`, `LFml.Arith`; widths (`Affine.W`, `CheckedScalar.W`, `CheckedBool.W`); `CheckedValue.ofTermFuel?_good`, `BooleanValue.ofTermFuel?_good`, `StorageEncoding.ofFormula?_good`, `leafArithmetic?_isSome`. |
| `Calculus/FlattenTotality.lean` | Read flattening never refuses its fragment (M4): the source fragment `LTm.Src`/`LFml.Src`, path views and guards (`SPath.viewFuel?_PathF`, `TSegs.guardFuel?_FK`), occurrence layouts (`Laid`, `occurrenceLayout?_isSome`), alias constraints in the arithmetic fragment, `flattenFuel?_Src`, `flattenFormulaFuel?_Src`, `flattenedArithmetic?_isSome`. |
| `Calculus/LiftTotality.lean` | Lifting removes every conditional of the fragment (M4): splits of `LTm.Src true` terms (`LTm.Src.split`, `LTm.Src.noCond`), the weight `LFml.condMax`, `LFml.liftCond_src`, lifting adds no atom (`LFml.liftCond_params`), and folding and lifting keep a leaf with no conditional (`LFml.liftCond_condSimp_of_conds`). |
| `Calculus/AutoComplete.lean` | The fragment of the automatic decision and its completeness (M4): the recognizers `LTm.src?`/`LFml.src?` (complete: `LTm.src?_complete`), the eliminated leaf `leafOf?`, `Fml.inExact`, `Fml.exactBounded`, `dependentReduction?_isSome`, `auto_complete`, `auto_countermodel`. |
| `Calculus/Prenex.lean` | A universal quantifier moved out of an implication's consequent, where the antecedent reads no atom of it (`LFml.prenex`, `LFml.prenex_holds`; M7), ahead of the universal prefix. |
| `Calculus/QuantArithmetic.lean` | Presburger subformulas compiled to `QFml` (M7, step 4): free parameters (`LFml.freeParams`, `LFml.holds_agree_free`), the representation of a valuation at positions (`Rep`), equations by the shared checked compiler, read-free (`eqn_correct`), binders at their variable's position (`QArith`, `QArith.compile`), and quantified subformulas abstracted to fresh Boolean atoms (`LFml.abstractQ`, `LFml.abstractQ_holds`). |
| `Calculus/QuantHoist.lean` | Preparations of a quantified leaf's body (`Prep`: valid where the body is, a refuting state of its own, `Prep.valid_iff`); reads of total paths at no bound variable hoisted out of the body's quantifiers to fresh atoms with their definitions in front (`LTm.hoist`, `LFml.hoistOut`, `hoistPrep`, `hoistPrep_valid_iff`; M7 step 4). |
| `Calculus/QuantExpand.lean` | Two exact preparations of a quantified body (M7, features 16 and 17): `bool` quantifiers expanded into their two instances (`LTm.substB`, `LFml.expandB`, `boolPrep`), positive quantifiers outside every negative one dropped to fresh atoms, one at a time (`LFml.ren`, `LFml.holds_ren`, `LFml.dropOne`, `LFml.dropOne_spec`, `dropPrep`); `expandPrep`, `expandPrep_valid_iff`. |
| `Calculus/FloorRegion.lean` | Floors and regions over an ordered key type (M7, features 18 and 20): `OrdK` (`uint`, `int`: values integers in `[lo, hi)`, the comparisons and the checked successor read on them), the floor in a list with a base (`floorIn`), the index set with its strict operands and range (`ApfSet`), its last interval read at the top (`lastIn`, `lastK`), the breakpoints between which it is constant (`ApfSet.breaks`, `dedupInt`, `lastIn_breaks`); a run of entries region by region, a region holding the default left out (`regionKeys`, `nextIn`, `regionList`), a read finding its key's last breakpoint's region (`lookupBy_regionList`), so a refutation's `2^255` keys are a term proved by region reasoning, not evaluated. |
| `Calculus/ArrayProperty.lean` | The array property fragment (M7 step 5, features 18 to 21 and 32): one pass per key type (`OrdK`, `apfPrepK`; `apfPrep` the `uint` pass then the `int` one), negative blocks of that type over roots read along their own paths (`ApfTm.rroot`, `rkey`, `rfield`, `rword`) and, at a `uint` pass, over the starting ledger (`ApfTm.net`, `ARoots`), every key of the pass's type along a root's mappings floored (`FloorShape.of`), instantiated at the index set (its base and, for `int` and the ledger, its largest value; `apfKeys`), each term once (`LFml.inst`, `LTm.subst1`, `LFml.apfInst`, `dedupKeys`; sound: `LFml.apfInst_sound`); exact on the fragment (`ApfFml.apfInst_refute`, Bradley–Manna–Sipma): each rebuilt root holds at a floored key what its floor in the index set held, the value under it copied, listed region by region (`FloorShape.rebuild`, `FloorShape.rebuild_word`, `entryAt_regionList`, `SRef.floorRef`, `MRoot.surgery`, `surgeryAll`), the ledger likewise (`netRebuild`, `NetFloor`, `NetRel.eval`; `apfState`); terms read alike (`ApfTm.eval_trel`, `ApfTm.eval_rel`), guards monotone, a ground operand that may halt among them (`GuardP.mono`, `GuardP.defined`), the block by its instance at the floors (`LFml.block_refute`); the checks computed (`relB`, `guardB`, `guardTot`, `bodyB`, `topB` and their `_sound`); `apfPrep`, `apfPrep_valid_iff` (`Prep.valid_iff` for it).; feature 32: dynamic arrays of primitives floored slot by slot (`FloorShape.arr`, `arr_rebuild_slot`, `arr_rebuild_len`, `ArrOK`, `FloorShape.rebuild_find`), extra index terms (`apfPrepWith`), index values by `LTm.evalC`. |
| `Calculus/QuantReduction.lean` | Leaves whose quantifiers past the prefix are Presburger: the front of `dependentReduction?`, the body prepared (`bodyPrep`: `expandPrep`, `apfPrep`, then `hoistPrep`), the abstracted body cut, flattened and compiled (the cut keeps its atoms, `LFml.atomBound_le_cutAll`), the definitions `bⱼ ↔ Qⱼ` (`QDefs`), one quantified sentence (`QuantReduction.sentenceF`), its verdict the leaf's (`valid_of_sentence`) and a false assignment a refuting state (`countermodelOf`, `verify`); `quantReduction?`. |
| `Calculus/QuantLedger.lean` | Ledger cut before quantifier abstraction, a shared typed parameter/ledger-code layout, exact quantified sentence and typed countermodels; closed quantified definitions decided with exact truth certificates and the existing ledger arithmetic backend, nonclosed definitions retaining the quantified path. `quantLedgerReduction?` is an additional final fallback, not a characterization of `inTarget`. |
| `Calculus/QuantTotality.lean` | The quantified fragment (`Fml.quantCovered`, `LFml.qfrag`, recognizers `LTm.num?`, `LTm.znum?`, `LTm.bool?`, `eqArith?`) and the totality of the quantified reduction (`QArith.compile_isSome`, `quantReduction?_isSome`). |

| `Calculus/LedgerBlock.lean` | Leaves with blocks over the ledger (M7, feature 21): `ledgerReduction?` with array property instantiation between the prefix and the ledger cut (`ledgerBlockReduction?`, `LedgerBlockReduction.valid_of_body`, `countermodelOfBody`, `verify`: a refutation's ledger pulled back, then rebuilt at the floors); the fragment `Fml.ledgerBlockCovered` and its totality `ledgerBlockReduction?_isSome`. |

| `Calculus/HashSwap.lean` | M7 step 7: a leaf's fresh byte strings (`allocAll` at `uint[]`) read as the words their hashes of one kind return (`Fml.swPrefix`, `Tm.sw`, one-element updates `Upd.sw1?`, value binders in the body); states read alike up to the heap (`SwRel`, `Tm.sw_eval`, `Tm.sw_denote`, `Fml.sw_holds`); the premise weakens a positive binder (`Fml.allPos`, `Fml.posAll`, `Fml.holdsS_of_sw`); a valid swapped leaf valid under the premise (`Fml.swPrefix_validH`). |
| `Calculus/HashReduction.lean` | The strings chosen from a family of one-word strings whose hashes are distinct and nonzero (`famBytes`, `famOK`: by the kernel for four per kind, `famBound`; `famN n` strings past it, their hashes computed, `famOKAll`, `famCheck`, `famCovered`), a heap of them meeting every premise (`FamHeap`, `Fml.hashFree_fam`, `Fml.holdsS_fam`); the swapped leaf at chosen classes (`Fml.swInst`) and its refutations, refutations of the leaf under its premise (`Fml.swInst_refutes`). |
