| `Calculus/HashKinds.lean` | M7 step 7, two kinds: a body read past new binders (`XMap`, `Tm.xk`, `Fml.xk`, one-element updates `Upd.xk1?` with value locals set; `XRelQ`: the extra words their hashes where the premise reads them, `Fml.xk_holdsQ`, `Fml.xk_hashIn`), the second kind's words as `∀ uint` binders linked to the first kind's (`Fml.tk`, `linkE`, `fixE` over a family of `N`); sound (`Fml.tk_validH`, the words chosen by `linkChoice`), refutations at the family (`Fml.tk_refutes`), complete by relabeling the second kind's words (`Fml.tk_complete`, `tkFix_core`); a second kind hashing one string needs no link (feature 27), a string the first kind hashes nowhere is linked to none (`Fml.tkKeep`, `linkE`'s `keep`, feature 29); the keyed roots of the words (`Fml.vRoots`, `kTypedB`). |
| `Calculus/HashKinds3.lean` | M7 step 7, three kinds (feature 28): the third kind's words a layer inside the second's (`Fml.tk3`, `Fml.tkBody3`: `Fml.tkBody` of the body read past the second kind's words, under the second layer's link); sound (`Fml.tk3_validH`), refutations at the family (`Fml.tk3_refutes`, `tkInst_core`), complete by relabeling the second kind's words over the fixed inner layer, then the third's under the outer binders, whose link reads no storage (`Fml.tk3_complete`, `Fml.tk3RL`). |
| `Calculus/HashMove.lean` | M7 step 7, feature 29: a refutation of the two-kind reading moved, the swapped words of the strings the link does not keep set by `linkChoice` to follow their extra words (`Moved`, `setMany`, one level framed at a time by `holds_setLocal_frame`), to one of the reading with every pair linked (`Fml.tkBody_move`, `Fml.tk_move`, `Fml.tk_valid_move`), which `Fml.tk_complete` relabels (`Fml.tk_complete_move`); the fragment `Fml.tkMove`. |
| `Calculus/OrLift.lean` | Fallbacks (`orElse`) lifted out of equations into the cases their left side returns or halts (`LTm.orSplit`, `LFml.orLift`, `LFml.orLift_holds`); guards extracted from equations as conjuncts (`LTm.guarded`, `LFml.gx`, `LFml.gx_holds`). |
| `Calculus/ArrayCut.lean` | Reads of array roots of primitives (`obsW`, `obsH`, `obsL`) cut to index and value atoms with what actual arrays make of them (`LTm.aCut?`, `ARead.cons`); the arrays rebuilt from the atoms (`realize`, `describes_realize`, `frames_realize`), the atoms set to the actual observations (`fill`, `cons_fill`); `LFml.aCut_valid_iff`, `LFml.aCut_refutes` (M7 step 6). |
| `Calculus/ArrayFold.lean` | Guards chained to the right (`LTm.reassoc`) and slot tests a length check of the same index settles replaced by `true` (`LTm.hasFold`, `LFml.hasFold_holds`). |
| `Calculus/ArrayBlock.lean` | Leaves with blocks over storage arrays (M7, feature 32): the arrays' pipeline with a preparation (`blockPrep`: lengths as atoms, `lenPrep`; array property instantiation, `apfPrepWith`), the instances' literal comparisons and Theory slot reads folded (`LFml.litFold`, `LFml.slotFold`), `arrayBlockReduction?`, its fragment `Fml.arrayBlockCovered` and totality `arrayBlockReduction?_isSome`. |
| `Calculus/ArrayReduction.lean` | The decision on leaves that read storage arrays (`arrayReduction?`, `ArrayReduction.verify` with its counterassignment shrunk), its fragment (`Fml.arrayCovered`) and totality (`arrayReduction?_isSome`). |
| `Calculus/MemoryWitness.lean` | Exact enumeration of given-heap memory binders (including unset and aliases), typed primitive-array witness heaps with exact lookup/identity proofs, and partial proof-carrying concrete countermodel checking; no global validity claim. |
| `Calculus/MemSwap.lean` | Fresh memory arrays of primitives (`Fml.allocAll`) cut to atoms (M7 step 6): each binder read as its length's word, each read of it at an index term (`Tm.ixOk`, named before the binder) as a word of its own under what actual arrays make of them (`mxCons`, Ackermann), a read `e < n ? w : ⊥` (`Tm.mx`, `Fml.mx`, `Fml.mxTop`); `MxRel` and the reads it holds (`MxOrig`), the terms read alike (`Tm.mx_eval`, `Tm.mx_denote`), the arrays realized from the atoms (`mxRealize`) and the atoms filled from the arrays (`mxFill`); exact at every state (`Fml.mx_holds`, `Fml.mxTop_holds`); words written in a leaf's update (feature 24): entries rewritten past each write (`MxEnt.wr`, `e₁ == e ? v : w`), objects apart (`MxRel.apart`), the update's landing a guard (`mxGuard`, `MxW.guard_iff`), the landed state related to the same cut state (`MxRel.wrs`, `MxInv`, `mx_updMem`). |
| `Calculus/MemFloor.lean` | Additive primitive-memory reconstruction prerequisite: existing integer floors as natural indices, exact original length/kind, optional-read equality with length in the floor set, retained reads, and `MxMap` fill/length-word agreement. No negative-block preparation or coverage claim. |
| `Calculus/MemInst.lean` | Context-indexed guarded point instances, exact primitive range-body recognition, substitution/weakening and fresh-allocation reconstruction certificates; whole-leaf preparation is supplied by `MemNegPrep` for its checked supported shape. |
| `Calculus/MemObserve.lean` | Equality-certified typed exterior recognition and optional read/length/operator observation stability under one fresh primitive-array reconstruction. Unsupported contexts, symbols and binders are refused. |
| `Calculus/MemNegPrep.lean` | Whole-leaf exact preparation of a fresh primitive-array negative uniform range block: scoped length atom, read/length/successor instances, stable exterior observations, same fresh heap identity, and capture-safe prefix binders; used before the memory cut. |
| `Calculus/MemStructWitness.lean` | Finite child-first typed allocation telescopes, realization and old-heap preservation, sharing and root freshness; scalar field cut with explicit heap-relative realizability and constructive global exactness. |
| `Calculus/MemStructCut.lean` | Primitive field observations and explicit child-heap countermodel transport; proof-carrying recognition of direct field/literal and reflexive equations under a single map-free fresh struct binder. |
| `Examples/MemoryStructWitness.lean` | Kernel and compiled full ordered observations for shared/distinct child graphs, absent-child refusal and fresh-parent controls. |
| `Calculus/MemHoist.lean` | Universal value binders moved out of consequents, conjuncts and past fresh objects' binders (M7 step 6, feature 23), so an index bound after its array is cut: renamings of locals (`Ren`, `Tm.ren`, `Fml.ren?`, `State.pull`, `Fml.holds_ren`), `Fml.hoist`, exact at every state (`Fml.hoist_holds`). |
| `Calculus/KIntBinder.lean` | Binders of KeY's `int` in positive positions read as starting-ledger entries at addresses of their own (M7, feature 26): `Fml.zT`, its fragment `Fml.zOK`; a leaf reading no ledger holds whatever the ledger (`Fml.netFree`, `holds_setNet`); refutation `Fml.holds_zT`, soundness `Fml.zT_holds`/`Fml.zT_valid`. |
| `Calculus/KIntTwoAnchors.lean` | Exact test-point elimination of `∀ y : Int. y > z → y > k`, for an outer KeY-int local and literal threshold; intrinsic shape certificate and at-every-state equivalence. |
| `Calculus/KIntEncode.lean` | Binders of KeY's `int` compared linearly with bounded terms encoded over two words (M7, feature 30), past an anchor whose keys cancel (feature 31: `AKey`, `Fml.holds_shift`, `Fml.kencAtA?`); substitution (`Sb`, `Fml.holds_sb`) and at-every-state equivalence (`Fml.holds_kenc`), used by `decideKEnc`; the exact strict-implication test point is a fallback in `Fml.kencBind`. |
| `Calculus/Decision.lean` | The closer's theorem (M7): `LeafVerdict`, `decideLeaf` (the pipelines `decideLeafA`: with the quantified fallback `quantReduction?`, the arrays' `arrayReduction?` and the ledger's `ledgerReduction?`; then the fresh memory arrays' `decideMem`; then the binders of KeY's `int` read as ledger entries, `decideKInt`; then bounded linear comparisons encoded over two words, `decideKEnc`) and `decideGoal` (`decideHash` on leaves with hashes, the first of `hashTries`: one kind `decideHashK`, two `decideHash2`; sound, certified refutations, complete on `Fml.Covered`: M4's fragment, `Fml.quantCovered`, `Fml.arrayCovered` or `Fml.ledgerCovered` (`Fml.CoveredA`), or `Fml.memCovered`, or `Fml.kintCovered`/`Fml.kencCovered`, and on `Fml.hashCovered`, `Fml.coveredK`/`Fml.coveredK2`), the fragment the user named (`Fml.inTarget`, its `Waivers`, the array property check `Fml.apfBlock`) and the open statement `DecisionTheorem`. |
| `Calculus/HashWrittenInput.lean` | Normalized byte equality, actual successful writes and update landings, finite-premise hash/input equivalence, and scope-correct allocation counterexample transfer. |
| `Calculus/HashWrittenRealize.lean` | Exact intrinsic syntax certificates for two fresh uint arrays, `s[0] = 1`, then hash equality; actual typed-array realization for both modalities and all three non-recovery kinds. Refuses other syntax; no general negative-array completeness. |
| `Calculus/HashWritten.lean` | Proof-backed intrinsic hash-input reduction composed with the existing memory cut, soundness and countermodel adapters. |
| `Calculus/DeclarationReduction.lean` | Exact removal of certified declaration premises through logical connectives and typed quantifiers (`declaration_reduce`, `declaration_read_reduce`, `DeclarationReadReduction.valid_iff`). |
| `Calculus/VisitedSet.lean` | A visited set with logarithmic membership (`HSet`: a search tree on a hash, buckets compared exactly) and the depth-first `walk` over it, with its closure theorem (`walk_closed`) and a variant threading a memo (`walkC_eq`); used by both arithmetic decisions. |
| `Calculus/LinearDecision.lean` | Complete binary-derivative decision of Boolean combinations of linear equations and inequalities over natural assignments (`AFml.decide_iff`): the normal form (`AFml.norm`), the walk reading one variable's bit at a time (`AFml.pstep`, `AFml.fastVerify`), the breadth-first search with its proved bound behind it; no external dependencies. |
| `Calculus/LinearHypotheses.lean` | Bounded proof-carrying propositional implication introduction and conjunction projection, before the complete arithmetic walk. Failure gives no verdict and preserves the fallback. |
| `Calculus/LinearCountermodel.lean` | Traced graph exploration equivalent to the arithmetic decision's fallback search, guaranteed counterassignment extraction, finite-list witnesses and a proof-or-model verifier (the walk's answer, or one traversal). |
| `Calculus/PresburgerDecision.lean` | Quantified linear arithmetic over naturals (`QFml`: `AFml` atoms read through variable maps, `∃` by position): its binary derivatives with projection (states over lists of the body's derivatives, a finite universe never computed), the zero test as reachability, `∃` lists sorted by hash (`canon`), the walk with memoized zero tests (`atZeroC`, `exTest`), the decision `QFml.decide_iff` and a proof-or-counterassignment `QFml.verify` (M7, step 4). |
| `Calculus/LinearSyntax.lean` | Macro-only `linear!{ … }` notation for the arithmetic intermediate language and its numbered-variable printer. |
| `Calculus/ScalarCode.lean` | Exact primitive-value codes, signed offset, checked decoding, codec inverses and arithmetic range constraints. |
| `Calculus/TypedLinear.lean` | Complete guarded arithmetic decisions over finite heterogeneous primitive contexts with decoded typed counterassignments. |
| `Calculus/LinearForms.lean` | Affine literals and variables, linear subtraction and exact equality/inequality translation. |
| `Calculus/ReadLayout.lean` | Distinct actual-reference layouts, typed observation vectors and successful-save realization of every vector. |
| `Calculus/ParameterLayout.lean` | Typed atom keys, finite parameter/storage vectors, prefix instantiation and independent reconstruction with exact observation readback. |
| `Calculus/InputLayout.lean` | Observed transaction fields before parameter/storage vectors, independent actual-state reconstruction and exact observation readback. |
| `Calculus/InputReduction.lean` | Exact source-program and typed-input compilation, original validity equivalence, complete proof-or-counterstate decisions and finite scalar-code replay. |
| `Calculus/StaticLinear.lean` | Automatic exact compilation of static primitive read/literal/defined-constant equalities and logical connectives, with original-formula proofs or guaranteed typed counterstates. |
| `Calculus/CheckedScalar.lean` | Exact definedness guards and affine signed/unsigned codes for checked addition/subtraction; defined equality preserves failed computations. |
| `Calculus/CheckedBool.lean` | Exact true/false success predicates for partial Boolean values, short circuit, strict equality and signed/unsigned comparisons. |
| `Calculus/CheckedLinear.lean` | Shared checked arithmetic, Boolean and definedness compilation over observed environment fields and finite typed parameter contexts; empty-prefix storage/update counterstates (`checkedReduction?`). |
| `Calculus/ParameterReduction.lean` | Exact universal-prefix compilation, validity equivalence and guaranteed original-source counterstates with failing typed parameter instances (`parameterReduction?`); equality-certified explicit witness layouts (`withLayout`). |
| `Calculus/DeclarationStaticReduction.lean` | Certified declaration normalization before static/checked/parameter/input decisions and trace replay, with a shared compiled recognizer and kernel-checked computation equality. |
| `Tools/CompleteVerification.lean` | Compiled exact decisions with kernel-checked original proofs or finite countermodel replay, `sol_exact`/`sol_exact?`, and actual-state report fallback. |
| `Tools/QueryArithmetic.lean` | Compiled arithmetic value selection for dependent-query certificates (`query_countermodel`) and independently kernel-checked finite-code replay. |
| `Tools/LeafArithmetic.lean` | Compiled automatic leaf countermodels (`leaf_countermodel`) with independently kernel-checked original-leaf refutations. |
| `Examples/ReadRealization.lean` | Mixed typed finite reads, equal locations and unrealizable conflicting aliases. |
| `Examples/ExactReduction.lean` | Exact leaf certificates and rejection of directional program/update fallbacks. |
| `Examples/QueryReduction.lean` | Original-formula counterstates with dependent mapping keys, typed quantified instances and nested mapped struct fields; unsupported-fragment regressions. |
| `Examples/ReadTrace.lean` | Nested-read and original-function trace counterstates, finite typed occurrences, skipped reads, source-level alias acceptance/conflict rejection and retained quantified requirements. |
| `Examples/QueryArithmetic.lean` | Solver-selected nested-read original counterstates, positive alias validity, heterogeneous signed/Boolean/unsigned extraction and forged-code/valid-query rejection. |
| `Examples/AliasConstraints.lean` | Generated alias/arithmetic original counterstates, source-path/checked-key recognition, nested typed keys/fields, subset aliases and failed path/result witness rejection. |
| `Examples/ReadFlattening.lean` | Automatic dependent leaves, positive/negative arithmetic, actual mixed storage, high fresh positions, failed/overflowing keys, alternatives and resource budgets. |
| `Examples/DependentReduction.lean` | Public original-function dependent-read verdicts, mixed arguments, minimal reports, forged-code rejection and retained quantified requirements. |
| `Examples/AutoComplete.lean` | `auto_complete` on writes at symbolic mapping keys read at another key: the eliminated leaf's conditionals, kernel-checked `bool`-keyed verdicts and countermodel, `uint`-keyed compiled verdict and code-checked refutation. |
| `Examples/Decision.lean` | `decideGoal` on products by a literal and on quantifiers in a consequent (both outside M4's fragment), Presburger quantifiers (a read in one hoisted: `evenGoal`), array property blocks (`apf`; over an inner mapping, `rowApf`, decided; a far key, `rowFar`, refuted by seven rows of twelve entries and an empty default row; a bound key not the last, two depths, a member past the key, a `bool` block, a halting guard operand and a positive `∀`, `gapColumn` … `gapPositiveForall`, decided since features 16 to 19; `int` keys and the ledger, `gapIntKeyed`, `gapLedgerBlock`, decided since features 20 and 21, false variants `intKeyedBad`, `ledgerBlockBad` refuted, and `intTwoSides`, `ledgerFar`, whose refutations are astronomically large, answering by the theorem); coverage kernel-checked for some (`*_covered`), verdicts compiled (`#eval` pins), with `sortedLe`'s exact decision/output equality kernel-certified and replayed; leaves of `Fml.inTarget` inside and outside the array property fragment (`gridApf`, `strictSorted` outside); literal operators written through the cut (`gapIntDiv` … `gapMulW`, once counterexamples, proved; `intDivBad` … `mulWBad` refuted; `&`/`|`/`^` at `int`, `addmod`/`mulmod` at a literal modulus, feature 22: `gapIntAnd`, `gapAddmod` proved, `intAndBad` … `addmodZero` refuted) and fifty reads (`gapManyReads_answers`); a ledger read past a payment (`gapLedgerWrite` refuted, `ledgerWriteOk`, `payBalanceOk` proved, `payBalanceBad` refuted, feature 25) and binders of KeY's `int` (`gapKIntBinder`, `kintBinderTwo` proved, `kintBinderBad` refuted, feature 26; a negative one, `gapKIntNeg`, decided by feature 30 in `Examples/DecisionKInt.lean`); storage arrays at symbolic indices (contract `Arrays`: `inRange`, `pastEnd`, `writeSame`, `writeOther`, `rowPast`, …), kernel-checked coverage of three; the counterexamples to `DecisionTheorem`, once one per shape left (`gapArrayBlock` … `gapWrittenMemory`, in the fragment by the kernel, `*_inTarget`); `gapWrittenMemory` now refuted by the written-memory continuation in `Examples/DecisionWrittenMemory.lean`; `gapHashNegForall` outside it since decision e3″ (`gapHashNegForall_outside`). Since 2026-11-02 this module holds the formulas and the storage-array checks only; the checks named above (`*_covered`, `*_inTarget`, `#eval` pins, `sortedLe`'s replay, `gapManyReads_answers`) are in the three modules below, each under 300 s. |
| `Examples/DecisionFeatures.lean` | `Examples/Decision.lean`'s checks on `Bank` and `Allow`: coverage, exactness and verdict pins of the M7 features, array property blocks, `rowFar`'s refutation and `sortedLe`'s certified replay. |
| `Examples/DecisionGaps.lean` | `Examples/Decision.lean`'s checks of the former counterexamples over `Gaps`: `*_inTarget`, KeY `int` binders, the ledger read past a payment, blocks past their last key, `int` and ledger blocks, the astronomically large refutations, fifty reads. |
| `Examples/DecisionGapOperators.lean` | `Examples/Decision.lean`'s checks of the literal operators written by the cut (`gapIntDiv` … `addmodZero`) and of the three hash kinds (`gapThreeKinds`, `gapMixedKinds`, `gapMixedPairs`). |
| `Examples/DecisionArrays.lean` | `decideLeaf` on blocks over storage arrays (M7, feature 32, `Calculus/ArrayBlock.lean`): `gapArrayBlock` (once a counterexample) proved, `arrayBlockBad` refuted, `arrayLenBlock` (a length guard) proved; the first two's coverage kernel-checked, `*_answers`, `gapArrayBlock_iff`.; exact closed `sampleVerdicts` replay is kernel-certified |
| `Examples/DecisionMemory.lean` | `decideLeaf` on leaves with fresh memory arrays (M7 step 6, `Calculus/MemSwap.lean`): `gapMemory` (once a counterexample) refuted, Ackermann's premise (`memAck`) proved, reads past the length, fixed arrays, two arrays apart, a `bool[]`; kernel-checked coverage of `gapMemory` and `memAck`; an index bound after its array (feature 23: `gapMemInner` refuted, `memInnerAck` proved) and writes in a leaf's update (feature 24: `gapMemWrite`, `memWriteOther` proved, `memWriteBad`, `memWriteDia` refuted), coverage kernel-checked; the original negative memory block `gapMemInnerNeg` now refuted by exact preparation; `gapMemParam` is refuted by its given-heap witness; unchanged `gapMemStruct` is refuted with constructed children, exact coverage and empty-heap vacuity controls. All three remain in the fragment. |
| `Examples/MemoryFloor.lean` | Compiled primitive-memory reconstruction controls plus kernel certificates for retained reads, empty-array halting, and missing-length/guard-breakpoint counterexamples. |
| `Examples/MemoryInstantiation.lean` | Kernel/compiled controls for scoped point substitution, binder refusal, failed/empty reads, fresh-local aliases, and the raw-length cut refusal. |
| `Examples/MemoryNegativeCountermodel.lean` | Kernel countermodel for the original `gapMemInnerNeg`: an empty fresh array refutes it at every base state; concrete `State.init Gaps` witness and non-validity corollary. The original definition is preserved; the exact closer certificate is in `MemoryNegativeDecision.lean`. |
| `Examples/MemoryNegativeDecision.lean` | Actual original `gapMemInnerNeg` coverage, returned refutation and countermodel kernel certificates; positive/negative uint/bool, optional failed-read, fresh-name and binder-scope controls. |
| `Examples/DecisionWrittenMemory.lean` | The original written-memory pin through all 18 relevant decider paths (now refuted by hash/goal), all-kind realized-schema controls, a certified positive composition, concrete heap-write/hash observations for all kinds and aliases, modulo-byte, tail/length, recovery and zero-length modality controls. |
| `Examples/DecisionHashes.lean` | `decideGoal` on leaves with hashes over the string-keyed mapping of `Examples/Tactics/Hashing.lean` (M7 step 7): `namesSet` proved, `namesSetBad` refuted, `namesSet_covered` kernel-checked; two kinds (`namesTwoKinds`, `twoKeys` over `Names2`; past an update, `namesLanded`; a value local set, `namesSetLocal`) proved and refuted; five strings (`fiveStrings`, `fiveChain`); a value quantifier in the body (`namesUnderForall`); coverage kernel-checked for one of each (`*_covered`), verdicts compiled; `namesBound` outside and `unknown`. |
| `Examples/DecisionKInt.lean` | Features 30 and 31: negative KeY `int` binders (`gapKIntNeg`), positive binders beside ledger reads, binders compared with a ledger entry or another binder (past an anchor); kernel-checked coverage with `*_answers`/`*_iff`, the earlier verdicts recorded (not evaluated in the build); exact kernel certificates now prove `gapKIntTwoAnchors` through all three decision entry points, with positive/refuted controls and refusal pins; the original `gapQuantLedger` statement is retained and now proved in `Examples/DecisionQuantLedger.lean`. |
| `Examples/DecisionQuantLedger.lean` | Kernel exact Leaf/Goal verdicts for the unchanged ledger-quantifier gap, alias/payment/negative/unbounded-entry controls, nonclosed parameter fallback certificates and two-code representation laws; runtime runner in `tests/quant-ledger/Run.lean`. |
| `Examples/DeclarationReduction.lean` | Generated declaration premises removed exactly, also beneath parameters, with true/false user requirements preserved and false quantified facts rejected. |
| `Examples/LinearDecision.lean` | Parity validity and extracted arithmetic models, negative coefficients/targets, different widths, finite-list witnesses and both verifier verdicts. |
| `Examples/TypedLinear.lean` | Codec boundaries and rejection, Boolean range constraints, unsigned 17 and mixed Boolean/signed/unsigned counterassignments. |
| `Examples/StaticLinear.lean` | Automatic original-formula counterstates, actual alias sharing, mixed typed reads, retained declaration/user premises and unsupported-source rejection. |
| `Examples/CheckedLinear.lean` | Checked source arithmetic, all signed/unsigned bounds, failed equality, intermediate cancellation, no-wrap premises, and declaration-normalized original counterstates. |
| `Examples/BooleanLinear.lean` | Original-source numeric comparisons, interval counterstates, partial Boolean expressions, short-circuit order and strict equality. |
| `Examples/ExactUpdates.lean` | Box/diamond update failure, successful false values, storage writes and counterstates, parallel binding/swap, and memory-context/push/program rejection. |
| `Examples/ParameterReduction.lean` | Automatic typed parameter and storage counterinstances, repeated reads, mixed types, overflow, updates and declaration premises beneath a universal prefix. |
| `Examples/ProgramReduction.lean` | Original-program proofs and counterstates, nested blocks, checked local failure, require/assert/revert, both branches, ignored panic, partial conditions and unsupported-program rejection. |
| `Examples/CallReduction.lean` | Void/scalar/tuple/nested call decisions and original counterstates, discarded/default returns, caller-local preservation, failed arguments and callee panic/revert. |
| `Examples/InputReduction.lean` | All five transaction fields, mixed typed parameters/storage, actual function validity and explicit original-function countermodel certificates. |
| `Examples/CompleteVerification.lean` | Public exact proof/countermodel reports, generated mapping premises, mixed function arguments, nonzero contract address and minimal report fallback. |

See `docs/complete-verification.md` for integration and open work.

## Examples

Two directories, by proof style (`.claude/rules/derivations.md`).

`Examples/Chains/` — the calculus's worked examples, one file per section in
its order, each example a chain in its printed lines and fresh names, read by
the scoped calculus (`open scoped Solidity.Scoped`):

| Module | Its examples |
|---|---|
| `Examples/Chains/Storage.lean` | Storage writes and reads, roots, rebinding, index, `push`/`pop`, nonsimple paths, side effects in receiver and index. |
| `Examples/Chains/StorageArrays.lean`, `Examples/Chains/StorageDelete.lean` | The further storage array cases; storage `delete`. |
| `Examples/Chains/StorageCoverage.lean` | One chain per storage rule, from a concrete state. |
| `Examples/Chains/FixedLength.lean` | A fixed array's length: copied to a dynamic one (`fixedCopy`, `lenOnSaveFixedCopy`), deleted, an element pushed; each to `true` past the program but the push. |
| `Examples/Chains/Arithmetic.lean` | The compound storage update. |
| `Examples/Chains/Memory.lean`, `Examples/Chains/MemoryDelete.lean`, `Examples/Chains/MemoryArrays.lean` | Memory aliasing, writes and reads; memory `delete`; memory arrays. |
| `Examples/Chains/MemoryCoverage.lean` | One chain per memory rule, from a concrete state. |
| `Examples/Chains/StorageToMemory.lean`, `Examples/Chains/MemoryToStorage.lean` | The copies between the two, with lazy reads. |
| `Examples/Chains/Payment.lean` | `transfer` under the box: a literal amount, a nonsimple amount, a storage receiver. |
| `Examples/Tactics/CheckedBlock.lean` | `checked { … }`: an underflow refuted inside the block and valid outside it, what the block writes, guards discharged by the closer, `&&` and a loop's condition in evaluation order, a narrow type's `assert`; `ignoringOverflow { … }`: a diamond that holds only with the overflow assumed away. |
| `Examples/Tactics/Bytes.lean` | `bytesN`, `bytes` and `string` as solc runs them, on the elaborator's reading (`docs/semantic-gaps.md`, "Bytes and strings"): literals left-aligned, comparison, bitwise operators, indexing (out of range reverts), `.length`, conversions between sizes and to `uintM`, implicit widening; in state variables, mapping keys and values, parameters and returns. |
| `Examples/Tactics/Hashing.lean` | `keccak256` (one byte string hashed twice), `abi.encode`/`abi.encodePacked` layouts proved, `abi.decode`, the `concat`s and a dynamic encoding run (`#run`), a `mapping(string => uint)` keyed by the hash (`docs/semantic-gaps.md`, "Hashing and the ABI"). |
| `Examples/Tactics/AbiFeatures.lean` | Kernel ERC-7201/byte-roundtrip proofs, ABI layouts, type rejection and malformed decoder input regressions. |
| `Examples/Tactics/AbiFeaturesImport.lean` | Exact-solc-0.8.38 source fixture, namespace/ABI/recovery runs and a deliberately false assertion. |
| `Examples/Tactics/InheritedSelectorsImport.lean` | Exact-solc-0.8.38 fixture `tests/abi-0.8.38/InheritedSelectors.sol`: selectors, `abi.encodeCall` and external function values of overridden and inherited (renamed) functions, pinned; calls through external function values refused by name. |
| `Examples/Tactics/AbiHexCall.lean` | Exact-width source hex evidence, declaration-based encodeCall conversion, source execution/proof scripts and a false-property counterexample. |
| `Examples/Tactics/Recovery.lean` | Kernel generator/address reductions and valid/high-s/invalid/boundary recovery vectors. |
| `Examples/Tactics/RevertData.lean` | Revert data reaching a `catch` (`docs/semantic-gaps.md`, "Revert data"): a self-call's `require(false, "boom")` reason bound by `catch Error(string memory r)` and its bytes proved, two reasons over-approximated, `catch (bytes memory d)`, the run on a concrete state, `catchDataBox` pinned; a custom error of a symbolic argument (`symbolicCustom`, data known in part) and another contract's reason (`externalReason`, `Fml.allocAll`). |
| `Examples/Tactics/ComputedRevert.lean` | Exact execution certificates for computed reasons, alias writes, empty payloads and eager require arguments; nested catch executable guards and rejected assembly imports. |
| `Examples/Tactics/FunctionTypes.lean` | Internal function types on the elaborator's reading (`docs/semantic-gaps.md`, "Function types"): a value is its function's tag, a call through it a dispatch over the functions of its signature; in locals, parameters, returns, state variables and struct members; reference arguments (memory/storage arrays, nonrecursive structs) dispatched by reference with aliasing runs; tuple assignments with several value returns and struct memory returns through function values, non-name callees included; an unassigned value reverts when called, `delete`, `==`; external address/selector projection, guarded cancellation and overflow-rejection regressions. |
| `Examples/Tactics/MemoryReturns.lean` | Several returns with memory ones (`CallRet.rets`, `RetBinders.snocMem`): kernel-checked runs of a value and a memory return, two memory returns with aliasing of a returned parameter, the unassigned default, a rebound target, two fresh objects, `bytes memory`, an omitted target, a narrow element, the same through function values named and not (`fnTupleDispatch`), two calculus proofs of the value target, the printed program; storage returns among several, a target of another element width, a mismatched return location and a narrow element through a function value refused. |
| `Examples/Tactics/CleanTail.lean` | Dirty array tails (O6): `Tokens.dangling` reachable and not clean, a push recycling a dirty slot (`reused_slot_retains_value`), runs that repair it, and the boundaries refuted (`leaveDirty_boundary_rejected`, `interruptRepair_callback_boundary_rejected`). |
| `Examples/Tactics/TailMode.lean` | The option of O6: `tokens.push()` read back proved under `ValidTail .clean` by `sol_prove` and `sol_decide` (`pushRead_clean`, `pushRead_clean_box`, `pushAlias_clean`), refuted under `.solc` at `Tokens.dangling` (`pushRead_solc_refuted`), and a dirty write of the program read back under either (`recycledWrite_solc`); as a contract's invariant (`Prog.exposesNone` decided, `pushRead_reachable`). |
| `Examples/Chains/CheckedArithmetic.lean`, `Examples/Chains/EvaluationOrder.lean` | Overflow reverts; the evaluation order of an indexed write. |

`Examples/Tactics/` — theorems `⊨ᶠ dl!{ … }` proved by `sol_symex; sol_close`
or `sol_decide`, derivations `⊢ φ` built one `apply` per taclet, and runs:

| Module | What it shows |
|---|---|
| `Examples/Tactics/Tour.lean` | The running example end to end. |
| `Examples/Tactics/PushReceiver.lean` | Kernel-checked primitive push binding execution, dirty-slot behavior and old-target-index positive/false cases; native capture rule-name pins in both modalities and a simple-alias update pin. |
| `Examples/Tactics/NewArrays.lean` | Kernel execution checks for fresh primitive and nested arrays, allocation-size effects and target evaluation order, with false-value and empty-read cases. |
| `Examples/Tactics/Aggregates.lean` | Inline common typing, snapshot timing, aliasing, tuple captures, negative admission and kernel execution checks. |
| `Examples/Tactics/AggregatesImport.lean` | Pinned Solidity 0.8.38 aggregate fixture, imported nonvacuity runs and a symbolic literal proof. |
| `Examples/Tactics/AggregateSlices.lean` | Stable slice identities, endpoint/index effects, binary schedules and catchable bounds panic. |
| `Examples/Tactics/SliceCopies.lean` | Primitive slice materialization, internal frames, returns, storage and hash consumers; finite execution certificates lifted to the unbounded run; readonly and shape refusals. |
| `Examples/Tactics/SliceCopiesImport.lean` | Pinned modern Solidity 0.8.38 source regression for slice copies, calldata locations and non-array aliases. |
| `Examples/Tactics/SliceReferenceCopies.lean` | Both-pipeline source certificates for fixed primitive-row slice copying, independent aliases, rebinding, widths, lazy branches, bounds and nominal refusals. |
| `Examples/Tactics/StorageSteps.lean` | One storage statement form at a time, as `apply` walks. |
| `Examples/Tactics/StorageSuite.lean`, `Examples/Tactics/StorageDelete.lean`, `Examples/Tactics/LedgerDelete.lean` | solkey's taclet suite on storage; `delete`; a struct holding a mapping deleted. |
| `Examples/Tactics/Branch.lean`, `Examples/Tactics/Revert.lean` | Two-goal splits; box and diamond on `revert`, `require`, `assert` (a check: `[ assert(false); ] true` is not valid). |
| `Examples/Tactics/Join.lean` | The join after a conditional (`ifElseJoin`): `n` sequential `if (c) { x = x + 1; }` in `5n + 3` nodes and one leaf, pinned; the M6 family proved. |
| `Examples/Tactics/LoopNotices.lean` | Located literal-loop warnings with annotation/nesting/callee/import controls and unchanged diamond-result/refutation certificates; a pinned constructor-only source fixture. |
| `Examples/Tactics/JoinControls.lean` | Conditional-term notation, lazy branch evaluation, signed-offset reader controls and inhabited false bounds (AU03). |
| `Examples/Tactics/LongContext.lean` | Kernel long-array certificate with a supplied local in a nonempty peeled context; true supplied-candidate and missing-argument refusal controls. |
| `Examples/Tactics/PolySize.lean` | The size of symbolic execution (`docs/automation.md`, M8): `#proof_size` (nodes, branches, open goals, the largest leaf in `Fml.tmSize`) of sequential `if`s that both assign, literal-bounded loops under their invariants, and the two combined, pinned for small `k`. |
| `Examples/Tactics/CallSummary.lean` | A call by its callee's summary (`internalCallSummary`): the family `f₀(x) = x + 1`, `fₖ(x) = fₖ₋₁(fₖ₋₁(x))`, its walk pinned (one node per call, where inlining made `7·2^(k+1) − 3`), proved for `k = 1…6`; a callee whose body drops an owed value is inlined; the checked family `f₀(x) = x * 3` proved for `k = 1…5`, its leaf one call site at a time (`Calculus/LeafShare.lean`), a false leaf failing within the heartbeats, and the leaf of sixteen products evaluated whole (`triplingFourWhole`). |
| `Examples/Tactics/Payment.lean`, `Examples/Tactics/Net.lean` | `transfer` and `send` as `⊢` walks with checked sequents; the frame, the ledger's postconditions and the runs, a refused send's included. |
| `Examples/Tactics/Values.lean`, `Examples/Tactics/Operators.lean`, `Examples/Tactics/Checked.lean` | Operators, checked arithmetic, `−−`, bitwise, shifts, `unchecked`, `uint8` … `int248`, casts. |
| `Examples/Tactics/LocalBindings.lean` | Binding-local width/ABI metadata under shadowing, captures and parallel updates; nearest-binding refinement and metadata-independent formula binders. |
| `Examples/Tactics/Calls.lean`, `Examples/Tactics/CallOperands.lean`, `Examples/Tactics/Callback.lean`, `Examples/Tactics/TryCatch.lean`, `Examples/Tactics/Contracts.lean` | Internal calls, call-valued operands, callbacks (`ProvesC`), `try`/`catch`, calls by contract (`useContract`). |

| `Examples/Tactics/Recursion.lean` | Recursive functions (`Stmt.callRec`): a countdown, a recursive sum and two mutually recursive functions elaborated and run (`decide +kernel`); the countdown's box without panic and `add(x, y) == x + y` by contract, proved by induction over the frames (`rec_contract_by`, `RecContract.unroll`, `sol_symex_rec`); under the callback reading, a countdown after a value call and a recursion that calls back (`rec_auto_c`, `ProvesC.recContract`). |

| `Examples/Tactics/ContractWorld.lean` | Explicit factory, instance identity, funding, rollback, catch boundary and linked-context regressions for the typed world API. |
| `Examples/Tactics/ContractWorldSource.lean` | Restricted complete source new/try-new and linked storage-field mutation fragments, with environment-admission and positive/negative regressions. |
| `Examples/Tactics/TransientWorld.lean` | Kernel controls for delegate-owned transient cells, distinct instances, transaction reset and rollback of successful descendants. |

| `Examples/Tactics/QuantifiedDiamond.lean` | Quantified scalar diamonds, for/continue, nonempty and false quantified posts, concrete successful-run witnesses and imported divergence controls in both pipelines. |
| `Examples/Tactics/LoopsImport.lean` | Loops from solc's AST (`tests/solc/Loops.sol`): the `/// @custom:key` clauses `scripts/solc-ast.mjs` reads from the source by the loop's `src` offset, printed above the loop by the front end (a clause over several `///` lines joined, as solkey's `KeyNatspec`); a `return` inside a loop lowered on import; five of its seven obligations proved, the two not proved named with why. |
| `Examples/Tactics/AllLeftLater.lean` | Quantified premises at a later universal index, a program local and an anonymised invariant-loop counter, through the public strategy and closer; proved and unavailable guard cases. |

| `Examples/Tactics/Rigid.lean` | A memory-writing diamond loop proved by bounded rigid branch pruning; forty self-doublings refuse before exponential substitution, contradictory unused premises conservatively retain both branches, and a division-by-zero prefix stays invalid. |
| `Examples/Tactics/SymbolicCounterexample.lean` | Counterexamples past every storage the search builds: a counting loop over a `2^255`-element array run in closed form (`Prog.runA` against `Prog.runF`), refuted by `sol_refute` where it overflows and where it ends late; a true property of the same loop not refuted. |

| `Examples/Tactics/Chains.lean` | Scoped chains pinned: a step, a named step, several, a rewrite past the program, a branch run into its `}`, the box; `#derivation`, `#chain` (pasted back and checked), `#chain_rest`, `#last_line`, a `FreshNames` table, a chain proving `Fml.Valid`, and the chains the tactics write on `dl!{ … }` read scoped (`sol_chain?`, `#chain`, `sol_rws?`). |

| `Examples/Tactics/CallbackRuns.lean` | The typed callback reading used: the deterministic run of a program that pays is a callback run (`Prog.exec_run`), and every callback run of one that does not is its deterministic run, decided by the kernel (`ExecP.forall_iff`); a send's three goals under `ProvesC`. |
| `Examples/Tactics/Derive.lean` | The scoped strategy tools pinned: `sol_prove`, `sol_symex`/`sol_close`, `sol_derive?`'s walk (replayed) and `sol_derive` on `Fml.Valid` goals, `sol_derive` leaving the leaves; `sol_prove` and a walk under `⊢ₖ` (`rule_admits`), and `sol_prove` refusing `loopFalse` there while it proves it under `⊢`. |
| `Examples/Tactics/ProofTree.lean` | The proof tree of a scoped goal pinned: `#proof_tree`, `#proof_node`, `#proof_tree_json` on `cdl!{}`. |

| `Examples/Tactics/Sequents.lean` | Scoped walks with checked `show sequent!{ … }` lines. |
| `Examples/Tactics/SolSpec.lean` | `Examples/Tactics/Specs.lean`'s obligations scoped and proved by the same `sol_spec`. |
| `Examples/Tactics/Boundaries.lean` | Pins typed external inputs, bounded ledger keys, segment composition and EVM word storage (including rejected ill-typed terms). |
| `Examples/Tactics/PushSuffixRun.lean` | Checks the native typed primitive push suffix runs and two-stage agreement on empty and recycled slots. |
| `Examples/Tactics/PushSuffixGuards.lean` | Checks outer DIV/MOD guards, signed zero typing and guarded pair quotation across an intermediate context. |
| `Examples/Tactics/PushSuffixRules.lean` | Checks all nine native push suffix rule premises, primitive-binding receiver capture, both complex storage-source captures, memory copy, twenty-six typed text roundtrips and the SolKey admission boundary for RHS and receiver capture. |
| `Examples/Tactics/PushSuffixSource.lean` | Imports the pinned Solidity push suffix fixture and checks signed/unsigned execution, indexed receiver order, recycled slots and arithmetic failures. |
| `Examples/Tactics/PushSource.lean` | Imports ten compiled primitive/reference push-source functions and checks copy independence, source effects, invalid indices, fresh-shaped source names and numbered capture-chain replay. |
| `Examples/Tactics/MemoryPush.lean` | Imports nine compiled memory-push functions and checks independent array/struct copies, recycled slots, changing receivers, complex memory-source capture, false-postcondition panics and invalid-source reversion; it refutes the old receiver-duplicating expansion. |
| `Examples/Tactics/StorageReturns.lean` | Pins seventeen elaborated storage-return source rows, twelve execution outcomes, sixteen closed-program printer checks (eight programs through both printers) and eight open-tail prefix/continuation checks. Direct push/pop call receivers use native capture; the imported effectful receiver also closes by `sol_prove`. |
| `Examples/Tactics/DeferredSource.lean` | Checks twenty-one kernel execution outcomes for the deferred-operand foundation: getter order, prefix failures, snapshots, caller locals, memory growth and explicit capture. It does not enable native effectful call-receiver syntax. |
| `Examples/Tactics/DeferredCallback.lean` | Checks nine deferred-capture callback cases, including resumed storage changing the pushed value, violated invariants suppressing a bad source, getter failure ordering and heap growth retained after scope end. |
| `Examples/Tactics/CallbackWk.lean` | Checks callback weakening with a fresh unset alias: resumed storage and allocation survive, violations propagate, and a prefix's memory temporary is dropped while the new alias remains. |
| `Examples/Tactics/CallbackUnwk.lean` | Recovers four explicit weakened callback runs: a push reads resumed storage and retains an allocation, a broken invariant propagates, a paying loop allocates and exits on its changed guard, and prefix scope exit retains the allocation after removing the temporary. |
| `Examples/Tactics/DeferredCorrespondence.lean` | Checks direct-to-capture resumed storage and allocation, unset getter precedence, prefix violation before a bad source, capture-to-direct resume, and a reference operand copied from allocated memory with the getter run once. |
| `Examples/Tactics/NativeDeferred.lean` | Checks six ordinary native outcomes: increments, getter failure, allocation, combined heap growth, caller-local mutation and memory-reference copy. |
| `Examples/Tactics/NativeDeferredSource.lean` | Checks four source programs and both printer roundtrips for canonical increment and primitive-call operands, including private scope exit. Empty deferred prefixes and private-local dependencies retain explicit fallback rendering; arbitrary prefixes do not have a reconstructed Solidity-expression spelling. |
| `Examples/Tactics/NativeDeferredCallback.lean` | Checks resumed storage/allocation, unset getter precedence, callback violation before a bad source and a copied memory reference through native callback clauses. |
| `Examples/Tactics/Nonvacuity.lean` | A normally returning program's true result proved and false result refuted and unprovable; strict primitive capture realizability and delayed landed-binder macro regressions. |
| `Examples/Tactics/Latex.lean` | Both LaTeX backends pinned: formulas, judgements, line breaking, proved chains, a derivation with the filter, `tests/solc/FixedLength.sol`'s obligation and derivation, the symbol table's coverage, definition links. |
| `Examples/Tactics/ProgramTrace.lean` | Checked program traces: capture order, read continuations, JSON rows, parameters, kernel submission, budget failure. |
| `Examples/Tactics/SelfPowerData.lean` | Both-pipeline concrete kernel executions for unsigned-power panic bytes in ordinary self-call frames, conditional/index ordering, rollback and negative expectations. |

At the root, the notation's own tests:

| Module | What it shows |
|---|---|
| `Examples/ChainNotation.lean`, `Examples/ChainRewrites.lean`, `Examples/ExampleNames.lean` | How a chain is written and checked, its rewrite links, the printed names of fresh variables. |
| `Examples/ChainExpand.lean` | The expansion links past the program (`Calculus/ExpandLaws.lean`), the closer on the expanded lines, which types expand. |
| `Examples/ChainRewritesOpen.lean` | The rewrite links while the program is open, on the scoped line (`Calculus/RewriteScoped.lean`), a step of the strategy after them in the same chain. |
| `Examples/Notation.lean` | What taclets, premises and sequents print, pinned. |
| `Examples/Verify.lean`, `Tools.lean` | `#verify`, `#counterexample` and the other commands, pinned. |
| `Examples/ProofTree.lean` | The proof tree's commands, `sol_derive?` and `sol_prove?`, pinned. |
| `Examples/Benchmark/*.lean` | solkey's benchmark contracts with their `@custom:key` clauses proved (`Counter`, `SimpleStorage`, `Mapping`, `Purchase`, `Coin`, `EtherWallet`, `ERC20`), and `Syntax`, which pins what elaborates away (units, casts, events, errors, enums, struct constructors, modifiers). |
| `Examples/Tactics/SignedNumericImport.lean` | Both-pipeline signed numeric source controls, negative assertions and checked/unchecked width/parity regressions. |
| `Syntax/Numeric.lean` | Typed value composition for checked signed power, with distinct signed base and unsigned exponent. |

## External try execution evidence

| Module | What it defines |
|---|---|
| `Semantics/ExternalTryWitness.lean` | Exact external oracle/decoder equations and normal-return witnesses for all four try alternatives; caller-failure controls. |
| `Counterexamples/DiamondTry.lean` | A reached try admits a caller revert, refuting its universal callback diamond under either payment flag. |
| `Examples/Tactics/ExternalTryWitness.lean` | Source-shaped explicit-oracle controls, ordered storage observations, caller failures and callback refusal. |

### Deterministic try formula witnesses

| Module | Defines |
|---|---|
| `Calculus/Sound/ExternalTryWitness.lean` | State-indexed deterministic diamond transport from explicit normal try and allocated catch-data witnesses; no validity or callback promotion. |
| `Examples/Tactics/ExternalTryFormula.lean` | Concrete success/catch formulas, allocated raw/reason payloads, explicit frame transport and refusal controls. |
| `Examples/Tactics/ExternalTryUniversal.lean` | Countermodel over the actual original source-domain builders: no universal normal-try family, deterministic validity or callback source-profile validity for the quoted Probe. |

### Explicit conditional try outcomes

| Module | Defines |
|---|---|
| `Semantics/TryConditions.lean` | Exact full-result self/oracle and payload-catch conditions, with argument-prefix success/failure transport. |
| `Semantics/TryCreateConditions.lean` | Exact creation-try conditions over the unchanged typed world interface. |
| `Calculus/Sound/TryConditions.lean` | Guarded ordinary formula transport for all results/frames and exact normal-or-exhausted diamond account. |
| `Examples/Tactics/TryConditions.lean` | Arbitrary-state explicitly guarded source success, certified failure boundaries and typed/runtime replay-context refusals. |


### Native self-try entry return bridge

| Module | Defines |
|---|---|
| `Semantics/EntryReturn.lean` | Conditional source/serializer call composition, exact heap/reference/caller capture, failures and bounded-to-ordinary execution agreement. |
| `Tools/EntryReturn.lean` | Exact-declaration, original-prefix-scope entry-only serializer for a primitive prefix plus one source-marked bytes/string memory result, or one return-only external-void function value. |
| `Examples/Tactics/EntryReturn.lean` | Actual native bridge quotations, bytes/once-only kernel snapshots, normal ordinary witnesses and negative admission/input controls. |
| `Examples/Tactics/EntryReturnFlow.lean` | Legacy loop-return, alias-mutation, modifier-continuation and default-memory kernel controls. |
| `Examples/Tactics/EntryReturnFlowIR.lean` | The same controls under existing viaIR modifier frames, with safe kernel declaration factoring for the large quotation. |
| `Examples/Tactics/EntryReturnMetadata.lean` | Signed narrow and fixed-byte return metadata, exact order and empty memory kernel control. |
| `Examples/Tactics/EntryReturnFunction.lean` | Output-only external-function address/selector, bytes24 padding, order and once-only kernel witnesses in both evaluation modes; function-input refusals remain. |

### Creation-try caller continuations

| Module | Defines |
|---|---|
| `Semantics/CreationTryFrame.lean` | Typed pending caller/creation/handler/tail composition over existing World operations, exact failure and allocation laws, universal bounded-to-ordinary approximation. |
| `Frontend/CreationTryImport.lean` | Complete-declaration, identity-resolved stateless unpaid creation-try source compiler with actual constructor, local argument, returned handle and Error payload branches. |
| `Tools/CreationTryReplay.lean` | Bounded native creation source observations with explicit checked runtime context, actual typed world threading and refusal controls. |
| `Examples/Tactics/CreationTrySource.lean` | Complete original creation source outputs, ordinary transport, argument/rollback/installation/wrong-byte kernel controls and malformed replay controls. |
| `Examples/Tactics/CreationTrySourceIR.lean` | Separately compiled viaIR original creation source with both ordered output kernel witnesses. |


### Linked library try source continuation

| Module | Owning role |
|---|---|
| `Semantics/LibraryTryFrame.lean` | Installed library try boundary with total raw extraction, outer decoder, exact caller resume/context/storage/ledger laws and ordinary transport. |
| `Frontend/LibraryTryImport.lean` | Complete checked library/caller declarations and exact scalar/storage-formal mapping into caller-typed programs. |
| `Tools/LibraryTryReplay.lean` | Explicit concrete linked runtime context, canonical Boolean input, once-only world execution and caller-storage observations. |
| `Examples/Tactics/LibraryTrySource.lean` | Original ordered outputs, ordinary-reading equality and actual prefix storage witnesses. |
| `Examples/Tactics/LibraryTryControls.lean` | Wrong role/identity/frame, decoder/input/static refusals, exact storage alias and atomic rollback controls. |
| `Examples/Tactics/LibraryTrySourceIR.lean` | Separately compiled via-IR original linked source with all ordered output certificates. |

M1 try producer side witnesses: `Evm/TryByteImage.lean` bounds scalar-frame
byte images and overlap/snapshots; `Evm/TryByteCapture.lean` reuses typed
allocation and raw halt; `Evm/TryPanicProducer.lean` computes the source-linked
36-byte component. `Examples/Tactics/TryPanicProducer.lean` keeps constructed-call
controls distinct. See [bounded milestone](feature-work/diamond-try-m1.md); none
admits physical assembly or proves target-byte/original-callee transport.

| Module | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `Evm/TryPanicCarrier.lean` | Closed intrinsic constructed-call carrier; no original-source construction, bytecode adequacy, callback change or source admission is asserted. |
| `Examples/Tactics/TryPanicCarrier.lean` | Nonvacuous constructed-call controls; no source or target adequacy is claimed. |
| `Evm/TryPanicSourcePlan.lean`, `Evm/TryPanicSourceFrame.lean`, `Evm/TryPanicSourceDeterminism.lean` | H8 bounded try/panic source model: `ThreeOp` plan, `BodyCompletedAt`, `FiniteEntry`, completion uniqueness ([H8](feature-work/diamond-try-h8-source-model.md)). |
| `Frontend/SolcJson/TryPanicSourceData.lean`, `Frontend/SolcJson/TryPanicSourcePlan.lean`, `Frontend/SolcJson/TryPanicSourceCaller.lean`, `Examples/Tactics/TryPanicSource{Plan,Determinism,Whole}.lean` | Quoted pinned AST/input and structural derivations; controls. |
| `Semantics/ContractWorldCertificates.lean` | Published paid-run/source-wrapper operation certificates, reused unchanged from origin/master cc155f36. |
| `Semantics/PaymentWorld.lean` | Typed stipend source frames over actual world calls; send completed-failure handling, transfer exact halt transport, no-op/funding and atomic rollback laws. |
| `Frontend/PaymentPlan.lean` | Complete declaration-resolved creation/send and initializer/receive/transfer/getter source shapes; unknown effects and full-gas calls refused. |
| `Frontend/PaymentImport.lean` | Imports actual receive/getter code, builds typed initializer handle assignment and paid creation/payment compositions with source identities. |
| `Tools/PaymentReplay.lean` | Ordered deterministic source-world observations with actual nested bytes and committed-world audits; gas remains an independent obligation. |
| `Examples/Tactics/PaymentSource.lean` | Kernel-checked original send and debug transfer source snapshots, initialized handle/funding/getter witnesses and wrong handle/kind/bytes controls. |
| `Backend/Creation/Artifact.lean` | G44.02 intrinsic artifact sections, executable CODECOPY/RETURN wrapper, exact minimal PUSH widths, layout and argument extraction laws. |
| `Backend/Creation/ArtifactChecks.lean` | G44.02 inhabited layout, argument separation and negative controls with headline kernel axiom pins. |
| `Backend/Creation/Execution.lean` | Independent byte-consuming PUSH/CODECOPY/RETURN/REVERT slice semantics and exact installed runtime proof. |
| `Backend/Creation/ExecutionChecks.lean` | Inhabited empty, word and PUSH-width boundary installation witnesses, byte-range negative controls and axiom pins. |
| `Backend/Creation/Placement.lean` | Relocated runtime-return slice with arithmetic alignment certificate and minimal offset PUSH width. |

Memory-loop validation helpers: `scripts/memory-loop-evm.mjs` records actual
four-mode upstream EVM observations and refusal controls;
`scripts/check-memory-loop-timing.py` freshly checks the entire original
`Derived17` group; `scripts/check-memory-loop-timing.mjs` validates cumulative
300-second evidence, with negative controls in its `.test.mjs` companion.

### P1 runtime frames (producer checkpoint; [runtime-frame-p1.md](feature-work/runtime-frame-p1.md))

| Module | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameSemantics.lean` | `callRec` replaced by its finite thunk body, with an equal execution. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameTarget.lean` | Published `Calls` certificates transported along that equation. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameTransport.lean` | Argument, entry/body extension and result export at the checked target endpoint. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameStack.lean` | Runtime-pointer word push/pop on the existing machine. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameSaved.lean` | Reserved frame words preserve `StateRepr`; scalar/reference saves. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameFootprint.lean` | Word intervals preserved by allocation, rebinding and array-write leaves. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameComposition.lean` | Checked certificates paired with constructor-derived scratch-safety proofs. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameRestore.lean` | Restoring a saved word into the original caller cell. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameContext.lean` | Whole caller-context capture/restore around a checked body. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameInterfaceV1.lean` | Frozen v1 signatures: `Emission`, `Entry`, `Returned` and the unproved `Pending*` premise structures. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameInterfaceV2.lean` | Frozen v2 `Pending*` structures: each needs an entry whose source run succeeds (`SuccessfulEntry`) or bare-reverts (`RevertingEntry`); v1 relations unchanged. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameInterfaceV3.lean` | Frozen v3: explicit external admission domain, exact-input/index `EntryCoverage`, `AdmissionAgreement` duty and independently witnessed admitted success and bare revert in every `Pending*` structure. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameRestoredRepresentation.lean` | Implements the v1 full restored representation from the source-tail equation and alias-flag transport. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameCapturedProgram.lean` | Capture, checked body and restore with full representation; captured bare-revert absorption. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameReference.lean`, `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameResult.lean` | Reached memory-reference and primitive-result export into caller bindings with the full reached representation. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameCapturedReference.lean` | Capture/body/restore followed by typed reference export and caller use; older-frame and allocator transport. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameCallAgreement.lean` | Inversion of an actual typed call into arguments, body frame and exported caller result (void and memory returns). |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameNoneProducer.lean` | v2 void-call, fixed-thunk and failure producers built from checked certificates and real entry witnesses. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3RecursiveProducer.lean`, `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3RecursiveUnwind.lean` | Runtime descent prefixes at every horizon and LIFO return loop with captured caller recovery (target side only). |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3RecursiveSource.lean` | Actual `callRec` countdown family: all-horizon execution and `RecursiveReturnCoherence`; no v3 recursive instance. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3CallProducer.lean`, `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3CallProducerControls.lean` | v3 `PendingCallProducer` for primitive value-returning calls (`coverage`, `returns`, `bare_revert`, `make`) and an applied control with real success and revert runs. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3CallProducerRef.lean` | v3 `PendingCallProducer` for typed memory-return and tuple call forms (`coverage`, `returns`, `bare_revert`, `make`); success/revert witnesses and profile agreement stay explicit inputs. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3BareRevertProducer.lean` | P1-REVERT: `bare_revert` for any statement; `directProducer`/`callProducer` inhabit `PendingBareRevertProducer` on one fixture with real reverting runs. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3FixedThunkProducer.lean` | P1-THUNK: `PendingFixedCallRecProducer` for one array-writing thunk with real success and out-of-bounds revert runs. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3CountdownWitness.lean` | Populated core-only countdown endpoint; actual prefixes/arguments, full caller reconstruction and ordered observations. No general recursive producer claim. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3BarePrefix.lean` | Derived reached entry/allocation input and exact bare-revert execution after a successful checked prefix; no rollback/profile closure. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3AllocatorReturn.lean` | Recursive return allocator transport and inhabited callee-only ownership client; zero source allocation, with initial ownership explicit. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3RecursiveResult.lean` | Actual allocator-backed native body/runtime `GrowingResult` client; retains shallow exhaustion mismatch. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3ParentRestore.lean` | Actual first-parent typed restore and allocator transport from reached child/saved words, with inhabited countdown client. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3ParentPrefixes.lean` | Typed actual running unwind prefixes, remaining payload transport and inhabited ordered-observation controls. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3ParentStop.lean` | Final false guard, typed completed unwind and stable later horizons, with populated and empty-history controls. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3NativeParents.lean` | Actual indexed native child-call/return clauses and represented parent poststate linkage; exhaustion and child-export controls. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameBytes.lean` | P6-backed numeric caller-cell restoration: byte-image save/clobber/restore runs on the existing machines; no dynamic loads or source/world rollback. |
| `Backend/StructuredFlow/BranchSelector.lean`, `Backend/StructuredFlow/ExitSelector.lean`, `Backend/StructuredFlow/CoreScopes.lean`, `Backend/StructuredFlow/CoreSequence.lean` | G36.01 target branch/exit selectors and core-compiler scope adapters ([G36](feature-work/roadmap-g36-structured-flow.md)). |
| `Backend/StructuredFlow/{Branch,Exit,CoreScope,CoreSequence}Controls.lean` | Concrete selector, exit and scope controls. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3RecursiveActivation.lean`, `RuntimeFrameV3RecursiveDescent.lean`, `RuntimeFrameV3RecursiveHistory.lean` | Countdown family: checked activation bridge, descent at every reached horizon, saved parent history and nearest-parent pop. |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameV3PairedUnwind.lean`, `RuntimeFrameV3RecursiveReturn.lean` | Countdown family: paired all-parent unwind and the descent-plus-unwind endpoint in the original `StateRepr` (hand-written image). |
| `Backend/InternalFlow/RuntimeFrameMutation.lean`, `RuntimeFrameNested.lean`, `RuntimeFrameReferenceProgram.lean`, `RuntimeFrameCodeEquality.lean` | One-level and nested call compilers (`compileCall`), reference-returning programs, Boolean instruction-list equality. |
| `Backend/InternalDispatch.lean`, `Backend/InternalDispatch/Typed.lean`, `Backend/InternalDispatch/Agreement.lean`, `Backend/InternalDispatch/Chain.lean` | G37.03/P4 typed dispatch tables, full-signature checks and the typed equality-guard chain (not emitted bytecode). |
| `Backend/InternalDispatch/FrozenV1.lean`, `Backend/InternalDispatch/FrozenV2.lean`, `Backend/InternalDispatch/FrozenV3.lean` | Dispatch consumers of the frozen P1 v1–v3 `Pending*` families; v3 transports an approved input through profile agreement and coverage. |
| `Backend/InternalDispatch/Controls.lean`, `Backend/InternalDispatch/FrozenV1Controls.lean`, `Backend/InternalDispatch/FrozenV2Controls.lean`, `Backend/InternalDispatch/FrozenV3Controls.lean` | Source-backed selector controls and refusals; empty-domain refusal for v3. |

|---|---|

| `Lowering/P9/InferenceCuts.lean` | Reusable exact cuts through the elaborator. |
| `Lowering/P9/DeclarationIdentity.lean` | Decidable equality of intrinsic source declarations. |
| `Lowering/P9/FragmentDecidable.lean` | Decision procedures for the existing call-closed value fragment. |

| `Lowering/P9/CallControls.lean`, `Lowering/P9/AdmissionControls.lean`, `Lowering/P9/ProgramControls.lean`, `Lowering/P9/GraphControls.lean`, `Lowering/P9/RecursiveControls.lean`, `Lowering/P9/ReturnControls.lean`, `Lowering/P9/CatalogControls.lean` | Inhabited admission, two-function and recursive graphs, horizon timeouts, and refusals (no body, wrong return, missing leaf). |
| `Lowering/P9/OverrideControls.lean` | An elaborated inherited override: dispatch of the base declaration selects the derived body, and an abstract bodyless base dispatched to a concrete override. |
| `Source/Preservation/Frames.lean`, `Source/Preservation/LocalFrames.lean`, `Source/Preservation/StatementFrames.lean`, `Source/Preservation/CallFrames.lean` | `MemOK` across `EM.sub`, scope and call frames; caller locals restored. |
| `Source/Preservation/Reads.lean`, `Source/Preservation/ValueConversions.lean`, `Source/Preservation/ResultValidity.lean` | Reads, implicit conversions and result references preserve the invariant. |
| `Source/Preservation/AllocationSites.lean`, `Source/Preservation/MemberPacking.lean`, `Source/Preservation/Encoding.lean`, `Source/Preservation/ArrayLiterals.lean` | Allocating string, struct, `abi.encode` and inline-array arms. |
| `Source/Preservation/Combinators.lean`, `Source/Preservation/Expressions.lean`, `Source/Preservation/Statements.lean`, `Source/Preservation/Fragment.lean` | Joint all-horizon memory preservation (`fragMemAt`) for the existing call value fragment under `CallsOK`. |
| `Source/Preservation/EncodingVariants.lean` | Preservation for every `abi.encode*` kind (`evalE_abiKind_mem`). |
| `Source/Preservation/Controls.lean` and its `*Controls.lean` | Allocation, frame, caller-reference, storage-copy, array-literal and fragment controls. |
| `Source/Progress/CheckedPrograms.lean` | Progress and memory preservation for elaborated programs with a checked complete catalogue. |
| `Source/Progress/Controls.lean` | Contract-backed admission, memory-prefix consumer and refusal controls. |

| `Source/Progress/EmitBuiltins.lean`, `Source/Progress/BuiltinStatements.lean` | Progress of direct `emit` nodes and builtin expression statements. |
| `Source/Progress/BuiltinControls.lean`, `Source/Progress/ScalarBuiltinControls.lean`, `Source/Progress/LiteralBuiltinControls.lean` | Builtin and event executions, argument order, zero modulus, hashes, messages, refusals and `checked_event`/`checked_call_statement` adapters. |

| `Lowering/P8/SourcePrograms.lean`, `SourceProgramControls.lean` | Checked original-byte frontend stages to actual all-unit Source elaboration and failure propagation; nonempty contract component proofs are in the acceptance modules. |
| `Lowering/P8/SourceProgramAcceptance.lean`, `SourceProgramAcceptanceControls.lean` | Exact actual elaborator success-bit laws and the full original kernel contract-control conjunction; ordered expectations unchanged. |
| `Lowering/P8/EntryRoots.lean`, `EntryRootControls.lean` | Actual function/receive/fallback selector registration and supplied-Program controls; pending root transport, not executed-entry or full admission adequacy. |
| `Lowering/P8/SourceObservationAdmission.lean`, `SourceObservationControls.lean` | All-output actual source elaboration followed by an observation guard; composed independent ProgramSite soundness, not full feature/profile adequacy. |

See [P8 admission](feature-work/p8-admission.md).

| `Yul/RawSource.lean`, `Yul/RawSourceAdapter.lean` | Concrete bool-control Source statement adapters. |
| `Yul/Checks/**`, `Frontend/SolcJson/Yul*.lean` | Kernel controls and integer-oracle vectors; solc JSON ingress. |
| `Evm/MemoryLoopHorizon.lean`, `Evm/MemoryLoopFiniteHorizon.lean` (+`Controls`) | P5 all-depth and finite-horizon loop transport ([VY2](feature-work/yul-physical-memory.md)). |
| `Evm/MemoryRecursiveDemandViews.lean`, `Evm/MemoryRecursiveDemandBounds.lean` | Typed recursive demand/cost cases and allocation-word bounds from computed finite heads and stopping bodies. |
| `Evm/MemoryRecursiveOneTurn.lean` | Stopping-body prefix-room reduction and full recursive loop transport with initial body room. |
| `Evm/MemoryRecursiveCert.lean`, `Evm/MemoryRecursiveCompile.lean`, `Evm/MemoryRecursiveSound.lean`, `Evm/MemoryRecursiveDispatch.lean`, `Evm/MemoryMixedLoop.lean` | Recursive and mixed-body loop certificates, compiler, correctness, dispatch fallback. |
| `Yul/Views/**` | Byte/word memory views, codecs, arrays, objects, heap inventories. |
| `Yul/Adapters/P6ByteWriteOrder{,Controls}` | Complete typed-frame disjoint byte-write commutation, metadata and empty-span controls. |
| `Yul/Adapters/MemoryBytes{,Controls}` | Exact byte-view/backend snapshot-copy and unchanged-map agreement; overlap controls. |
| `Yul/Adapters/RawPhysical{,Controls}` | Existing H11 raw-local frame to bounded P6 physical bytes; explicit readback boundaries and overlay administration |
| `Yul/Views/ComputedPlacement{,Controls}` | Checked actual-heap ordinary placement witness with exact unsupported/overflow accounting. |
| `Yul/Views/ComputedAllocation{,Controls}` | Actual Source allocation preserves computed old geometry and derives fresh extent and every-initial-H MemOK; target refinement remains separate. |
| `Yul/Adapters/ComputedFrameHeap{,Controls}` | Computed geometry, canonical inventory and complete Source/frame roundtrip; allocator refinement remains separate. |
| `Yul/Adapters/**` | Source/frame-memory agreement with the P6 interface; writeback and slices. |
| `Yul/Compilation/**` | Target-side Yul compilation combinators: stack slots, lowering, opcodes, control ([VY3](feature-work/inline-assembly-vy3.md)). |
| `Yul/Compilation/PureStatements.lean`, `Yul/Compilation/ScopedStatements.lean` | Intrinsic Yul skip/assignment/sequence and initialized/zero scope compilation; actual Yul evaluator/Scalar certificates, not full Source/G24 adequacy. |
| `Yul/Compilation/OpenExpressions.lean`, `Yul/Compilation/OpenStatements.lean`, `Yul/Compilation/ScopedRawStatements.lean` | Actual VY1 raw binding pack/evaluation/writeback consumers; same-fuel raw Yul/Scalar compilation, with typed Solidity/G24 embedding still open. |
| `Yul/Compilation/NormalControl.lean`, `Yul/Compilation/NormalStackTransport.lean` | Actual intrinsic nested-normal-control compiler evidence; complete stack-only block execution in the existing model, with native/Source/G24 adequacy open. |
| `Yul/Compilation/PhysicalStore.lean` | Actual VY2 byte-view/typed-image agreement with the existing constant-byte compiler slice; native/Source/G24/MemOK closure remains open. |
| `Yul/World/**` | Yul world interface: raw windows, observations, adapters, creation, lifecycle ([VY4](feature-work/inline-assembly-vy4.md)). |
| `Lowering/P8/FailureProjection.lean`, `Lowering/P8/SourceRealization.lean` | Failure-plan operations on `FailureResources.Bound`; Source session machine; no P8a instance ([VY5](feature-work/vy5-composition.md)). |
| `Yul/Composition/**` | Generic G01 stage composition with toy controls; P6 MSTORE/REVERT frame correctness. |

## Label execution
| Module | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `Backend/LabelExec/Step.lean`, `Backend/LabelExec/Fetch.lean`, `Backend/LabelExec/Packets.lean` | Byte-PC step over the numeric slice and JUMP/JUMPI/JUMPDEST; fetch behind a prefix; branch packets ([label execution](feature-work/backend-label-exec.md)). |
| `Backend/LabelExec/Assembly.lean`, `Backend/LabelExec/Loops.lean`, `Backend/LabelExec/ScalarTransfer.lean` | Assembled jumps and tests; top-level loop header/backedge; `Scalar.step` lifting. |
| `Backend/LabelExec/Placement.lean`, `Backend/LabelExec/Checks.lean` | Arbitrarily placed local loop packets at whole-runtime base zero; standalone assembled-loop witness and negative controls. |
| `Backend/LabelExec/LiteralLoops.lean`, `Backend/LabelExec/LiteralChecks.lean` | Actual head/PUSH-to-test composition for literal conditions; zero/nonzero assembled entry controls, no reached-test premise. |
| `Backend/LabelExec/Boundaries.lean`, `Backend/LabelExec/LoopExit.lean`, `Backend/LabelExec/LoopExitControls.lean` | Actual indexed fetch/tag execution and six-step zero-loop exit; skipped unsupported-body and EOF controls. |
| `Backend/LabelExec/Fragment.lean`, `Backend/LabelExec/FragmentSound.lean`, `Backend/LabelExec/FragmentControls.lean` | Intrinsic stack-height/capacity refinement of existing numeric code and Block; actual one-step successor derivation and value-frame/capacity countercontrols. |
| `Backend/Transient/{State,Instructions,TypedSource,Code,Frames,Controls,SourceFrames}.lean` | P7 transient target: owner-indexed word bank, executed TLOAD/TSTORE code, selected bool Source correspondence, reset and local callback rollback; see [P7](feature-work/p7-transient-target.md). |
| `Backend/Transient/{WordSource,WordControls}.lean` | `Val.word` Source port and signed/unsigned write/reset controls with explicit selected-slot/default premises. |
| `Backend/Transient/{Mixed,SourceCallback}.lean` | Persistent/memory/transient composition under reset, and Source environment callback execution with caller-state, static, revert and timeout controls. |

### G46.05 direct hash-query receipts (optional Bridge)
| Module (under `Bridge/SolidityBridge/`) | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `HashBridge/Queries.lean`, `HashBridge/Step.lean`, `HashBridge/Layout.lean` | Reused actual opaque digest/query and conditional storage-region interfaces. |
| `HashBridge/Prefix.lean`, `HashBridge/PrefixControls.lean` | Actual explicit-step query receipts, derived KECCAK frame, repeated-input and fuel controls; [scope](feature-work/hash-direct-prefix.md). |
| `HashBridge/ChargedGuard.lean`, `HashBridge/Charged.lean`, `HashBridge/ChargedControls.lean` | Exact KECCAK memory/base/check order and all-fuel fetched-X-to-query receipt with full actual successor fields; logical charge/fuel/static controls. |
| `ExternalEvm/OutcomeDriver.lean` | Reused owned generic observation of pinned X checks/step/terminal dispatch; no replacement evaluator. |
| `HashBridge/Frame.lean`, `HashBridge/FrameSound.lean`, `HashBridge/FrameHash.lean`, `HashBridge/FrameControls.lean` | Recursive direct-query refinement of existing driver cuts; all-fuel existence/erasure and same-KECCAK-head continuation; error-retains-query control. |
| `HashBridge/QueryShape.lean`, `HashBridge/QueryUnique.lean`, `HashBridge/QueryControls.lean` | Fixed-state/fuel query-list uniqueness and unique existence; forbids favorable empty receipts after an executed hash. |
| `HashBridge/{QueryCut,CutSound,HashCuts,CutControls}.lean` | Checked same-run query cuts, residual execution and actual hash/error client |
| `HashBridge/CutComposition.lean`, `HashBridge/TwoHashCuts.lean` | Same-run cut grafting and conditional two-checked-hash client with derived residual receipt. |
| `HashBridge/PairControls.lean` | Concrete two-KECCAK edge producer and composed symbolic two-query/error cut. |
| `HashBridge/XiQueries.lean`, `HashBridge/XiControls.lean` | Actual public-Xi direct-query soundness/existence/uniqueness and fresh-empty-stack underflow control. |
| `HashBridge/ThetaQueries.lean`, `HashBridge/ThetaControls.lean` | Public CODE-message direct-query soundness/existence/uniqueness, exact result policy and handled-child checkpoint control. |
| `HashBridge/CallQueries.lean`, `HashBridge/CallControls.lean` | Actual call-operation CODE query receipt, reply/state installation and nonuniform low-fuel/depth control. |
| `ExternalEvm/{DriverPush,ChargedDispatch,RequireCut,RequireLocalCut,RequireLocalState,StaticcallDriver}.lean` | Reused committed owned driver closure; five exact files, one unused-simp deletion in StaticcallDriver. |
| `HashBridge/StaticQueries.lean`, `HashBridge/StaticQueryControls.lean` | Checked STATICCALL child/caller query provenance and actual fuel/check ordering controls. |
- `Yul/EvmSigned.lean`, `Yul/Checks/EvmSigned.lean`: shared-word signed readings, bounded integer encoding and universal SDIV/SMOD/SLT/SGT target-step agreement, with kernel boundary/composition/refusal controls.
- Frontend/Yul/{Grammar,DeclarationParse,ClosedParse,WordParse,OccurrenceParse,ScopedParse,Parsing}: checked source parsing composed with the core Yul certificates; declaration namespaces remain Solidity.Yul.
- Frontend/Yul/Checks/{Parsing,DeclarationParsing,ClosedSyntax,WordSyntax,Occurrences,ScopedSyntax}: parser witnesses and refusal controls separated from pure preparation controls.
- Yul/EvmArithmetic: unsigned arithmetic and two-step wrapped multiplication/division correspondence to the existing target machine. Yul/Checks/EvmArithmetic: intrinsic expression, fuel, overflow, zero and malformed-stack controls.
- Yul/EvmBitwise: comparison, bitwise, shift and EXP correspondence to actual target steps. Yul/Checks/EvmBitwise: mixed intrinsic/target execution and boundary/refusal controls.
- Yul/RawSourceVoidReturn: actual Source void-return short-circuit transport with exact raw-frame retention and explicit clean projection. Yul/Checks/RawSourceVoidReturn: dirty-frame, skipped declaration-tail, horizon and return-index controls.
- Yul/RawSourceReturnControl: void-return transport through do/while, unchecked and literal while, plus existing initializer-refusal law. Yul/Checks/RawSourceReturnControl: skipped-assignment, dirty-frame, flow and typed-tail controls.
- Yul/RawLocalDeclaration: context-indexed local snapshot and concrete Boolean declaration Source correspondence. Yul/Checks/RawLocalDeclaration: executed context growth, shifted dirty-word, timeout and full-prefix controls.
- Yul/RawLocalScope: executed Boolean declaration/scope closure, exact Source restoration and existing adapter implementation. Yul/Checks/RawLocalScope: precise horizons, dirty-frame and two-scope composition controls.
- Yul/RawDefaultDeclaration: typed constant-default lookup and Source declaration correspondence, including all integer widths. Yul/Checks/RawDefaultDeclaration: actual uint8/uint256, shifted dirty-prefix and helper-boundary controls.
| `Yul/Adapters/AllocatedComputedFrame{,Controls}` | Actual allocation to complete canonical physical frame and whole-source unchanged writeback, with derived enlarged-heap support. |

## Executable bytecode selection

| Module | Role |
|---|---|
| `Backend/Selection/Interface.lean` | Selection, frame, structured-control and same-program composition certificate statements; see [executable bytecode selection](feature-work/executable-bytecode-selection.md). |
| `Backend/Selection/Premises.lean` | Typed G24.03 and G45.05-BR premises and G49.02 runtime-consumer statement; no external result assumed or runtime proof claimed. |
